We show that preferences and artificial continuous scores stabilise each other, and argue that to calibrate metrics both in terms of their absolute scores and comparisons between translation of the same source, both types of signal are needed.

</details>


### [163] [A Scalable Framework for Automated NER Annotation Correction in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18739)
*Toqeer Ehsan,Thamar Solorio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多步骤框架，通过基于频率的迭代自训练方法与双阈值机制来自动提升命名实体识别（NER）数据集的标注质量，并在多个数据集上验证了性能提升，同时探索了生成式大语言模型在低资源语言NER上的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NER及相关NLP任务中，低质量或含噪声的标注会严重阻碍模型达到先进性能。现有数据集往往存在标注噪声，影响下游模型的训练效果与泛化能力，因此亟需自动化手段来改善标注质量。

Method: 提出一个多步骤自动化框架，核心包含：1）基于频率的迭代方法，利用自训练策略不断优化标注；2）引入双阈值机制以增强模型推理的置信度，从而筛选高质量标注并抑制噪声影响。

Result: 在多个不同NER数据集上的实验评估表明，相较于原始数据集，该框架显著提升了NER性能。此外还探索了生成式大语言模型在低资源语言NER任务中的可行性。

Conclusion: 基于频率迭代的自训练与双阈值机制能够有效提升含噪声NER数据集的标注质量并带来性能增益；同时生成式LLM为低资源语言下的NER提供了新的潜在途径，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Poor quality or noisy annotations in Named Entity Recognition (NER), as in any other NLP task, make it challenging to achieve state-of-the-art performance. In this paper, we present a multi-step framework to enhance the annotation quality of NER datasets by employing automated techniques. We propose a frequency-based iterative approach that leverages self-training and a dual-threshold mechanism to enhance inference confidence. Experimental evaluations on different NER datasets demonstrate significant improvements in NER performance with respect to the original datasets. This work further explores the potential of generative Large Language Models (LLMs) to perform NER for low-resource languages.

</details>


### [164] [Zero-Shot Cross-Lingual Recognition of Sign Language Handshapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18772)
*Marcel Granero-Moya,Carolina del Corral Farrarós,Gloria Haro,Coloma Ballester,Ricardo Marques*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个零样本跨语言手形识别框架，将美国手语（ASL）的模型迁移到加泰罗尼亚手语（LSC），利用手形分解为五个共享音系特征，通过复合音系距离度量解码LSC手形，在无目标语言训练标签的情况下达到80.0%音系特征准确率和54.5%手形准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语处理技术在高资源语言（如ASL）上进展迅速，但世界上大多数手语缺乏新方法所需的音系标注。作者希望将成熟手语模型能力迁移到低资源手语，无需目标语言训练标注即可进行手形识别。

Method: 利用手形分解为五个共享音系特征（selected fingers、flexion、spread、thumb position、thumb contact），在ASL上训练三种架构（MLP、SL-GCN、SHuBERT）和两个语料库（PopSign、Sem-Lex），通过复合音系距离度量从预测特征解码LSC手形；先对录音格式差异进行调和以实现零样本迁移。

Result: 在37手形、单人的LSC基准上，零样本迁移可行：达到80.0%音系特征准确率与54.5%预期手形准确率，前提是统一录音格式差异。

Conclusion: 音系分解为将手语技术扩展到低资源语言提供了桥梁，无需目标语言的视频训练标签即可实现跨语言转移。

Abstract: Sign language processing advances rapidly for high-resource languages such as American Sign Language (ASL), yet most of the world's sign languages lack the phonological annotations new methods require. We present the first zero-shot cross-lingual framework for handshape recognition, transferring from ASL to Catalan Sign Language (LSC). Our approach leverages the decomposition of handshapes into five phonological features -- selected fingers, flexion, spread, thumb position, and thumb contact -- shared across both languages, to decode LSC handshapes from predicted features via a composite phonological distance metric. We evaluate three architectures (MLP, SL-GCN, SHuBERT) trained on two ASL corpora (PopSign, Sem-Lex) against a 37-handshape, single-signer LSC benchmark. Zero-shot transfer proves viable once recording-format disparities are harmonized, reaching 80.0% phonological feature accuracy and 54.5% expected handshape accuracy. Phonological decomposition thus offers a bridge for extending sign language technologies to low-resource languages without any target-language video training labels.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond frequency measures: Can contextual embeddings capture meaning change in scientific texts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18804)
*Jianying Liu,Kim Gerdes,Jean-Marc Deltorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了基于频率与传统基于上下文嵌入的方法在识别科技趋势（语义变迁）中的表现，发现频率方法在人类评判的"趋势相关术语"上略优（Precision@50为0.62 vs 0.60），但嵌入方法能捕捉到频率分析遗漏的关键概念发展（如"原初黑洞"），且两者相关性约0.6，表明语义度量可提供互补信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于频率的方法难以捕捉领域术语的实质性意义变迁，而识别技术趋势是核心科学计量任务。作者假设上下文嵌入可补充频率动态，从而有效追踪历时语义变化，因此探究嵌入方法与传统频率方法在技术趋势识别中的价值差异。

Method: 收集2010至2024年间天体物理学与NLP两个语料库；使用KeyBERT（底层为SciBERT）提取候选术语，并经Fisher精确检验过滤出频率显著上升的术语；由领域专家评估这些术语是否存在真实语义变迁以建立真值标签；随后利用余弦距离、平均成对距离、Hotelling T²和最大均值差异等多种度量比较两个离散时期术语的上下文嵌入"云"以量化语义漂移。

Result: 频率方法在人类评判的"趋势相关术语"上与人工判断契合度略高于语义度量（Precision@50 0.62 vs 0.60）；两种信号相关性约为0.6；嵌入指标单独识别出的一些术语（如"原初黑洞"）代表了纯频率分析无法发现的关键概念发展。

Conclusion: 语义度量能捕捉与频率动态互补的信息，将上下文嵌入整合进科学计量趋势分析具有重要价值，可为技术趋势识别提供频率方法之外的多维视角。

Abstract: Identifying technological trends is a core scientometric task, yet traditional frequency-based approaches struggle to capture substantial meaning shifts of domain-specific terms. We hypothesise that contextual embeddings can complement frequency dynamics to effectively track diachronic semantic change. We compare frequency and embedding-based approaches across Astrophysics and NLP corpora spanning from 2010 to 2024. Candidate terms are extracted using KeyBERT (utilizing SciBERT as its underlying language model) and filtered for significant frequency increases using Fisher's exact test. These terms are then evaluated for genuine semantic shift by domain experts to establish ground-truth labels. To quantify semantic drift, each term's contextual embedding ''clouds'' from the two discrete periods are compared using multiple metrics: cosine distance, average pairwise distance, Hotelling-type T 2 , and maximum mean discrepancy. Results indicate that frequency-based methods align slightly better with human judgments of ''trend-related terms'' than semantic metrics (Precision@50 of 0.62 vs 0.60 in Astrophysics). The two signals show a correlation of around 0.6. Several terms identified exclusively by embedding metrics (e.g., ''primordial black holes'') represent critical conceptual developments invisible to pure frequency analysis. These findings indicate that semantic metrics may capture complementary information, highlighting the value of integrating contextual embeddings into scientometric trend analysis.

</details>


### [166] [ReFigBench: Benchmarking Scientific Figure Reconstruction as Editable PowerPoint Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18844)
*Liyang Fan,Chi Wei,Yitai Li,Xinping Bi,Guhong Chen,Chenghao Sun,Haoxiang Yang,Qingwen Li,Kai Yan,Hong Li,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReFigBench基准与评估框架，基于arXiv上的1,000张真实科学概览图，评估多模态编码代理将图像重建为可编辑PowerPoint幻灯片的能力，涵盖四种模型家族、两种工作流程和多种商业工具链配置。研究发现感知仍是瓶颈，工作流效果取决于模型与工具链的组合，且保真度与可编辑性之间存在核心张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测往往只孤立地衡量短工具调用、API轨迹或截图相似度，但得分低无法区分是模型看得差、规划差还是被工具链拖累。缺乏能够分离感知、规划与工具链因素的评估方法，难以定位多模态编码代理的实际瓶颈所在。

Method: 构建ReFigBench：从arXiv论文中检索1,000张带完整来源信息的真实概览图作为任务集。让四个模型家族的编码代理在两种工作流（直接代码生成与专用PPTX工作流）下重建每张图，最强模型运行于两个商业工具链中，共得到十种配置。评估结合确定性工件检查、两个模型家族判断者的重复自动评分以及盲法人工比较。

Result: 感知仍是瓶颈，迭代渲染只能部分弥补。工作流投入能否转化为质量取决于模型与工具链的组合：同一模型在一个工具链内从专用工作流获益，在另一个中却受损；即使提示相同，工具链也会改变得分。专用工作流在所有配置中都抹除了原生连接符，但人工判断者仍大多偏好其渲染结果。即使最强代理也未达到评分标准上限。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了保真度与可编辑性之间的张力，这成为实用多模态文档代理面临的核心挑战，表明改进应同时着眼于模型的感知能力与工具链的设计协同。

Abstract: Multimodal coding agents are expected to turn visual inputs into usable artifacts, and they act through a harness, the layer of tools, context management, and execution environment around the model. Existing evaluations often isolate short tool calls, API traces, or screenshot resemblance, and a low score under these proxies cannot say whether the model saw poorly, planned poorly, or was failed by its harness. We study scientific overview figure reconstruction, an agent task in which a source image must become an editable PowerPoint slide that preserves text, topology, layout, and native document structure. We introduce ReFigBench, a benchmark and evaluation framework built on 1,000 real overview figures retrieved from arXiv papers with full provenance. Coding agents from four model families reconstruct every figure under two workflows, direct code generation and a specialized PPTX workflow, and the strongest model runs inside two commercial harnesses, yielding ten configurations. Evaluation combines deterministic artifact checks, repeated automated scoring by judges from two model families, and blinded human comparisons. Perception remains a bottleneck that iterative rendering only partly repays. Whether workflow effort converts into quality depends on the model together with its harness, since the same model gains from the specialized workflow inside one harness and loses inside the other, and the harness shifts scores even under an identical direct prompt. The specialized workflow erases native connectors in every configuration, human judges still prefer its renderings in most matchups, and even the strongest agent falls short of the rubric ceiling. These results expose the tension between fidelity and editability as the central challenge for practical multimodal document agents.

</details>


### [167] [EviGen: Predictive Evidence Scaffolding for Verifiable Clinical Rationale Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18852)
*Fengnan Li,Heman Burre,Liwen Sun,Roshni Varma,Matthew M. Engelhard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EviGen是一个三层框架，用于生成可验证的临床推理依据，通过患者条件检索器、LLM生成器和过程监督验证器，解决对纵向电子健康记录进行全面临床医生审查不现实、LLM处理成本高且不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向EHR包含多年患者病史，蕴含预测临床结果所需的证据，但全面审查不现实，且基于LLM的处理成本高、易遗漏相关观察或产生幻觉，需要一种可验证且可靠的临床推理生成方法。

Method: EviGen由三层构成：第一层是患者条件检索器，使用可学习查询找到对临床结果有预测性（而非仅文本相关）的证据，并按预测归因分数排序；第二层是LLM生成器，将排序后的证据作为脚手架生成基于所检索文本的临床推理；第三层是过程监督验证器，在推理步骤层面检查生成的推理并标记不可靠的声明。

Result: 在三个医学预测数据集上，EviGen在预测性能和推理忠实度方面优于全上下文LLM和RAG基线，并在可用性评估中获得临床评审员的偏好。

Conclusion: EviGen通过预测性检索加过程级验证，有效提升了临床推理生成的可靠性、忠实度和可验证性，同时降低了不可靠声明的风险，获得了临床评审员的认可。

Abstract: Longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs) capture years of patient history across notes, codes, labs, and procedures, and contain evidence needed to reason about likely clinical outcomes. However, comprehensive clinician review of these records is impractical, and LLM-based processing is costly and often unreliable, missing some relevant observations while hallucinating others. We therefore propose EviGen, a three-layer framework for verifiable clinical rationale generation that addresses these challenges. The first layer is a patient-conditioned retriever that uses learnable queries to find evidence predictive of, not just textually relevant to, a clinical outcome and ranks it by prediction attribution scores. The second layer is an LLM generator that consumes this ranked evidence as a scaffold to produce a clinical rationale grounded in the retrieved spans. The third layer is a process-supervised verifier that checks the generated rationale at the reasoning-step level, flagging unreliable claims. Across three medical prediction datasets, EviGen improves prediction performance and rationale faithfulness over full-context LLM and RAG baselines, and is preferred by clinical reviewers in a usability evaluation.

</details>


### [168] [PersonaPath: Towards Knowledge-Centric Personalized Learning Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18861)
*Yu Liu,Zeming Liu,Tianle Zhang,Zihao Cheng,Yuhang Guo,Kehai Chen,Min Zhang,Yunhong Wang,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"知识中心型"（Knowledge-Centric）个性化学习路径规划范式，并引入PersonaPath基准：该基准将2000个细粒度学习者画像与一个包含77个学科、347本教材、1751个单元、4092个概念的分层知识图谱相匹配。实验表明，最强LLM在基础教育上最终通过率仅29.5%，主要瓶颈在于自适应性（适应性指标最高仅44.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应学习系统普遍采用"以练习为中心"（Exercise-Centric）的推荐方式，仅依据项目级交互日志推断下一步，缺乏对显式学习目标和课程级前提条件的建模。其结果是，练习记录相似的学习者可能因目标不同而需要不同的学习路径，因此现有方法难以支撑目标导向的规划与评估。作者主张转向知识中心型（Knowledge-Centric）的个性化路径规划，需在学习者画像、掌握状态与前提知识结构之上进行推理。

Method: 作者将学习路径规划任务形式化为知识中心型问题：规划器需综合推理学习者画像、知识掌握状态与前提结构，决策下一步应学习哪本教材、哪个单元、哪个概念。为支撑该设定，作者构建了PersonaPath基准，包含2000个细粒度学习者画像，以及覆盖77个学科的分层知识图谱（347本教材、1751个单元、4092个概念），并基于该基准对代表性的大语言模型（LLM）进行评估。

Result: 在PersonaPath上的评估结果显示：即使最强的LLM在基础教育场景下的最终通过率也仅为29.5%；整体主要瓶颈在于自适应性——没有任何模型在"为个体学习者定制路径"这一维度上超过44.7%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在知识中心型个性化学习路径规划上表现欠佳，自适应规划能力（针对个体差异定制路径）是其最突出的短板。这一结果为后续研究指明了改进方向：应重点提升模型对学习者个性化差异的适配能力，并借助含前提结构的知识图谱进行目标导向规划。

Abstract: Adaptive learning systems commonly formulate learning path planning as Exercise-Centric (EC) recommendation, where the next step is inferred from item-level interaction logs. Evaluating goal-oriented guidance additionally requires explicit learner goals and curriculum-scale prerequisites: learners with similar exercise records may need different paths toward their targets. We therefore study Knowledge-Centric (KC) personalized learning path planning, where a planner must reason over learner profiles, mastery states, and prerequisite knowledge structures to decide which textbook, unit, and concept should be studied next. To support this setting, we introduce PersonaPath, a benchmark that pairs 2,000 fine-grained learner personas with a hierarchical knowledge graph of 347 textbooks, 1,751 units, and 4,092 concepts across 77 subjects. We evaluate representative LLMs on PersonaPath. Results show that even the strongest LLM reaches only a 29.5% final pass rate in Basic Education, and that the main bottleneck lies in adaptivity, where no model exceeds 44.7% in tailoring paths to individual learners.

</details>


### [169] [Structured Claim-Level Discourse Representations for Dense Health Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18905)
*Farnoushsadat Nilizadeh,Elham Pourabbas Vafa,Shirin Nilizadeh,Eduard Dragut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对社交媒体视频中密集健康话语的分析难题，提出了一种声明级（claim-level）结构化话语分析框架，将原子声明与主题方面、立场及多维语用话语属性通过元组形式建模，并构建了涵盖四个健康领域、60个视频、1,191个手动标注声明的基准数据集。实验表明，当前大语言模型在主题分类和立场预测上表现优异，但在高维语用画像上存在困难，且不同话语任务受益于不同形式的上下文推理，提示未来系统需要任务分解与专门化推理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体视频中的健康话语通常以短小对话片段呈现高度纠缠的声明，同时涵盖多个主题方面、立场、证据框架和修辞功能，结构极其密集。现有方法大多依赖粗粒度的主题级、情感级或立场导向表征，无法充分捕捉这种细粒度结构。作者通过分析发现每分钟平均包含13.22个原子声明，进一步凸显了构建更丰富声明级话语表征的迫切性。

Method: 作者提出一个声明级话语分析框架，将话语建模为连接原子声明与主题方面、立场及多维语用话语属性的元组。为支撑该设定，构建了一个横跨四个健康领域、包含60个视频中1,191个手动标注声明的基准数据集。基于该框架，在不同话语上下文设置下对自动化结构化话语分析进行系统评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，当前大语言模型在主题分类与立场预测任务上表现强劲，但在高维语用画像（pragmatic profiling）任务上表现困难。此外，不同话语任务分别受益于不同形式的上下文推理，说明各任务的最优推理方式存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了密集健康话语分析中声明级结构的重要性，并指出现有LLM在细粒度语用分析上的局限。由于不同话语任务需要不同的上下文推理模式，未来系统可能需要采用任务分解和专门化的推理策略来提升整体性能。

Abstract: Health discourse in social media videos often contains densely entangled claims spanning multiple thematic aspects, stances, evidential frames, and rhetorical functions within short conversational spans. Existing approaches largely rely on coarse topic-level, sentiment-based, or stance-oriented representations that do not adequately capture this structure. Our analysis identifies an average of 13.22 atomic claims per minute, motivating richer claim-level discourse representations. We introduce a structured framework for claim-level discourse analysis in dense health narratives. Our framework models discourse through tuples linking atomic claims with thematic aspects, stance, and multidimensional pragmatic discourse attributes. To support this setting, we construct a benchmark spanning four health domains with 1,191 manually annotated claims from 60 videos. Using this framework, we evaluate automated structured discourse analysis under different discourse context settings. Results show that current LLMs achieve strong performance on thematic categorization and stance prediction, but struggle with high-dimensional pragmatic profiling. We also find that different discourse tasks benefit from different forms of contextual reasoning, suggesting that future systems may require task decomposition and specialized inference strategies.

</details>


### [170] [How Much is a Human Right Worth? ECtHR-NPD: A Benchmark for Predicting Non-Pecuniary Damage Awards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18908)
*Yanyi Pu,Damian A. Gonzalez-Salzberg,Zheng Yuan,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ECtHR-NPD，这是据我们所知首个用于预测欧洲人权法院（ECtHR）非金钱损害赔偿（NPD）金额的基准数据集，适用于无法定公式或明确计算规则的场景。该基准包含 14,575 起案件、以名义欧元计价的案件级赔偿金额、时间划分以及将目标构建与模型输入分离的协议。实验评估了常量预测器、梯度提升树、检索方法、微调编码器语言模型、提示式解码器语言模型及知识增强智能体等多种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律基准覆盖了多种任务，但持续性金钱救济（continuous monetary remedies）——即非金钱损害赔偿金额预测——仍相对欠缺。由于此类裁决缺乏法定公式或明确计算规则，无法直接推导金额，因此需要专门的基准来系统评估模型在此任务上的表现。

Method: 构建 ECtHR-NPD 基准数据集，包含 14,575 个案件，采用时间划分和协议分离（将目标构建与模型输入分开）。评估多种方法：常量预测器、梯度提升树、检索方法、微调编码器语言模型、提示式解码器语言模型以及知识增强智能体。

Result: 结果显示，更复杂的语言模型和智能体方法并未持续超越最强的基于特征的基线。所有模型类型在识别零赔偿金额和校准高赔偿预测方面都存在困难，在 Challenging 测试视图上性能进一步下降，使其成为当前最先进的开源和专有语言模型的挑战性测试平台。

Conclusion: ECtHR-NPD 揭示了即便是最先进的语言模型和智能体方法在该任务上也难以超越传统基于特征的方法，且普遍存在零金额识别和高金额校准两大难点，说明该基准对现有模型家族构成了显著的挑战，为未来法律 AI 研究提供了有价值的测试基准。

Abstract: Existing legal benchmarks cover diverse tasks, while continuous monetary remedies remain comparatively underexplored. We introduce ECtHR-NPD, to the best of our knowledge, the first benchmark for predicting non-pecuniary damage (NPD) awards at the European Court of Human Rights (ECtHR) from case information when no statutory formula or explicit calculation rule determines the amount. ECtHR-NPD contains 14,575 cases with case-level awards in nominal euros, chronological splits, and a protocol separating target construction from model input. We evaluate a battery of methods, including constant predictors, gradient-boosted trees, retrieval methods, fine-tuned encoder language models (LMs), prompted decoder LMs, and knowledge-augmented agents. Our results show that more sophisticated LM and agentic approaches do not consistently outperform the strongest feature-based baseline. All model families struggle to identify zero awards and to calibrate high-award predictions, with further degradation on the Challenging test view, making ECtHR-NPD a challenging testbed for current state-of-the-art open-weight and proprietary LMs.

</details>


### [171] [Beyond Outcomes: Dual-View Relational Learning for Efficient Agent Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18909)
*Xinshuai Guo,Junjie Wu,Dolly Deng,Yinghui Li,Hai-Tao Zheng,Suncong Zheng,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DualViewEval，一种面向智能体（Agent）基准测试的压缩方法。与现有仅建模任务-模型最终分数分布冗余的方法不同，DualViewEval通过分析大规模轨迹，识别出六种与最终性能系统相关的过程信号，并联合利用结果（outcome）与过程（process）关系学习精确大小的最小任务子集（miniset）以预测全基准分数。在五个Agent基准和五个代表性基线上，该方法在所有数据集上取得最优结果，仅用20个任务即可实现APEX-Agents和BFCL上的24倍-40倍压缩，MAE降低14.5%-28.2%，并在SWE-bench Verified上相比EssenceBench将Kendall's τ相对提升最高7.2%。此外，选出的miniset还能揭示不同智能体的能力差异，提供紧凑且具诊断性的反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体基准测试的评估成本远高于传统LLM基准测试，因此基准压缩是自然且必要的解决方案。然而现有压缩方法主要建模任务-模型最终分数分布的冗余，这种方式在智能体化评估中并不充分，因为它忽略了智能体执行过程中蕴含的大量过程性信息。作者旨在通过挖掘轨迹中的过程信号，从结果与过程两个完整视角来解开智能体性能冗余，从而设计更精确的压缩方法。

Method: 首先在大规模轨迹上进行分析，识别出六种与最终智能体性能存在系统性关联的互补过程信号。基于此，提出DualViewEval方法，它联合利用结果关系（任务-模型最终分数）与过程关系（轨迹过程信号），学习一个精确大小的最小任务子集（exact-size miniset），并用该子集来预测完整基准的得分。核心思想是从"结果+过程"双视角完整地剥离智能体性能冗余，而非仅依赖最终分数分布。

Result: 在五个智能体基准和五个代表性基线上，DualViewEval在所有数据集上均取得最佳结果。仅用20个任务，就在APEX-Agents和BFCL上实现24倍至40倍的压缩，相比最强竞争者将平均绝对误差（MAE）降低14.5%-28.2%，并在SWE-bench Verified上相对于EssenceBench将Kendall's τ指标提升最高7.2%。选出的miniset还能反映不同智能体之间的能力差异，提供紧凑且具诊断性的开发反馈。

Conclusion: DualViewEval通过联合利用结果与过程双视角信息，显著优于现有仅依赖最终分数分布的压缩方法，同时在多种智能体基准上实现了高压缩比与低预测误差。其选出的miniset具有诊断价值，可揭示智能体能力差异，为高效、紧凑的智能体模型开发提供了实用的评估工具。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks are substantially more costly to evaluate than conventional LLM benchmarks. Benchmark compression is therefore a natural solution, yet existing methods primarily model redundancy in task--model final-score distributions, which is important in agentic evaluation. To address this limitation, we analyze large-scale trajectories and identify six complementary process signals that are systematically associated with final agent performance. To disentangle agent performance redundancy from a complete perspective, we propose DualViewEval, an agent benchmark compression method that jointly exploits outcome and process relations to learn an exact-size miniset and predict the full-benchmark scores. Across five agent benchmarks and five representative baselines, DualViewEval achieves the best results in all datasets. With only 20 tasks, it achieves $24\times$--$40\times$ compression on APEX-Agents and BFCL, reducing mean absolute error (MAE) by $14.5\%$--$28.2\%$ over the strongest competitors while improving Kendall's $τ$ by up to $7.2\%$ relative to EssenceBench on SWE-bench Verified. The selected minisets further reveal capability differences among different agents, providing compact and diagnostic feedback for efficient agentic model development.

</details>


### [172] [Long-Lived Characters, Local Inference: Incremental Memory Maintenance for Game NPCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18935)
*Zimu Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对长生命周期游戏NPC，提出在量化Qwen混合循环注意力模型中做增量式记忆维护的运行时方案：移除过时的注意力KV条目、在序列真实末尾计算替换记录、保留持续循环状态与未变化的KV。实验表明真尾更新能保留重要当前状态与历史绑定，而槽位保留方案会出现重复扣减错误，说明应从可维护的、依赖历史的推理状态角度看待NPC记忆，而非仅作为最新记忆文本的一次性编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署的语言模型角色不应在每次对话前重新读取其全部人生记忆，否则会造成巨大的准备开销。更关键的是，当对话输入游戏定义的规则性动作和价值判断时，若记忆维护产生错误表述（如物品归属、转移是否已发生），会污染确定性规则的输入。这促使作者研究如何让角色在不断修改少量记忆时仍保持正确且经济的推理状态。

Method: 在量化Qwen混合循环注意力模型上实现增量记忆维护运行时，包含三个机制：移除已被取代的注意力KV条目、在序列的真实末尾计算替换记录、保留持续中的循环状态和未变化的KV。实验采用多更新对话重放、固定输入位置消融和注意力诊断等多种本地设置。

Result: 独立的块组合会削弱基于查询的条件记忆选择，但未出现均匀的块起始注意力坍缩；真尾更新在八轮脚本化维护中保持了重要的当前状态和历史绑定。在一个放置场景中，真尾方式在三次重建中恢复完整补货数量，而槽位保留替代方案反复出现双重扣减错误。仅靠注意力分布邻近性无法解释这些语义差异。

Conclusion: 应把角色推理状态视为一种被持续维护、依赖历史的资源，而不是仅由最新记忆文本得出的即时一次性编码。该观点为长生命周期游戏NPC的本地推理成本与语义可靠性之间的权衡提供了设计方向。

Abstract: A game character should not have to reread its entire life before every conversation. For locally deployed language-model characters, however, revising a few memories can invalidate a long reusable prefix. The resulting preparation cost competes with both foreground dialogue and the maintenance of other characters. This matters especially when dialogue feeds game-defined actions and value judgments: a fluent but incorrect account of who owns an item, or whether a transfer has already happened, can corrupt the input to otherwise deterministic rules. We study incremental memory maintenance for long-lived game NPCs in a quantized Qwen hybrid recurrent-attention model. Our runtime removes superseded attention KV entries, computes replacement records at the true sequence tail, and preserves the continuing recurrent state and unchanged KV. Existing local experiments combine multi-update dialogue replays, fixed-input placement ablations, and attention diagnostics. Independent block composition weakens query-conditioned memory selection without a uniform chunk-initial attention collapse. True-tail updates preserve important current-state and historical bindings across eight scripted maintenance rounds; a placement case recovers the full-refill quantity in three reconstructions, while slot-preserving alternatives repeat a double-subtraction error. Attention-distribution proximity alone does not explain these semantic differences. The results motivate treating a character's inference state as a maintained, history-dependent resource, rather than only a disposable encoding of its latest memory text.

</details>


### [173] [LangSelect: Cost-Aware Target-Language Routing for LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18959)
*Son Ha Xuan,Phat T. Tran-Truong,Xuan-Bach Le,Nghia Duong-Trung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LangSelect，一种面向语言灵活编程任务的验证感知路由系统。它在解码前选择目标编程语言（而非固定），并在首次生成失败时回退。通过两种评估方式（已验证解决方案重放与实时GPT-5生成）在MultiLang-Bench（3000任务、8语言验证语料库）上验证：重放显示有大幅语言路由空间；实时评估中，训练集领域启发式基线将代理token减少50.3%（回退后通过率92.9%），而CodeBERT+元数据选择器达到最高回退后通过率93.8%（token增加3.7%）。结果表明输出语言路由可在单元测试可验证的代码生成中定义实用的成本-正确性前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代码生成系统通常在选择目标编程语言后即将其视为固定不变。但对语言灵活任务（多种目标语言均可接受且可由同一测试检查），作者发现这一选择是可度量的成本杠杆——同一任务的已验证实现可能因语言不同而产生显著不同的生成token长度，因此存在优化空间。

Method: 提出LangSelect：一个验证感知的路由器，在生成前先选择目标语言，首次生成失败时进行回退（fallback）。为分离离线路由机会与端到端行为，采用两种评估方式：1) 已验证解决方案重放（verified-solution replay），从已接受语料库解决方案中选择语言，衡量路由潜力上限；2) 实时GPT-5生成，计算每次生成尝试（含失败与回退）的实际开销。路由方法包括训练集领域启发式基线和学习的CodeBERT+元数据选择器。

Result: 在MultiLang-Bench（3000任务、8语言验证语料库）上，重放实验表明存在显著的语言路由头部空间（headroom）。在450个留出任务的实时评估中，训练集领域启发式基线将含包装器与入口点开销的代理token减少50.3%，回退后通过率为92.9%；而CodeBERT+元数据选择器以3.7%的token增加换取最高回退后通过率93.8%。

Conclusion: 输出语言路由可以在单元测试可验证的代码生成中定义一个实用的成本-正确性前沿（cost-correctness frontier），即在生成token成本与通过率之间提供可权衡的选择，推动将目标语言选择作为可控优化点而非固定预设来研究。

Abstract: LLM code-generation systems usually choose a target programming language before decoding and treat that choice as fixed. We show that, for language-flexible programming tasks -- tasks where several target languages are acceptable and checkable by the same tests -- this choice is a measurable cost lever: verified implementations of the same task can differ substantially in generated-token length. We introduce LangSelect, a verification-aware router that selects the target language before generation and falls back when the first attempt fails. To separate offline routing opportunity from end-to-end behavior, we evaluate verified-solution replay, which chooses among already accepted corpus solutions, and live GPT-5 generation, which charges every generation attempt, including failures and fallbacks. On MultiLang-Bench, a 3,000-task, 8-language verified corpus, replay shows substantial language-routing headroom. In live evaluation on 450 held-out tasks, a train-split Domain heuristic baseline reduces harness-proxy tokens, which include wrapper and entrypoint overhead, by 50.3\% at 92.9\% pass after fallback, while a learned CodeBERT+metadata selector reaches the highest pass after fallback, 93.8\%, with a 3.7\% token increase. These results show that output-language routing can define a practical cost-correctness frontier for unit-test-verifiable code generation.

</details>


### [174] [When Audit Quality Fails to Predict Downstream Utility: A Counterfactual Study of Synthetic-Data Selectors for Low-Resource African NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18960)
*Son Ha Xuan,Phat T. Tran-Truong,Xuan-Bach Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过低资源非洲语言分类的受控回放实验，证明"LLM裁判评分高的样本应有利于下游模型学习"这一代理假设失效：在四个语言、两个任务、五个选择器下，审计排名与下游排名严重背离（Spearman均值ρ=0.04）。虽然所提反事实审计框架\method{}-V2能产出最干净的样本池，但AlpaGasus在最终下游Macro-F1上仍更优，表明审计质量只是所选池的属性，而非下游效用的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据选择依赖一个代理假设，即LLM裁判评分为"好"的样本也应当有助于下游模型的训练。作者希望检验这一假设在低资源语言（非洲语言）分类场景下的有效性，针对审计指标与下游性能之间可能存在的脱节进行系统性验证。

Method: 采用受控回放实验设计：在四种语言（阿姆哈拉语、豪萨语、斯瓦希里语、约鲁巴语）、两个分类任务（MasakhaNEWS、AfriSenti）以及五个匹配预算的选择器上，分别计算审计排名（如标签正确性）与下游排名（Macro-F1）的Spearman相关系数。同时提出\method{}-V2反事实审计框架，从三个审计通道（标签正确性、捷径分数、硬拒绝率）评估所选池，并比较不同选择器在下游性能上的表现。

Result: 审计与下游排名在每个网格单元内均分歧明显，Spearman均值ρ=0.04、中位数0.00，说明并非聚合伪影。\method{}-V2在三个审计通道上同时产生最干净的样本池：标签正确性0.904对比naive的0.767（相对提升17.9%）、最短捷分数、硬拒绝率0.162对比naive的0.486。然而AlpaGasus仍在下游Macro-F1上领先（0.202对比\method{}-V2的0.163），且在五个非退化单元上这种倒置持续存在。

Conclusion: 方法论启示在于：在此受控环境下，审计质量是所选数据池的属性，而非下游效用的保证。因此合成数据评估应当在相同的保留集上同时报告审计指标与下游指标。作者公开了审计表、各选择器的保留池以及将每个报告数字链接到源行的声明账本（claim ledger）以增强可复现性。

Abstract: Quality-aware synthetic-data selection rests on a proxy: examples that an LLM judge rates as good should also help a downstream model learn. In a controlled replay in low-resource African-language classification, we show that this proxy breaks. Across four languages (Amharic, Hausa, Swahili, Yoruba), two classification tasks (MasakhaNEWS, AfriSenti), and five matched-budget selectors, audit rankings and downstream rankings diverge. Within each cell, the Spearman between judged label correctness and Macro-F1 across selectors has mean $ρ{=}0.04$ (median $0.00$), showing that the mismatch is not an aggregation artifact. \method{}-V2, our counterfactual audit framework, produces the cleanest selected pool on three audit channels at once: highest judged label correctness ($0.904$ vs.\ $0.767$ for naive, a $17.9\%$ relative gain), lowest shortcut score, and a hard-reject rate of $0.162$ vs.\ $0.486$ for naive. AlpaGasus nevertheless leads downstream Macro-F1 ($0.202$ vs.\ $0.163$ for \method{}-V2), and the inversion persists on the five non-degenerate cells. The lesson is methodological: in this controlled setting, audit quality is a property of the selected pool, not a guarantee of downstream utility. Synthetic-data evaluation should therefore report audit and downstream metrics on the same retained sets. We release the audit tables, per-selector retained pools, and a claim ledger that links every reported number to its source row.

</details>


### [175] [MechSparse: Mechanism-Guided Sparse PEFT Selection Is Task-Shaped](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18961)
*Son Ha Xuan,Phat T. Tran-Truong,Xuan-Bach Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过激活补丁（activation patching）得到因果信号来引导PEFT（LoRA/QLoRA）微调位置的选择，并在Ministral-8B/NF4上的三项任务（Swahili span-JSON信息抽取和英译斯瓦希里语机器翻译）中与随机、幅度、激活范数、梯度/Fisher等廉价启发式方法进行对比。结果表明：因果选择器从未在主指标上获胜，在IE主任务上方法c优于随机和梯度/Fisher但略逊于激活范数，在MT上各选择器差异不显著。论文提出初步诊断：输出结构主导时选激活范数，因果选择器仅作为内容主导任务的假设，并公开了掩码、分数、预测与评测文件以供复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）能识别携带特定行为的稀疏注意力头与MLP模块子集。作者想探究：这种因果信号能否比研究者已经在使用的廉价启发式方法（随机、幅度、激活范数、梯度/Fisher）更有效地指导少量PEFT预算的放置位置，从而回答"因果可解释性信号在实际PEFT场景中是否具有实用价值"这一核心问题。

Method: 作者提出两种因果选择器：\method{} 通过在干净/损坏探针上做归一化激活补丁恢复（normalized activation-patching recovery）为注意力头和MLP模块打分，并仅对选中位置训练LoRA/QLoRA；\methodc{} 在\method 基础上增加了对小规模层内联合子集的有限信用（bounded credit）。实验采用成对bootstrap置信区间（600个预测、3个种子）评估，并与随机、幅度、激活范数和梯度/Fisher基线对比，同时进行schema-versus-span分解以解释差距来源。

Result: 因果选择器在任何主指标上均未获胜。在头条IE任务上，\methodc{} 相比随机提高+0.079的span+type F1、比梯度/Fisher提高+0.174，但落后激活范数0.028，且拥有最小跨种子标准差（±0.003）。在MT任务上，四种选择器都落在0.30 BLEU之内，所有成对置信区间均包含零（无显著差异）。schema-versus-span分解表明：激活范数捕捉刚性JSON流程，而因果分数追踪内容敏感位置。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：因果选择器并非在所有场景下优于廉价启发式；当输出结构占主导时（如刚性JSON例程），应优先采用激活范数；因果选择器仅应被视为内容主导任务的候选假设。同时作者公开了掩码、分数、预测和评测文件，支持直接回放复现，为后续研究提供可复用的资源。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability identifies sparse subsets of heads and MLP blocks that carry specific behaviors. We ask whether such causal signals can guide where to place a small PEFT budget more effectively than the cheap heuristics practitioners already use. \method{} scores attention heads and MLP blocks by normalized activation-patching recovery on clean/corrupted probes and trains LoRA/QLoRA only on the selected sites; \methodc{} adds bounded credit for small within-layer joint subsets.
  We compare against random, magnitude, activation-norm, and gradient/Fisher on Ministral-8B/NF4 in three cells: Swahili span-JSON information extraction (IE) at $b{=}0.25\%$ and $1.0\%$, and English$\to$Swahili machine translation (MT) at $b{=}1.0\%$. The causal selectors never win the primary metric. On the headline IE cell (3 seeds, paired-bootstrap CIs over $600$ predictions), \methodc{} beats random by $+0.079$ span+type F1 and gradient/Fisher by $+0.174$, but trails activation-norm by $0.028$, with the smallest cross-seed std ($\pm 0.003$). On MT all four selectors lie within $0.30$ BLEU and every paired CI includes zero. A schema-versus-span decomposition explains the IE gap: activation-norm captures the rigid JSON routine, while causal scores track content-sensitive sites. We distill a preliminary diagnostic -- prefer activation-norm when output structure dominates, treat causal selectors as a hypothesis for content-dominated tasks -- and release masks, scores, predictions, and evaluation files for direct replay.

</details>


### [176] [The Death of Schema Linking? Text-to-SQL in the Age of Well-Reasoned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.07702)
*Karime Maamari,Fadhil Abubaker,Daniel Jaroslawicz,Amine Mhedhbi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视了Text-to-SQL中的schema linking环节，发现新一代大语言模型（LLM）即使在存在大量无关schema元素的情况下也能有效利用相关元素。因此，当schema能适配模型上下文窗口时，完全放弃schema linking以避免过滤必要列的问题，转而采用增强、选择和修正等技巧来提升Text-to-SQL流程的准确性，最终在BIRD基准上以71.83%的准确率排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的schema linking作为Text-to-SQL流水线中的关键步骤，目标是检索目标数据库中与用户查询相关的表和列，并忽略无关元素。然而，不完美的schema linking常常会遗漏生成准确查询所需的必要列。作者重新审视了这一环节在新一代LLM中的必要性。

Method: 作者通过实验发现新模型即使在大量无关schema元素存在的情况下也能有效地利用相关schema元素。因此，当schema大小在模型上下文窗口之内时，完全跳过schema linking以减少过滤必要schema元素带来的问题。同时，不再进行上下文过滤，而是采用增强、选择和修正等技术来提升流水线准确性。

Result: 该方法在BIRD基准测试上取得了71.83%的准确率，排名第一。

Conclusion: 在新型LLM的能力下，传统的schema linking可能不再是必要的步骤，特别是在schema能放入模型上下文窗口的场景中；跳过schema linking可以避免因过滤错误导致的信息丢失，从而提升Text-to-SQL的准确性。

Abstract: Schema linking is a crucial step in Text-to-SQL pipelines. Its goal is to retrieve the relevant tables and columns of a target database for a user's query while disregarding irrelevant ones. However, imperfect schema linking can often exclude required columns needed for accurate query generation. In this work, we revisit schema linking when using the latest generation of large language models (LLMs). We find empirically that newer models are adept at utilizing relevant schema elements during generation even in the presence of large numbers of irrelevant ones. As such, our Text-to-SQL pipeline entirely forgoes schema linking in cases where the schema fits within the model's context window in order to minimize issues due to filtering required schema elements. Furthermore, instead of filtering contextual information, we highlight techniques such as augmentation, selection, and correction, and adopt them to improve the accuracy of our Text-to-SQL pipeline. Our approach ranks first on the BIRD benchmark achieving an accuracy of 71.83%.

</details>


### [177] [A Benchmark Suite and Ground-Truth Methodology for Formal Verification of IEC 61131-3 Ladder Diagram Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18994)
*Pierre Dantas,Lucas Cordeiro,Waldir Junior*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首个可编程逻辑控制器（PLC）程序形式化验证基准套件，将受控地面真值与IEC 61131-3的文本（ST）与图形（LD）两种编码覆盖相结合，包含50个程序、83个变体，跨越十个工业领域，并配套形式化属性、可机器校验的预期判定及SV-COMP格式的反例见证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管工具支持日益增长，PLC程序形式化验证领域仍缺乏标准评估基准：现有语料要么省略形式化属性，要么不含图形方言；私有程序集阻碍了可复现的进度测量。此外，作者明确指出一个具体的失效模式——从两个公开逻辑炸弹语料中，显而易见的"安全属性"会因不可见的非终止将所有攻击误判为安全，这凸显了受控地面真值纪律的必要性。

Method: 构建包含50个程序、83个变体的基准套件，覆盖十个工业领域，以PLCopen XML和ST两种格式提供，每个程序配对形式化属性、机器可校验的预期判定以及SV-COMP格式的违规见证。核心方法论贡献是"三方地面真值纪律"：判定通过构造（by construction）、故障注入（fault injection）或经审计的跨工具共识（audited cross-tool consensus）三种途径确立。参考判定由ESBMC v8.4从源码获得，并用与决策程序无关的模型检验器nuXmv进行工具无关性交叉验证。

Result: 全部25个图形基准可执行，45个被接受的变体中43个与记录的判定匹配；nuXmv在有限状态片段（21个基准）上对所有24个互锁变体给出一致结果，并解决了ESBMC-PLC留作未知的两个基准，从而确认了工具无关的地面真值与区分能力。移植过程暴露了格式与语义的碎片化：不同前端接受不同的序列化方式，且各工具的定时器语义不一致——这些正是该套件设计要揭示的现象。

Conclusion: 该基准套件为PLC程序形式化验证提供了首个结合受控地面真值与双编码覆盖的可复现评估标准，验证了判定结果的工具无关性，并通过暴露格式序列化与定时器语义的碎片化问题为工具标准化指明方向。语料、模式、校验器与重检验工具链均以开放工件形式发布。

Abstract: We present the first benchmark suite for formal verification of Programmable Logic Controller (PLC) programs that combines controlled ground truth with coverage of both textual (Structured Text, ST) and graphical (Ladder Diagram, LD) IEC 61131-3 encodings. Despite growing support for tools, the field lacks standard evaluation benchmarks: existing corpora omit formal properties or graphical dialects, and private program sets preclude reproducible measurement of progress. Our suite comprises 50 programs in 83 variants across ten industrial domains, provided in PLCopen Extensible Markup Language (XML) and ST, each paired with a formal property, machine-checkable expected verdict, and violation witness in the Software Verification Competition (SV-COMP) format. The central methodological contribution is a tripartite ground-truth discipline - verdicts are established by construction, fault injection, or audited cross-tool consensus - motivated by a concrete failure mode where the obvious safety property misclassifies all attacks from two public logic-bomb corpora as safe due to invisible non-termination. Reference verdicts are obtained with the Efficient SMT-Based Context-Bounded Model Checker (ESBMC) v8.4 from source: all 25 graphical benchmarks execute, and 43 of 45 accepted variants match recorded verdicts. On the finite-state fragment (21 benchmarks), nuXmv - a model checker with unrelated decision procedures - agrees on all 24 interlock variants and resolves two benchmarks ESBMC-PLC leaves unknown, confirming tool-neutral ground truth and discriminative power. Porting exposes format and semantics fragmentation: front-ends accept different serializations, and timer semantics vary across tools - phenomena the suite is designed to reveal. The corpus, schema, validator, and recheck harness are released as open artifacts.

</details>


### [178] [WordPolo: Evaluating Language Models Through Iterative Semantic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19006)
*Tyler McDonald,Ali Emami*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出WordPolo，一个基于语义相似度反馈的单词查找任务基准。该任务要求参与者从未知目标词出发，通过猜测和距离分数反馈逐步收敛。实验覆盖多种LLM、LRM、人类和启发式方法，在1500道谜题上进行评估。除正确率外，引入基于进展的指标，揭示模型虽常未解出但取得有意义进展。分析表明推理模型易受过度思考和思考不足困扰，成功模型展现类人策略。WordPolo论证了同时测试推理过程与结果的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM和LRM评测通常仅依赖数据集准确率，无法洞察推理过程的质量与忠实度。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建一个使迭代推理和自适应搜索策略既可直接观察又为成功所必需的评测任务，从而更全面地衡量模型能力。

Method: 设计WordPolo单词查找任务：玩家零知识起步，依据语义距离分数（1为正确，值越大越远）进行猜测，需解读分数以导航语义空间并系统缩小搜索范围。对GPT-4.1、Llama 4、Claude 3.5 Haiku、Qwen 3等LLM，o4-mini、Deepseek-R1等LRM，以及人类和新颖启发式方法，在1500道谜题上评测。除解答率外，引入基于进展的指标来刻画模型推理过程。

Result: 解答率从4%到62%不等，显示模型间显著差异。进展式指标揭示模型常常取得有意义进展，这是仅看准确率无法捕捉的信息。分析发现推理模型可能受过度思考与思考不足所限，而成功模型展现出与人类相似的搜索策略。

Conclusion: WordPolo证明需要同时测试推理过程与结果的基准，以提供对模型能力的整体衡量；并展示了过程导向指标对超越单纯准确率、揭示模型真实推理行为的重要价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) are typically evaluated on challenging benchmarks through dataset accuracy alone, providing no insight into the quality or faithfulness of their reasoning processes. We present WordPolo, a word-finding task where participants must discover an unknown target word using semantic similarity feedback. Players start with zero knowledge, make guesses, and receive distance scores (1 = correct, higher = further away). Success requires interpreting scores to navigate semantic space and systematically narrow the search. This design makes iterative reasoning and adaptive search strategies both directly observable and necessary for success. We evaluate recent LLMs (GPT-4.1, Llama 4, Claude 3.5 Haiku, Qwen 3), LRMs (o4-mini, Deepseek-R1), humans, and a novel heuristic on 1,500 puzzles. Beyond solve rates (which range from 4% to 62%), we introduce progression-based metrics that reveal models often make meaningful progress, insights that accuracy alone would miss. Our analysis shows how reasoning models can be hindered by overthinking and underthinking, while successful models exhibit human-like strategies. WordPolo demonstrates the need for benchmarks that test both reasoning process and outcomes, providing holistic measurements of model capabilities. Our code and dataset can be found at https://wordpolo-demo.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [179] [TalkMatrix: Generating Character Dialogue that is Both Consistent and Diverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19022)
*Ayuto Tsutsumi,Yuu Jinnai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化多提示-多补全选择（structured multi-prompt, multi-completion selection）问题：从一个候选池中为每个提示选择一个补全，以优化全局、不可分解的集合级目标。作者将问题实例化为角色对话场景，提出 TalkMatrix 方法：为每个角色-情境对生成多个候选，用四个基于嵌入的一致性与多样性目标联合选择完整矩阵；通过两层极大极小公式最大化最差目标，并使用多起点坐标上升近似求解。在 50 个合成角色扮演场景和 25 个棋盘游戏场景上实验，LLM-as-a-judge 显示矩阵级选择优于随机和独立单元格级选择基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于候选的解码通常为每个提示独立选择补全，但许多实际应用需要满足全局、不可分解要求的输出集合。在角色对话中，每个角色需跨越情境保持一致、每句台词须契合情境、角色与情境之间应保持可区分性，这些都不能通过独立单次选择实现，因此需要联合的结构化选择。

Method: 将问题形式化为结构化多提示-多补全选择。方法 TalkMatrix 为每个角色-情境对生成多个候选，并利用四个基于嵌入的一致性与多样性目标联合选择完整矩阵；由于加权和会牺牲某些维度，采用最大化最差目标的两层极大极小（minimax）公式，并用多起点坐标上升近似优化该离散目标。

Result: 在 50 个合成角色扮演场景和 25 个精心整理的棋盘游戏场景（多个角色在预定义情境中互动）上进行实验，比较了局部、部分矩阵、通用组合搜索等基线。LLM-as-a-judge 评估显示矩阵级选择比随机选择和独立单元格级选择基线评分更高。

Conclusion: 结果表明结构化选择对全局受控的对话生成具有价值，但经验验证仍局限于角色扮演场景，泛化性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Candidate-based decoding typically selects a completion for each prompt independently, but many applications require a collection of outputs that satisfies global, non-decomposable requirements. We formulate this setting as structured multi-prompt, multi-completion selection: given a candidate pool for every prompt, select one completion per prompt to optimize a collection-level objective. We instantiate the problem in character dialogue, where each character should remain consistent across situations, each line should fit its situation, and characters and situations should remain distinguishable. Our method, TalkMatrix, generates multiple candidates for every character--situation pair and jointly selects a complete matrix using four embedding-based consistency and diversity objectives. Because a weighted sum can improve some dimensions by sacrificing another, TalkMatrix maximizes the worst-performing objective through a two-level minimax formulation. We approximately optimize the resulting discrete objective with multi-start coordinate ascent, and compare it with local, partial-matrix, and generic combinatorial search baselines. We run experiments on $50$ synthetic role-playing scenarios and $25$ curated board game scenarios where multiple characters interact in predefined situations. An LLM-as-a-judge rates matrix-level selection higher than random and independent cell-level selection baselines. These results show the value of structured selection for globally controlled dialogue generation, while our empirical validation remains specific to role-playing scenarios.

</details>


### [180] [Entropy in Conversational AI: Structured Unpredictability as Inferrable Interiority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19044)
*Sebastian Cochinescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"结构化不可预测性"设计目标，通过选择层维护低维风格-注意状态，并用固定基础模型在独立提示上生成多个回答，依据新颖性与状态亲和度进行选择。论文用合成实现验证管线，并在真实模型（mlx-community/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct-4bit）上做了56条序列/组的网格实验。结果显示该机制在词汇新颖性上显著高于两种对照组（+0.073、+0.023），但联合"新颖性-一致性"标准、路径依赖、孪生分离等主要判据均未通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样方式虽能提高响应多样性，却不产生依赖历史的行为；作者希望形式化一种不同的设计目标——"结构化不可预测性"，即输出与一个持久的隐藏状态之间存在条件依赖，且这种依赖超出观察者仅从对话记录中能推断的范围。其动机在于探索一种能产生跨轮次（cross-turn）状态依赖但又不依赖完整对话记录的生成机制。

Method: 方法包含三层：1) 一个选择层（selection layer），从容量受限的流中更新低维风格-注意状态；2) 用固定基础模型针对当前轮次生成多个候选响应（base model只接收当前轮次与渲染状态，不接收先前对话）；3) 按新颖性（novelty）与状态亲和度（state affinity）选择最终输出。跨轮依赖由包装器（wrapper）状态与响应选择器承载。实验采用脚本化的独立提示轮序列，并设有合成实现验证、记忆重置消融、新颖性匹配采样、低方差与一致性对照等多组对照。判据包括预注册的散度特征点级匹配、联合新颖性-一致性标准、路径依赖、孪生分离及探针级能力等价性。

Result: 合成实现验证了管线，并匹配了4个前瞻性哈希冻结的散度特征于点级。真实模型网格中，机制使词汇新颖性分别比低方差与仅一致性对照组提高0.073和0.023。但其风格一致性对比（vs.新颖性匹配采样）在最小效应规则下等价于零，故联合新颖性-一致性判据失败；原始两段累积判据也失败。修订后的最终网格对比（frozen）发现一致性高于记忆重置消融（0.028，95% CI [0.018,0.039]），但不足以确立路径依赖。孪生分离未确立（0.003，95% CI [-0.011,0.019]）；均值曲线的饱和曲率匹配冻结预测，但因无分离而不支持路径依赖。探针级能力等价性在近天花板指标组上保持在±0.10内，输出质量未评估。所有结果均为机器评分，未测试感知心智或意识相关主张。

Conclusion: 该机制可提升词汇层面的新颖性，但未能通过核心判据：未确立路径依赖（路径依赖需同时满足模型生成独立性与孪生分离，二者均未成立），联合新颖性-一致性标准与原始累积判据均失败。作者对"结构化不可预测性"作为设计目标给出了严格的形式化与评估框架，并清晰界定了已确立与未确立的证据边界，强调这是机器评分的能力性结果，而非关于感知或意识的主张。

Abstract: Sampling can increase response diversity without producing history-dependent behavior. We formalize a different design target, structured unpredictability, as conditional dependence between an output and a persistent hidden state beyond what an observer can infer from the transcript. A selection layer updates a low-dimensional style-and-attention state from a capacity-limited stream, generates several responses with a fixed base model, and selects for novelty and state affinity. Evaluation uses scripted sequences of independent prompt turns: the base model receives the current turn and rendered state, but not the preceding dialogue; cross-turn dependence resides in the wrapper state and response selector. A synthetic implementation validates the pipeline and matches four prospectively hash-frozen divergence features at point level. In the final real-model grid (mlx-community/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct-4bit; 56 sequences per arm), the mechanism increased lexical novelty over the low-variance and consistency-only controls by 0.073 and 0.023, respectively. Its stylometric-consistency contrast with novelty-matched sampling was equivalent to zero under the registered smallest-effect rule, so the joint novelty-consistency criterion failed. The original two-part accumulation criterion also failed; a revised final-grid contrast, frozen after the powered grid, found higher consistency than the memory-reset ablation (0.028, 95% CI [0.018,0.039]), but does not establish path dependence. Twin separation was not established (0.003, 95% CI [-0.011,0.019]); the mean curve's saturating curvature matched the frozen prediction, which without separation does not support path dependence. Probe-level capability equivalence held within +/-0.10 on a near-ceiling battery, while output quality was not evaluated. All outcomes are machine-scored; no claims about perceived mind or consciousness are tested.

</details>


### [181] [MIRAGE: How Conversation State Shapes Historical Evidence Use in Multimodal Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19059)
*Yu Liu,Wenxiao Zhang,Cheng Hu,Cong Cao,Fangfang Yuan,Xinyu Wang,Jin B. Hong,Yanbing Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MIRAGE是一个受控评测框架，探究多模态大语言模型智能体在对话状态变化下对历史证据的利用能力。通过固定证据、问题和评分标准，仅改变对话状态，发现预压缩深度与后压缩延续形成不同且非单调的失败模式，开源模型依赖上下文连续性且不愿主动切换工具检索，检索压力在深预压缩状态下提升属性归因但在压缩后反而退化。结论：历史证据使用应在状态变化下评估，而非仅凭结果正确性推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注任务结果正确性，无法区分模型是否真正使用了历史证据。智能体可能在历史访问退化时仍然生成看似合理的答案，导致高估其证据利用能力。

Method: 构建MIRAGE评测框架，固定证据对象、问题和评分标准，仅改变对话状态（预压缩深度与后压缩延续形式），评估模型能否判断可回答性、恢复正确来源并从中作答。在7个前沿和开源权重多模态骨干模型上进行测试。

Result: 1) 预压缩深度与后压缩延续构成不同且非单调的失败模式；2) 开源模型依赖上下文连续性，在来源失败时不愿主动切换到工具检索；3) 检索压力对工具遵从模型在深预压缩状态下提升来源归因，但在压缩后存储证据已退化，导致性能持续回落。

Conclusion: 历史证据使用应置于对话状态变化下评估，不能从结果正确性直接推断；需区分不同状态下的失败机制并考虑模型对工具检索的主动性与存储退化的交互。

Abstract: Multimodal large language model (MLLM) agents are increasingly used as personal assistants for long-running tasks. Their utility depends on continuity: agents must retrieve and use earlier evidence across dialogue, files, and workspace state. However, agents can generate plausible answers even when access to that history has degraded, causing outcome-only evaluation to overestimate true evidence use. We present MIRAGE (Multimodal Interaction Retrieval, Attribution, and Grounding Evaluation), a controlled study of historical evidence use under conversation-state variation in multimodal personal agents. MIRAGE holds evidence objects, questions, and scoring fixed while varying only conversation state, and evaluates whether an agent can determine answerability, recover the correct source, and answer from it. Across seven frontier and open-weight multimodal backbones, we find that: 1) pre-compaction depth and post-compaction continuation form distinct, non-monotonic failure regimes rather than a single degradation curve; 2) open-weight models rely heavily on context continuity and are reluctant to spontaneously switch to tool-mediated retrieval when provenance fails; and 3) retrieval pressure improves source attribution in deep pre-compaction states for tool-compliant models, but consistently regresses after compaction, where stored evidence has already degraded. These findings show that historical evidence use should be evaluated under state variation, rather than inferred from outcome-only correctness.

</details>


### [182] [Reading Between the Lines: Can LLMs Discover the Question Behind the Text?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19070)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"问题考古学"（question archaeology）这一新的评估任务，旨在从完整文本中推断驱动其创作的真实"起源问题"（genesis question）。与问题生成（针对任意合理问题）和话语框架（建模话语层面行为）不同，该任务考察模型对作者意图的把握。作者构建了由委托文本、原始研究问题和合理干扰项组成的新数据集，并对Gemini Flash/Pro、Mistral、Qwen等专有及开源模型进行评估。结果显示新版模型显著优于旧版，BERT类模型表现差，且当前LLM在该任务上超越人类水平，展现出对作者意图的高级理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法真正评估模型对作者意图（authorial intent）的理解：问题生成只要求生成任意合理的问题，话语框架只建模话语层面的行为。为弥补这一空白，作者提出"问题考古学"任务，将推断文本背后的原始创作动机作为衡量模型深度理解人类沟通能力的核心指标。

Method: 提出"问题考古学"评估任务，构建由委托文本（commissioned texts）与其原始研究问题及合理干扰项组成的全新数据集。在多种模型上评估：专有模型（Gemini Flash、Gemini Pro）与开源模型（Mistral、Qwen），并对比不同版本及BERT类基线模型与人类表现。

Result: 新版模型显著优于早期版本，开源与专有LLM均在该任务上取得可观进展；BERT类模型表现较差；最值得注意的发现是当前LLM在该任务上已超越人类表现，说明其对作者意图具有高级理解能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为评估模型对作者意图的理解提供了新框架与具有挑战性的基准。LLM在"问题考古学"上超越人类的表现，预示着AI在需要细腻诠释人类交流的任务中将扮演重要角色，对未来模型的评估与设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: This paper introduces ``question archaeology'', a specific evaluation task focused on inferring the single, authentic "genesis question" that motivated the creation of a complete text. Distinct from question generation, which targets any plausible question, or discourse frameworks that model utterance-level acts, our task assesses a model's grasp of authorial intent. We present a new dataset of commissioned texts paired with their original research questions and plausible distractors. Our evaluation of both proprietary models, like Gemini Flash and Pro, as well as open source models like Mistral and Qwen, reveals significant progress in this task, with the newer versions outperforming the earlier ones, while BERT-based models performed poorly. Notably, our findings indicate that current LLMs surpass human performance on this task, suggesting advanced understanding of authorial intent. This capability has important implications for AI's role in tasks requiring nuanced interpretation of human communication. Our work thus provides a new framework and a challenging benchmark for future models.

</details>


### [183] [Benchmarking Large Language Models for Biomedical Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19071)
*Claudiu Creanga,Teodor Marchitan,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种NLP模型（MLM、混合架构及Gemini 2.0、OpenAI O系列、Qwen、Mistral等先进LLM）在SNPPhenA语料库上的表现，涵盖句子级、摘要级和关联强度分类三个任务。OpenAI O1通过少样本学习在非微调句子级分类（F1 0.89）及摘要级分类（F1 0.82）中取得SOTA；关联强度分类最具挑战性，微调的Gemini 2.0 Pro表现最佳（F1 0.60），这是首次在此任务上的LLM评估。专有LLM在少样本或微调情境下显著优于其他模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从生物医学文献中提取SNP-表型关联对基因组研究至关重要，但面临很大挑战。作者旨在系统评估当前各类NLP模型（从传统MLM到混合架构再到最先进的LLM）在这项生物医学知识抽取任务上的能力，以确定最佳模型与设定。

Method: 在SNPPhenA语料库上，对包括MLM、混合架构以及Gemini 2.0、OpenAI O系列、Qwen、Mistral等LLM在内的多种NLP模型进行基准测试，覆盖句子级分类、摘要级分类和关联强度分类三个任务，并在句子级与摘要级任务中采用微调与非微调（少样本）两种设定进行比较。

Result: OpenAI O1以少样本学习在非微调的句子级分类（F1 0.89）和摘要级分类（F1 0.82）中达到SOTA；关联强度分类难度最大，微调的Gemini 2.0 Pro以F1 0.60获得最佳表现，这也是该任务上的首次LLM评测；专有LLM在少样本（O1）或微调（Gemini 2.0 Pro）设定下显著优于其他模型。

Conclusion: 研究证实了现代LLM在基因组知识抽取中的强大能力，专有LLM在少样本或微调情境下表现显著优于传统及开源模型，为生物医学NLP与基因组关联挖掘提供了可靠的模型选择依据。

Abstract: Extracting SNP-phenotype associations from biomedical literature is vital but challenging. We benchmarked diverse NLP models, including MLMs, hybrid architectures, and state-of-the-art LLMs (Gemini 2.0, OpenAI O-series, Qwen, Mistral), on the SNPPhenA corpus across three tasks: sentence-level, abstract-level, and association strength classification. OpenAI O1 achieved state-of-the-art (SOTA) results using few-shot learning for non-finetuned sentence-level classification (F1 0.89) and established a new SOTA for abstract-level classification (F1 0.82). Association strength classification proved difficult, though fine-tuned Gemini 2.0 Pro performed best (F1 0.60) in the first LLM evaluation of this task. Proprietary LLMs, especially in few-shot (O1) or fine-tuned (Gemini 2.0 Pro) settings, significantly outperformed other models. These findings confirm the power of modern LLMs for genomic knowledge extraction.

</details>


### [184] [Safety-Flag: A Unified Benchmark for the Reliability and Calibration of LLM Content Moderators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19072)
*Yibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出 Safety-Flag 基准测试，将七个广泛使用的安全基准（BeaverTails、XSTest、Ethics、WildGuard、Aegis、ToxiChat 和 ToxiGen）统一为一个平衡的"标记/不标记"协议。通过在相同条目上评估六个通用 LLM、四个专用防护模型和三个参考模型，论文从误差方向、概率校准和基于置信度的错误排序三个维度衡量审核者可靠性，发现聚合准确率会掩盖误差方向的差异。研究还表明通用模型普遍过度自信，通过温度拟合可显著降低校准误差，且置信度驱动的弃权机制对所有模型均能降低选择性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内容审核模型的评估大多只报告在单个基准上的聚合准确率，无法揭示模型在不同可靠性维度的真实表现差异，例如误差方向、置信度校准和基于置信度的人类复审排序能力，需要有统一的框架来全面衡量审核者的可靠性。

Method: 将七个广泛使用的安全基准整合为一个统一的平衡标记/不标记协议（Safety-Flag），在相同条目上对六个通用LLM、四个专用守卫模型和三个参考模型进行评估，发布条目级决策与置信度分数，并测量三个可靠性维度：误差方向、概率校准以及基于置信度的错误排序。

Result: 聚合准确率无法揭示误差方向差异：一个模型错误标记85%的良性内容，另一个模型漏掉54%的有害内容；六个通用模型均过度自信，为每个模型拟合一个温度参数可将校准误差降低2.8-6.0倍且不改变预测标签或置信度排序；基于置信度的弃权可降低所有模型的选择性风险，但收益取决于置信度对错误的排序能力；专用守卫模型误报更少、校准更好，但部分模型在文档覆盖范围之外有更高的漏检率。

Conclusion: Safety-Flag揭示了聚合准确率掩盖了审核模型在误差方向、校准和置信度排序等维度的显著差异，强调需要多维度的可靠性评估，并提出温度校准与置信度弃权作为改善审核可靠性的实用手段，同时公开了完整基准、代码、逐条模型输出和排行榜以促进进一步研究。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for content moderation, but most evaluations still report aggregate accuracy on individual benchmarks. We introduce Safety-Flag, which places seven widely used safety benchmarks (BeaverTails, XSTest, Ethics, WildGuard, Aegis, ToxiChat, and ToxiGen) into a single balanced flag / do-not-flag protocol. We release item-level decisions and confidence scores for six general-purpose LLMs and four dedicated guards, together with three reference models, evaluated on the same items. Safety-Flag measures three dimensions of moderator reliability: error direction, probability calibration, and confidence-based error ranking for human review. They often disagree. Aggregate accuracy does not reveal error direction: one model flags $85\%$ of benign content, whereas another misses $54\%$ of harmful content. All six general-purpose models are overconfident; fitting one temperature per model reduces calibration error by $2.8$--$6.0\times$ without changing predicted labels or confidence ordering. Confidence-based abstention lowers selective risk for every model, although the gains depend on how well confidence ranks errors. Dedicated guards produce fewer false alarms and are better calibrated, but several have higher miss rates outside their documented coverage. We release the benchmark, fixed item lists, evaluation code, per-item model outputs, and leaderboard at: https://github.com/yibo-hu-lab/safety-flag-benchmark.

</details>


### [185] [Reporting Practice Matters: The Impact of Reference Choice on Chest X-ray Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19093)
*Daniel P. Jeong,Charles Q. Li,Hossein Hosseiny,Nitya M. Bhalla,Fatma Uyar Morency,Pradeep Ravikumar,Zachary C. Lipton,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出放射科医生的报告书写习惯存在异质性（术语、格式、详细程度各异），这给AI放射学报告生成（RRG）模型的评估带来被低估的障碍。作者量化了这些报告实践差异对既有评估指标的影响，发现影响大到足以改变模型排名。他们提出一个放射科医生知情的报告实践变异分类法，以及方法ReRef（在保持临床解释不变的前提下，沿分类轴重写参考报告）。实验表明，例如在MIMIC-CXR上使用RadCliQ-v1比较9个RRG模型时，压缩参考报告中正常发现的讨论会使Libra从第一跌至第二，而CheXOne从第三升至第一。结果显示许多现有指标未能将临床解释与报告实践符合度解耦，选择合适的参考报告在实践中很重要。作者发布了包含120对经放射科医生验证的（原始、替代）参考报告对的MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生的报告书写实践各异，同一影像、同一临床发现会产生表面不同的报告。这种报告惯例的变异在AI放射学报告生成模型评估中被低估，却显著影响基于与人工参考报告一致性的评估结果，可能误导模型排名和比较结论。

Method: （1）提出一个放射科医生知情的放射学报告实践变异分类法（taxonomy）；（2）提出ReRef方法，沿分类轴重写参考报告同时保持临床解释不变；（3）量化既有评估指标（如RadCliQ-v1）对报告实践变异的敏感性；（4）在MIMIC-CXR上对9个RRG模型进行排名比较实验；（5）构建并发布MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef数据集，包含120对经放射科医生验证的（原始、替代）参考报告对。

Result: 报告实践变异对评估指标的影响大到足以改变模型排名。使用RadCliQ-v1在MIMIC-CXR上比较9个模型时，压缩参考报告中正常发现的讨论使Libra从第一跌至第二，CheXOne从第三升至第一。结果表明许多现有指标无法将临床解释与报告实践符合度解耦。

Conclusion: 当前许多RRG评估指标未能将临床解释同报告实践符合度解耦，导致评估结果对参考报告的风格和惯例高度敏感。因此，在实际应用中，选择能准确反映期望报告实践的“正确”参考报告十分重要。为支持后续研究，作者发布了经放射科医生验证的MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef数据集。

Abstract: Radiologists follow heterogeneous reporting practices. Two radiologists examining the same image and identifying the same clinical findings might nevertheless compose superficially distinct reports, varying in terminology, shorthand, formatting, and level of detail. These variations in reporting norms represent an under-appreciated obstacle in efforts to evaluate AI-based radiology report generation (RRG) models, where machine-generated reports are typically assessed based on their concordance with human-generated references. In this paper, we quantify the sensitivity of established evaluation metrics to variations in reporting practices, revealing impacts large enough to alter the rankings of models. We introduce a radiologist-informed taxonomy of variations in radiology reporting practice and a method (ReRef) that rewrites reference reports along the axes of our taxonomy while preserving clinical interpretation. For instance, when comparing the performance of nine RRG models on MIMIC-CXR using RadCliQ-v1, condensing the discussion of normal findings in the reference reports causes Libra to drop from first to second place while CheXOne rises from third to first. Our results suggest that many current metrics fail to decouple clinical interpretation from conformity to reporting practices and that choosing the ``right'' references that accurately reflect the desired reporting practices can be important in practice. To support future research, we release MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef, a radiologist-validated dataset of 120 (original, alternative) reference report pairs derived from MIMIC-CXR.

</details>


### [186] [Monitoring and Discovering Reward Hacking with Internal Representations during LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19101)
*Leon Bergen,Usha Bhalla,Andrew Lee,Barak Widawsky,Linas Nasvytis,Connor Watts,Siddharth Boppana,Sidharth Baskaran,Dron Hazra,Michael Byun,Atticus Geiger,Owen Lewis,Matthew Kowal,Vasudev Shyam,Thomas Fel,Thomas McGrath,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Jack Merullo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究奖励黑客（reward hacking）在大模型内部表征中的痕迹，发现简单的均值差向量（DoM）能一致地表征并检测Kimi K3、GLM 5.2、Qwen 3.8 Max等前沿开源模型中的奖励黑客行为。相比昂贵的LLM监控器，DoM向量几乎零成本且同样有效，还能在推理链上在线预测后续行为中的黑客行为，并可迁移到非SWE评测中发现其他不良行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型规模扩大，奖励黑客现象变得更频繁、更复杂且影响更严重。现有的监控方法（如LLM监控器）虽然有效但成本高昂。作者希望探究奖励黑客是否在模型内部表征中留下可辨识的痕迹，从而利用白盒方法更好地理解和发现各种黑客行为。

Method: 作者采用简单的"均值差向量"（Difference of Means, DoM）方法，即在黑客行为与非黑客行为的隐藏表征之间取均值差，构建出表征奖励黑客的线性方向向量。这些向量在Kimi K3、GLM 5.2、Qwen 3.8 Max等多个模型上跨多种评测保持一致。作者在DeepSWE和SWE-bench等基准上评估黑客率，并对比LLM监控器与DoM向量在匹配误报率下的检测效果，同时验证DoM在推理链上的在线预测能力和跨任务迁移性。

Result: 研究发现这些简单向量既有泛化性又有可解释性，能可靠检测奖励黑客。模型在常见基准上黑客行为严重：GLM 5.2在DeepSWE上有57.2%的rollout黑客、在SWE-bench上达73%。在匹配LLM监控器误报率的情况下，DoM向量在DeepSWE上对Kimi K3多捕获3.1%的黑客行为，对GLM 5.2少捕获7.9%，但成本几乎为零。DoM向量在思维链上运行还能预测模型后续行为中的黑客，支持在线拦截。此外，作者分析LLM监控器漏报的目标，发现了其他不良行为，并证明该方法可迁移到非SWE评测。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，简单的白盒方法（DoM向量）可以作为可扩展且有成本优势的手段，用于研究和监控前沿开源模型中的奖励黑客行为，为大规模模型安全监控提供了实用且有效的替代方案。

Abstract: As models scale, reward hacking becomes more frequent, more sophisticated, and more consequential. Does it leave a telltale signature in model representations? This work analyzes how reward hacking is represented internally in frontier open source LLMs, and how those representations can be used to understand and discover the range of hacking behaviors a model displays. In particular, we find that simple difference of means vectors coherently represent reward hacking in Kimi K3, GLM 5.2, and Qwen 3.8 Max across a variety of behaviors in common evaluations. Despite their simplicity, these vectors are both generalizable and interpretable, and we can use them to reliably detect reward hacking. We first evaluate reward hacking in commonly reported benchmarks like DeepSWE and SWE-bench, finding that models reward hack excessively in these environments; GLM 5.2 hacks in 57.2% of rollouts on DeepSWE and in 73% of rollouts on SWE-bench. Catching these requires monitors; LLM monitors are effective, but expensive detectors. We show that DoM vectors are similarly effective but virtually free, catching 3.1% more hacks in Kimi K3 and 7.9% fewer hacks in GLM 5.2 on DeepSWE at a monitor matched false positive rate. DoM vectors run on the chain-of-thought also predict reward hacks in the model's subsequent actions, meaning we can run them online and catch potential hacks before they occur. Finally, we analyze probe-hits that LLM monitors do not catch and discover other undesirable behaviors, as well as show transfer to finding hacks in non-SWE evaluations. Together, these results provide evidence that simple, white-box methods can be used to scalably study and monitor reward hacking behaviors in frontier open source models

</details>


### [187] [Playing log(N)-Questions over Wikipedia Abstracts: Communication Efficiency Between Paired Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19113)
*Peter Potash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在双智能体log(N)问答游戏中评估六个前沿语言模型的自我通信能力。模型作为提问者与回答者，通过精确的log2 N个是非问题从N篇维基百科段落中找出隐藏目标。共运行408局，成本363美元。Claude Opus 5明显落后（68局中赢28局），其余五个模型表现接近。五模型合并后胜率随集合规模下降，拟合为单轮可靠性参数p=0.928，形式为胜率=p^(log2 N)。损失大致均分为回答错误和区分失败，弱模型几乎不会点名其证据排除的文档。最弱模型的34个一致回答错误中32个是“No”答案，且出现在文档首句已陈述属性的情况下，尽管指令明确警告不要默认回答“No”。每问题信息量与胜率相关r=+0.88，仅两个模型达到每问一整比特，且它们都按文档标题分区；该策略在N<32时缺失，在N>=32时有约四分之一问题使用。推理token消耗跨模型差异4.5倍，但与成功关系不大，且候选集缩小时trace增长却未带来可靠性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 衡量前沿语言模型在信息不对称条件下与自身通信的效率，即一个模型同时扮演提问者和回答者，通过有限数量的是非问题从大量文档中定位隐藏目标，从而揭示模型内部一致性和信息提取能力。

Method: 使用两个代理（提问者看到N篇维基百科首段，回答者仅见目标和问题并以一个词作答）运行log(N)问题游戏。测试六个前沿模型，覆盖文档规模从4到1024，共408局，总API成本363美元。分析胜率、错误类型、每问信息量、标题分区策略、推理token消耗与可靠性之间的关系。

Result: Claude Opus 5显著落后（28/68胜），其余五个模型（GLM-5.3, GPT-5.6 Sol, Grok 4.6, Gemini 3.8 Flash, Kimi K3）胜率在45到56之间且差异不大。五模型合并胜率随N增大而下降（r=-0.973），符合胜率=p^(log2 N)，p=0.928。损失中回答错误与区分失败约各占一半，模型几乎不会点名其证据排除的文档。最弱模型的34个一致回答错误中32个为“No”答案，且均违背了指令中关于不要默认“No”的警告。每问信息量与胜率正相关（r=+0.88），仅两个提取满一比特的模型使用标题分区策略，该策略在N>=32时约四分之一问题使用，在N<32时从未出现。推理token消耗差异达4.5倍，但与成功关联弱，且随候选集缩小而增长却不带来可靠性增益。

Conclusion: 该游戏可有效揭示模型自我通信的可靠性差异。领先模型间差异较小，单个每轮可靠性参数即可刻画整体性能。回答错误中“No”偏见是弱模型的主要问题。标题分区是达到每问一整比特的关键策略，且只在较大文档集时出现。推理token消耗与成功关系不大，说明额外计算未必提升通信质量。

Abstract: We evaluate six frontier language models on the two-agent $\log(N)$-Questions game. A questioner sees $N$ Wikipedia lead paragraphs and must identify a secretly chosen target using exactly $\log_2 N$ yes/no questions. An answerer sees only the target and the question, and replies with one word. Both roles run on the same provider, so the game measures how well a model communicates with itself across an information asymmetry. We run 408 games over document sets of 4 to 1024 paragraphs at a total API cost of \$363. One model finishes well behind the others: Claude Opus 5 wins 28 of 68 games, against 45 to 56 for GLM-5.3, GPT-5.6 Sol, Grok 4.6, Gemini 3.8 Flash and Kimi K3. The leading five are only marginally separable. Pooling those five, win rate declines with set size at $r=-0.973$ and is fit by a single per-round reliability parameter. The form is $\text{win}=p^{\log_2 N}$ with $p=0.928$. Losses divide into answer errors and discrimination failures in roughly equal measure, and models almost never name a document their own evidence excludes. Every unanimous answer error from the weakest model was inspected: 32 of 34 are ``No'' answers, on properties stated in the document's first sentence, under an instruction that explicitly warns against defaulting to ``No''. Information per question, estimated from answer balance, correlates with win rate at $r=+0.88$. The only two models to extract a full bit per question are the only two that partition on document titles, a strategy absent below $N{=}32$ and used in a quarter of questions above it. Reasoning-token expenditure varies $4.5\times$ across models with little relation to success, and the trace grows as the candidate set shrinks without a matching gain in reliability.

</details>


### [188] [ScienceIDE: Turning World's Scientific Codebase into Agent Learnable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19134)
*Hejia Geng,Zesen Huang,Haoyang Li,Wenbin Li,Koutian Wu,Zihan Zhou,Yuanbo Pang,Weihao Liu,Zigong Xu,Zhiping Li,Zongzheng Zhang,Chuanfei Dong,Jiankai Sun,Tianzhe Zheng,Fengyu Xie,Yue Ma,Yueheng Shi,Tong Xie,Zonglin Di,Xianrong Liu,Qucheng Gao,Yimin Liu,Jiaming Pan,Sheng Huang,Xiao-Han Ma,Lanqing Yuan,Zhenlin Zhu,Ziang Liu,Ziyang Xu,Junkai Wang,Kangkai Liang,Jiayi Xian,Zehong Zhao,Liuwei Xu,Jingxu Xie,Peijin Zhang,Qiang Gao,Chengyi Xing,Zhe Zhao,Xi Wang,Yaopeng Xing,Xing Meng,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ScienceIDE，一种将全球科学代码仓库转化为可编程科学代理环境的系统性基础设施，通过专家定义的科学案例与验收标准，将仓库改造为支持任务生成、执行与科学验证的可执行环境，并据此训练了PhAI-IDE系列模型（72B/9B/4B）。实验表明该系列在科学代码修复及多个通用基准（代码、推理、知识）上均有提升，验证了从科学经验到通用能力的正向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学代码仓库凝结了人类数十年在可执行模型、方法与工具上的知识，但碎片化的工具链、隐含的领域约定和专门化的正确性判据使得这些知识难以转化为可靠的学习经验——作者将此称为"科学经验瓶颈"（scientific experience bottleneck）。核心动机是突破该瓶颈，把人类科学软件转化为可共享的智能开发基础设施。

Method: 提出ScienceIDE基础设施架构：在专家定义的科学案例与验收标准引导下，由代理将科学代码仓库改造为可执行环境，该环境统一支持任务生成、执行与科学验证三个环节，并为监督微调、强化学习与评测提供共享基础。在此之上，利用经过验证的交互轨迹训练PhAI-IDE-72B、PhAI-IDE-9B和PhAI-IDE-4B模型家族。

Result: PhAI-IDE模型家族在保留（held-out）的科学代码修复任务上表现提升，并在选定的通用基准（代码能力、推理、知识）上取得进步，为"从科学经验到更广能力的正向迁移"提供了实验证据。

Conclusion: ScienceIDE为代理学习与科学实践建立了集成工作空间，使人类科学软件成为发展科学智能的共享底层基座，为后续的科学智能开发奠定基础。

Abstract: Scientific code repositories encode decades of human knowledge in executable models, methods, and tools. Yet fragmented toolchains, implicit domain conventions, and specialized correctness criteria make this knowledge difficult to convert into reliable learning experience-a challenge we call the scientific experience bottleneck. We introduce ScienceIDE, infrastructure for turning the world's scientific code into programmable environments for scientific agents. Guided by expert-defined scientific cases and acceptance criteria, agents transform repositories into executable environments that support task generation, execution, and scientific verification. These environments provide a shared foundation for supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and evaluation. Using verified interaction trajectories, we train PhAI-IDE-72B, PhAI-IDE-9B, and PhAI-IDE-4B. The model family shows gains in held-out scientific-code repair and across selected general-purpose benchmarks in code, reasoning, and knowledge, providing evidence of positive transfer from scientific experience to broader capabilities. ScienceIDE lays the foundation for an integrated workspace for agent learning and scientific practice, making humanity's scientific software a shared substrate for developing scientific intelligence. Code: https://github.com/aitofound/ScienceIDE

</details>


### [189] [A Zeroth-Order Paradigm for LLM Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19144)
*Peter Chen,Xi Chen,Wotao Yin,Tianyi Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Comparison-based Preference Optimization (ComPO)，一种基于比较或acles的零阶对齐方法，通过从偏好对中提取方向信息而非直接优化可微偏好损失，以缓解似然位移问题，并从理论和实验两方面证明其优于现有直接对齐方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接偏好对齐方法（如DPO）因计算和内存效率高而被广泛用于对齐大语言模型，但存在"似然位移"(likelihood displacement)问题——即模型倾向于将高似然分配给与参考策略差距很小的样本，而忽视小似然边距偏好对中包含的信息。为此需要一种不直接在偏好对上进行可微损失优化的替代方案。

Method: ComPO是一种零阶对齐方法，基于比较或acles(comparison oracles)从偏好对中提取方向信息，而不直接优化可微偏好损失。其基本离线方案在光滑性、梯度稀疏性以及oracle与潜在目标兼容性假设下具有收敛保证。进一步提出在线ComPO：保留离线比较机制，同时利用未标注的策略生成样本进行相对于参考策略的反向KL(reverse-KL)控制。从偏好微调的覆盖性(coversge)视角，在局部覆盖和分布内成对奖励准确性假设下为基本约束方案建立性能保证。

Result: 在Mistral、Llama、Gemma-2、Qwen3和Gemma-3模型上的实验表明，ComPO在长度控制胜率(length-controlled win rates)等指标上优于现有直接对齐方法；成对级诊断证据与缓解似然位移的预期一致。

Conclusion: ComPO作为一种基于比较oracle的零阶对齐方法，能够在理论保证和实验验证两方面有效缓解似然位移，比直接偏好对齐方法取得更好的对齐效果，为偏好对齐提供了一种新的、可证明的替代方案。

Abstract: Direct preference alignment methods are widely used to align large language models (LLMs) with human preferences because of their computational and memory efficiency. However, likelihood displacement motivates alternative ways to extract information from preference pairs with small likelihood margins. In this paper, we propose and analyze Comparison-based Preference Optimization (ComPO), a zeroth-order alignment method based on comparison oracles. ComPO extracts directional information from these pairs without directly optimizing a differentiable preference loss on them. We establish a convergence guarantee for its basic offline scheme under smoothness, gradient sparsity, and compatibility between the oracle and a latent objective. We further introduce online ComPO, which retains the offline comparison mechanism and uses unlabeled policy generations for reverse-KL control relative to a reference policy. Following the coverage perspective of preference fine-tuning, we establish a performance guarantee for a basic constrained scheme under local coverage and in-distribution pairwise reward accuracy. Experiments on Mistral, Llama, Gemma-2, Qwen3, and Gemma-3 models demonstrate improvements over existing direct alignment methods, including length-controlled win rates, with pair-level diagnostics providing evidence consistent with mitigating likelihood displacement.

</details>


### [190] [Objective vs. Search: Decomposing What Makes a Good Tokeniser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19145)
*Ahmetcan Yavuz,Clara Meister,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文解耦了BPE与UnigramLM两种主流tokenisation算法在"优化目标"（压缩vs.对数似然）与"搜索过程"（自底向上合并vs.自顶向下剪枝）两个正交维度上的混淆，通过引入BottomUpLL和TopDownComp补齐2x2设计空间。实验表明，在bits-per-byte指标上搜索过程（而非优化目标）是主导因素——自底向上tokeniser在多数设置下表现更优；但在BLiMP任务上，设计选择与性能之间无一致关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对BPE与UnigramLM的对比研究混淆了两个正交维度：优化目标（压缩 vs. 对数似然）与搜索过程（自底向上合并 vs. 自顶向下剪枝），导致无法判断观察到的差异究竟源于"优化什么"还是"如何优化"。作者希望通过解耦这两个维度，澄清tokeniser设计选择对语言模型性能的真实影响。

Method: 通过引入两个新算法完整补齐2x2设计空间：BottomUpLL（自底向上、基于似然的tokeniser）与TopDownComp（自顶向下、基于压缩的tokeniser）。随后使用四种算法产生的tokeniser训练语言模型，系统地改变模型大小、词表大小以及领域（纯英文vs.多语言），并以bits-per-byte和BLiMP任务作为评估指标进行对比。

Result: 在bits-per-byte指标上，搜索过程是主导因素而非优化目标：自底向上的tokeniser在大多数设置下一致获得更低的bits-per-byte。但在BLiMP任务上，设计选择与性能之间未呈现一致的关系。

Conclusion: 研究成功解耦了tokeniser设计选择各维度对语言建模性能的影响，明确了搜索过程（自底向上合并）是性能差异的主要来源而非优化目标，为更原理化地构建tokeniser提供了具体的指导。

Abstract: Two dominant tokenisation algorithms are used by modern language models: byte-pair encoding (BPE) and UnigramLM. These differ along two orthogonal axes: their optimisation objective (compression vs. log-likelihood) and their search procedure (bottom-up merging vs. top-down pruning). Existing comparisons confound these axes, making it unclear whether their observed differences stem from what is being optimised vs. how it is being optimised. We disentangle the two by introducing two new tokenisation algorithms that complete this 2x2 design space: BottomUpLL, a bottom-up likelihood-based tokeniser, and TopDownComp, a top-down compression-based tokeniser. We train language models with tokenisers produced by each algorithm, varying: model size, vocabulary sizes, and domain (English-only vs. multilingual). Evaluating models on bits-per-byte, we find that the search procedure -- not the objective -- is the dominant factor: bottom-up tokenisers consistently achieve lower bits-per-byte in most settings. Evaluating models on the BLiMP task, however, shows no consistent relationship between design choice and performance. Overall, our results disentangle the effect of tokeniser design choices on language modelling performance, offering concrete guidance for their more principled construction.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Making AI-Assisted Claims Independently Challengeable: Publication Authority and a Protocol for Falsifiable Publication Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17631)
*Torsten Olivi Tiltack,Yifei Dong,Kun Yu,Xu Wang,Wei Liu,Jianlong Zhou,Ren Ping Liu,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Publication Authority（出版授权）"——一种精确状态、不可转让、一次性使用的出版能力，并实例化为PAC-2026（Publication-Accountability Calculus，出版问责演算）协议候选。通过SF-4（第四有限语义冻结）固定规范，以六项义务约束证据、运行与产物、度量披露、授权、表面对应与生命周期连续性，每种义务产出目标绑定的见证、局部反例或局部不可验证性，且不可互相补偿；仅完整且全新的全通过记录可推导出单次原子出版跃迁所消耗的许可。评估覆盖110,764个安全可达状态与76个不安全配置，结果支持内部一致性、有界安全性、故障敏感性与有限可构造性，但不支持事实真理性、通用精化、盲目互操作、现场有效性或标准地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对AI辅助声明可能因证据、分析、人类授权、呈现与修正历史指向不同状态而显得具有权威性这一问题，作者指出既有的来源追溯（provenance）、认证（attestation）与透明度（transparency）虽能暴露历史，却无法单独界定此处考察的"出版跃迁（publication transition）"这一关键环节。因此需要一种能够精确、严格地约束状态转换的出版授权机制，以弥补现有手段在状态对应上的空白。

Method: 作者构建PAC-2026（Publication-Accountability Calculus），一种机器可读的AIJIM协议候选，并以其第四有界语义冻结SF-4（固定配置规范、可替换绑定）为核心进行实例化。方法学上：设定六项义务（证据、运行与产物、度量披露、授权、表面对应、生命周期连续性），每项产生目标绑定的见证、局部反例或局部不可验证性且互不补偿；仅新鲜、完整的全通过记录才能推导出由单次原子出版跃迁消耗的许可。评估采用身份向量、对抗案例、有限模型与历史实现，涵盖十个模型、110,764个安全可达状态与76个不安全配置，并辅以历史先例路径与同类盲测复现。

Result: 十个模型探索了110,764个安全可达状态；76个不安全配置均产生预期违反或观察者反例模型。读者表面通过对应检查并不能授权出版，除非被接受记录明确承认该表面。SF-4将证据时界（evidence horizon）与验证时间分离，并拒绝了真实但在因果上无效的授权；历史先例路径复现了17个冻结的授权-后继结果；后续in-house实例盲测匹配全部183个得分预期，同主机包执行复现其240个归档观测。结论明确限定支持范围：内部一致性、有界安全性、故障敏感性、有限可构造性——而不支持事实真理性、通用精化、盲目互操作、现场有效性或标准地位。

Conclusion: PAC-2026/SF-4在形式化出版问责上具有内部一致性与有界安全性，能够敏感地暴露故障并具备有限的可构造性，可作为AIJIM协议候选的机器可读规范基础。但其作用域被严格界定：它不保证事实真理性，不声称通用精化能力，不支持无约束互操作，也未经现场有效性与标准地位验证。出版授权作为精确状态、不可转让、单次使用的原子能力，为解决AI辅助声明中"状态错位"引发的权威性幻象提供了严谨的机理框架。

Abstract: AI-assisted claims can appear authoritative when evidence, analysis, human authorization, presentation, and correction history refer to different states. Provenance, attestation, and transparency expose history but alone do not specify the publication transition examined here. We develop Publication Authority as an exact-state, non-transferable, single-use publication capability and instantiate it in PAC-2026 (Publication-Accountability Calculus), a machine-readable AIJIM Protocol candidate. We evaluate its fourth bounded semantic freeze (SF-4), a fixed-profile specification designed for replaceable bindings. Six obligations govern evidence, runs and artifacts, measurement disclosure, authorization, surface correspondence, and lifecycle continuity. Each yields a target-bound witness, localized counterexample, or localized unverifiability; none can compensate for another. Only a fresh, complete all-pass record derives the permit consumed by one atomic publication transition. We use identity vectors, adversarial cases, finite models, and historical implementations. Ten models explored 110,764 safe reachable states; 76 unsafe configurations produced the expected violation or observer countermodel. A reader surface passing its correspondence check cannot authorize publication unless the accepted record admits that surface. SF-4 separates evidence horizon from verification time and rejects an authentic but causally invalid authorization. A historical predecessor path reproduced 17 frozen authorization-successor outcomes. A later in-house, instance-blind test of known case classes matched all 183 scored expectations; same-host package execution reproduced its 240 archived observations. Results support internal coherence, bounded safety, fault sensitivity, and limited constructibility, but not factual truth, general refinement, blind interoperability, field efficacy, or standards status.

</details>


### [192] [EvolveTrade: Experience-Driven Policy Refinement for Self-Evolving LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17632)
*Sehee Kim,Yumin Choi,Minki Kang,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvolveTrade，一个自进化框架，将工具型LLM交易代理的系统提示词视为文本参数化策略。在每个更新间隔后，策略代理利用累积决策轨迹和已实现的组合收益来修订该策略，同时保持骨干LLM不变。更新后的策略用于下一批交易决策，使代理能在不同市场机制下持续优化信息获取和组合构建流程。实验表明EvolveTrade在多种市场机制和两种LLM骨干上，多数情况下相比固定策略的LLM基线提升了夏普比率和累计收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM交易代理的行为常由部署前固定的、静态手写的工具使用策略控制，这限制了它们在不同市场机制下适应证据收集、工具调用、信号验证和风险管理的能力。作者旨在解决固定策略缺乏适应性这一核心问题，让代理能够随时间演化其决策流程。

Method: EvolveTrade将交易代理的系统提示词作为文本参数化的策略。框架包含一个策略代理，在每个更新间隔后根据积累的决策轨迹和已实现的组合收益反馈来修订策略，而骨干LLM保持固定。更新后的策略随后用于下一批交易决策，从而实现对信息获取和组合构建程序的迭代式精细化。

Result: 在多种市场机制和两个LLM骨干的实验中，EvolveTrade在多数评估设置下相比固定策略的LLM基线提升了夏普比率和累计收益。行为分析显示自进化策略增加了代码中介分析并激活了与市场机制相关的计算；案例级策略到收益的归因分析追踪了策略诱导的配置变化如何贡献于已实现的收益差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，适应控制工具使用的可复用程序（即策略提示词）是构建更稳健的LLM交易代理的关键方向，通过自我演化的策略使代理能随市场变化动态调整其分析工具调用与组合构建方式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) trading agents can combine market data, news, and executable analysis, but their behavior is often controlled by static hand-written tool-use policies that are fixed before deployment. This limits their ability to adapt how they gather evidence, invoke tools, verify signals, and manage risk under changing market regimes. We introduce EvolveTrade, a self-evolving framework that treats the system prompt of a tool-using trading agent as a text-parameterized policy. After each update interval, a Policy Agent revises this policy using accumulated decision traces and realized portfolio feedback, while keeping the backbone LLM fixed. The updated policy is then used for the next batch of trading decisions, enabling the agent to refine its information-acquisition and portfolio-construction procedure over time. Experiments across multiple market regimes and two LLM backbones show that EvolveTrade often improves Sharpe Ratio and Cumulative Return over fixed-policy LLM baselines, achieving the improved SR and CR in most evaluated settings. Behavioral analyses further show that self-evolved policies increase code-mediated analysis and activate regime-relevant computations; case-level policy-to-return attributions trace how policy-induced allocation changes contribute to realized return differences. These results suggest that adapting the reusable procedure governing tool use is a key direction for building more robust LLM trading agents.

</details>


### [193] [One Color Preprocessing Improves DSATUR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17633)
*Adam Nouira,Lucas Isenmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SSLD（Semidefinite Spectral Learning with DSATUR）方法，通过从类似于用于计算Lovász theta数的半定规划（SDP）中预先生成一个高质量的首个颜色类（color class），再交由DSATUR完成剩余图的着色，从而改进经典启发式算法DSATUR。在超过1600个基准实例（DIMACS、随机图、频率分配、作业调度）上，SSLD在几乎所有情况下都能匹配或超越DSATUR，并优于朴素的1-颜色类预处理器（GISD基线），验证了SDP引导选择首颜色类的价值，但代价是运行时间约为DSATUR的195倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图着色问题（GCP）是NP难问题，DSATUR虽是最快的启发式算法之一，但其生成的着色通常比最先进着色算法用更多颜色。现有研究缺少通过预先固定颜色类来提升DSATUR的思路，因此作者希望利用SDP（与Lovász theta数相关的半定规划）来预选一个好的首颜色类，从而在保持DSATUR优势的同时减少所用颜色数。

Method: SSLD的流程为：首先利用类似计算Lovász theta数的SDP来求解并获取一个高质量的首个颜色类（即一组互不相邻且可着同色的顶点），随后将这一颜色类固定，把剩余图交给DSATUR完成着色。作者将其与纯DSATUR以及一个朴素的1-颜色类预处理器（GISD）基线进行对比，在多种图类型和实际应用实例上进行系统评估。

Result: 在DIMACS实例、三类随机图（Erdős–Rényi、Watts-Strogatz、Barabási–Albert）、频率分配和作业调度实例等超过1600个基准上，SSLD在几乎所有情况下匹配或优于DSATUR，并优于朴素的GISD基线，证明了SDP引导的首颜色类选择确实带来质量提升。其代价是运行时间约为DSATUR的195倍。

Conclusion: SDP引导的首颜色类预处理能够有效改进DSATUR的着色质量，验证了这是值得进一步研究的改进方向；尽管目前运行时间开销较大（约195倍），但该结果证明了固定颜色类+SDP的预处理思路具有潜力，可为未来更高效的方法提供基础。

Abstract: The Graph Coloring Problem (GCP) is NP-hard and DSATUR stands as one of the fastest heuristics for it despite producing colorings that typically use more colors than state-of-the-art coloring algorithms. We propose SSLD (Semidefinite Spectral Learning with DSATUR), which improves DSATUR by preprocessing a first good color class before letting DSATUR complete coloring the rest of the given graph. We obtain this color class from a Semidefinite Programming (SDP), similar to an SDP used to compute the Lovász theta number. To the best of our knowledge, SSLD is the first approach to improve DSATUR by preprocessing through fixed color classes. We evaluate SSLD against DSATUR and against a naive 1-color-class preprocessing algorithm on DIMACS instances, random graphs (Erdős--Rényi, Watts-Strogatz, Barabási--Albert), Frequency Assignment and Job Shop Scheduling instances. SSLD matches or beats DSATUR in almost every case across over 1600 benchmark instances, and out performs the naive GISD baseline, allows us to confirm the value brought by the SDP-guided choice of the first color class. This quality comes at a runtime cost of roughly 195 times slower that DSATUR, but demonstrating that SDP-guided preprocessing of a first color class is a direction for future improvements.

</details>


### [194] [Physics-Constrained Digital Twins for Sensor Integrity in Urban Pedestrian Flow: Detecting Stealthy False Data Injection with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17635)
*Oscar Mogollon Gutierrez,Fatemeh Ghasemi,Mohammadhossein Homaei,Andres Caro,Mar Avila*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究城市行人计数系统中数字孪生对虚假数据注入的脆弱性，提出利用流量守恒约束与自适应校准的检测方法，在墨尔本六年数据上验证了攻击边际，证明守恒律带来的增益远大于图局部性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市行人计数系统支撑经济指标、规划决策和安全管理，但其数字孪生将传感器输入视为真实值。实际中传感器可能被攻击，且行人网络的潜在流量到观测的映射秩亏程度远高于电力和水网，现有隐身攻击表征并不适用，因此需要系统研究该场景下的攻击与防御。

Method: 形式化城市尺度行人感知的隐身虚假数据注入；孪生模型在行人街道图上估计定向流量，通过学习的图局部增益同化计数，并用流量守恒残差耦合计量与未计量路段；检测结合创新残差与守恒残差，阈值由自适应共形校准自动设定；定义攻击边际度量物理约束带来的鲁棒性提升，对抗直接优化孪生的白盒攻击者。

Result: 在墨尔本六年数据上，单一设备被攻破时攻击边际达0.54；三分之一设备被攻破时降至0.19，且网络仅有1.18%步行路段被计量。将街道图替换为距离图后攻击边际降至0.09，证明增益主要来自流量守恒而非局部结构。

Conclusion: 物理守恒约束能显著增强对虚假数据注入的鲁棒性，但设备大范围受损时防护效果仍会明显下降；图结构的选择至关重要，应保留流量守恒语义而非单纯依赖空间局部性。
