Abstract: Current Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) paradigms generally treat query routing and agent fine-tuning as separate processes, limiting their ability to respond to evolving agent capabilities. This disconnect prevents routing strategies from adapting to evolving agent capabilities during post-training and prevents agents from achieving synergistic data-driven specialization. To resolve this, we introduce CERA-MoA (Co-Evolving Router with continually learning Agents for Mixture-of-Agents), an iterative reinforcement learning framework where the dynamic router and independent agent policies co-evolve. We design a predictive familiarity estimator that leverages mid-layer hidden states to evaluate semantic competence among agents, avoiding the overhead of full rollouts. Based on these familiarity scores, a cumulative-threshold adaptive routing mechanism dynamically activates a tailored minimal agent subset, achieving a trade-off between task performance and efficiency. By proactively allocating targeted training samples to agents based on their evolving competence, CERA-MoA promotes capability differentiation. Extensive experiments across various domains demonstrate that CERA-MoA outperforms state-of-the-art static-agent routing and fix-workflow fine-tuning baselines.

</details>


### [261] [Compositional Policy Violations: When Step-Level Compliance Fails In Agentic AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18820)
*Ashwini Kurady,Sri Sai Charith Grandhi,Rajesh Gupta,Sumit Mamoria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示智能体工作流治理中"策略与执行范围不匹配"的系统性问题：现有治理仅针对单个步骤（输入输出分类器、逐轮护栏、span级评估器），而组织真实策略（如转介阈值、权限上限、审查要求）是整次执行的属性。这种错位导致一种称为"组合策略违例(CPV)"的失效模式——每个步骤单独通过自身检查，但组合后的执行整体违反治理策略。文章提出CPV的四类分类法，并论证正确修复方式由被守护量的变更位置决定，最后引入一个基于血缘(provenance)的运行时架构，在完整执行轨迹上评估策略、从原始血缘而非流水线的派生表示中重算被守护量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机在于：监管环境中智能体工作流做出的决策影响重大，但现有的治理机制几乎全部是"步骤作用域"(step-scoped)的。组织真正持有的策略（授权上限、转介阈值、审查要求等）本质上是整次执行的组合属性，而非任何单一步骤的属性。这种范围不匹配使系统在"每一步都合规、整体却违例"的状态下失效，且由于单步谓词无法评估该步骤自身并未决定的属性，单纯提高步骤级监控器的精度也无法检测这一失效类别。因此需要将治理从步骤级别提升到执行轨迹级别。

Method: 方法上，作者首先将CPV形式化定义为"步骤级合规性无法组合(compose)"的失效，即各步骤均合规但整体执行违反策略。随后提出四种CPV类型的分类学：Authority Creep（权限扩张）、Threshold Laundering（阈值洗白）、Cumulative Sum Violation（累积和违例）、Context Collapse（上下文坍缩）。关键洞察是：每一类的正确修复方式由"被守护量(guarded quantity)在何处发生变更"决定。基于此，作者引入一个基于血缘感知的运行时架构，在整个执行轨迹上评估策略，从原始血缘数据而非流水线的派生表示中重算被守护量，从而保证策略评估基于真实来源而非中间表示。

Result: 主要成果包括：1）定义并形式化了一个此前未命名的重要失效类别——组合策略违例(CPV)，指出其无法被任何步骤级监控器精度的改进所检测；2）提出包含Authority Creep、Threshold Laundering、Cumulative Sum Violation、Context Collapse四类的CPV分类学，并将各类的正确修复与"被守护量变更位置"建立对应关系；3）设计了一个血缘感知的运行时架构，在完整执行轨迹上评估策略，并从原始血缘重算被守护量，从而解决步骤级治理无法覆盖整体属性的根本矛盾。

Conclusion: 结论表明，对智能体工作流的治理不能停留在步骤级监控的精度提升上——这是一个结构性而非准确性的问题。治理必须转向"执行轨迹级策略评估"：策略应作为整次执行的组合属性来审查，被守护量应从原始血缘(provenance)重算，而非依赖流水线内部的派生表示。这种血缘感知的运行时架构是弥合组织真实策略与执行级治理之间差距的正确方向，其分类学为不同CPV类型提供了针对性的修复指引。

Abstract: Agentic workflows now make consequential decisions in regulated settings, and the governance placed around them is almost entirely step-scoped: input-output classifiers, per turn rails, and span-level evaluators. The policies organizations actually hold, such as referral thresholds, authority limits, and review requirements, are properties of the whole execution rather than of any one step. This mismatch admits a failure mode we call a Compositional Policy Violation (CPV): every individual step passes its own check while the composed execution violates the governing policy. A predicate over a single step cannot evaluate a property that step does not determine, so no improvement in the accuracy of the step-scoped monitors detects this class. We define CPVs as the failure of step-level compliance to compose, and present a taxonomy of four types: Authority Creep, Threshold Laundering, Cumulative Sum Violation, and Context Collapse. We show that the correct repair for each class is dictated by where the guarded quantity mutates. We then introduce a provenance-aware runtime architecture that evaluates policies over complete execution traces, recomputing guarded quantities from raw provenance rather than the pipeline's derived representation.

</details>


### [262] [Infinite-Parameter LLMs: Generating and Adapting Weights from Live Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18842)
*Jinli Hu,Ross M. Clarke,Yichuan Zhang,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"无限参数大语言模型"（Infinite-Parameter LLM），一种受Mixture-of-Experts启发的架构：一个紧凑的hypernetwork将运行时提供的数据转化为对共享基础网络的低秩调制，从而由实时数据生成前馈权重而非存储在固定参数库中。与传统权重生成器（只读一次上下文后就冻结）不同，该模型对生成器的潜码维持贝叶斯信念并在线更新，使有效权重随会话进程持续重推导。存储足迹保持固定，但可编译的权重是无限多的。该方案在运行时知识/行为上相比in-context学习更节省计算、释放上下文窗口、跨轮次持久且泛化更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型的扩展律建立在静态预训练数据上，但部署后的模型面对的是实时交互数据（用户提供的事实、纠正），这些数据不在训练集中。传统模型权重冻结无法学习此类数据，只能将其放入prompt中每次重读并在请求结束后丢弃。本文旨在回答如何通过将实时交互写入权重来学习，克服in-context方式的计算浪费、上下文窗口占用和无法持久化的局限。

Method: 受MoE启发，方法采用紧凑hypernetwork将运行时数据转化为共享基础网络的低秩调制。关键创新在于：传统权重生成器只读一次上下文即冻结，而本模型对生成器潜码维持贝叶斯信念（Bayesian belief）并在线更新（online update），使有效权重由演化的信念持续重推导而非一次读数后固定。这样存储足迹不变，但可编译的权重在效果上是无限的。

Result: 将运行时供应的知识与行为承载于权重而非prompt中，可获得多重收益：计算上被摊销（amortized in compute）、释放上下文窗口、跨轮次持久保留，且相比in-context使用能更好泛化。论文还制定了专门针对该机制的评测协议，用以对照in-context学习和检索进行检验。

Conclusion: 本文提出一种从实时交互中学习的新架构，融合MoE思想与贝叶斯在线推断，实现了固定存储下的无限有效权重空间。该方案针对部署场景中in-context学习和检索的固有局限，提供了一种可持久、省计算、释放上下文且泛化更强的替代路径，并通过专门评测协议加以验证。

Abstract: The scaling laws hold that a language model grows more capable with more parameters and more training data, and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have ridden these laws to remarkable results, activating only a fraction of an enormous stored parameter bank for each token. That success is built on static pretraining data. A deployed model faces a different world, where much of the data that would make it more useful is not in its training set but in the live interaction it is currently handling, such as the facts a user supplies or the corrections they give. A conventional model cannot learn from this data, because its weights are frozen after training. Instead, the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time are placed in the prompt, by retrieval or instruction, and re-read on every request only to be discarded once the request ends. We ask how an architecture could learn from live interaction by writing it into its weights. Taking inspiration from MoE, we propose the \textbf{Infinite-Parameter LLM}. A compact hypernetwork turns the data given at run time into a low-rank modulation of a shared base network, so the feed-forward weights are generated from live data rather than stored in a fixed bank. Where prior weight generators read the context once and freeze, we carry a Bayesian belief over the generator's latent code and update it online, so the effective weight is re-derived from that evolving belief as the session proceeds rather than fixed after one read. The stored footprint stays fixed, yet the weights the model can compile are effectively infinite. For the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time, carrying them in the weights rather than the prompt is amortized in compute, frees the context window, persists across turns, and can generalise better than in-context use. We specify an evaluation protocol that tests exactly this against in-context learning and retrieval.

</details>


### [263] [Suppressed, Not Erased: A Representational Trace of Edited Facts Survives Even Weight-Free Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18985)
*Priyansh Srivastava,Romit Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过线性探针直接检测知识编辑后原始事实是否仍可从隐藏状态解码；发现即使编辑行为成功，原始对象仍能以高于随机的概率被线性解码（GPT-2-XL上ROME 0.96、约束微调0.86、GRACE 0.79，随机水平0.50），表明编辑抑制而非擦除了原关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑基准仅验证“编辑后局部正确性”（即新事实在邻近提示上是否生成正确），但未检查模型内部是否仍保留原始事实的可解码信息。作者希望直接测量编辑后残留知识，以区分“覆盖/擦除”与“抑制/遮蔽”两种编辑机制。

Method: 使用线性迹探针（linear trace probe）从模型隐藏状态中解码原始对象；在GPT-2-XL上对50个CounterFact编辑应用三种机制不同的编辑器（ROME、约束微调、基于记忆的GRACE），并评估解码准确率。同时尝试了一种“再学习节省”指标作为辅助工具。

Result: 所有编辑在生成层面均100%成功，但原始对象的线性可解码性仍远高于随机水平：ROME 0.96、约束微调0.86、GRACE 0.79，随机基线0.50。GRACE不修改任何基座权重，仅通过外部记忆覆盖事实，因此残留迹不能归因于权重更新不充分。再学习节省指标不稳定，被报告为阴性方法学结果而非证据。

Conclusion: 知识编辑即使在行为上成功，也只是在表征空间中抑制原始关联，而非真正擦除；残留痕迹在模型内部仍可被线性机制恢复。作者将再学习节省视为不可靠方法，并公开代码与数据以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Knowledge-editing benchmarks certify local correctness, whether an edited model produces the new fact on near-edit prompts but not how much of the original fact remains decodable inside the model. We study residual knowledge directly with a linear trace probe: after editing a fact, we ask whether the original object is still recoverable from the model's hidden states. On GPT-2-XL, across three mechanistically distinct editors applied to 50 CounterFact edits, the original object remains linearly decodable well above chance after a successful edit (probe accuracy 0.96 for ROME, 0.86 for constrained fine-tuning, and 0.79 for the memory-based editor GRACE, against a chance level of 0.50; all edits reach 100% generation-based success). The GRACE result is the most informative: GRACE changes zero base-model weights, overriding the fact through an external memory, yet the original object is still decodable from the underlying network, so the residual trace cannot be attributed to an incomplete weight update. We read this as evidence that editing, even when behaviorally successful, suppresses rather than erases the original association in representational space. We also report a relearning-savings instrument that did not behave reliably in our setting and discuss why; we treat it as a negative methodological result rather than evidence. Code and data are released.

</details>


### [264] [Function Lives Where Variance Doesn't: Task-Weighted Charts of a Language Model's Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18989)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出任务加权图表（task-weighted charts）框架，将“语言模型计算实际使用多少维度”这一问题置于具体功能（functional）下给出良定义：在功能自身的度量下，用低维坐标系对表征做最小二乘拟合，从而把蒸馏转化为普通最小二乘问题。实验覆盖三个模型族、7000万到70亿参数的六个模型，发现：在保持困惑度偏差5%以内的条件下，下一个词预测需要残差流宽度的70%-90%，这部分宽度被语言的稀有尾部消耗；方差分布无法预测该需求，例如GPT-2的激活方差90%集中在两个方向上但对功能几乎没有贡献。维度是依功能而定的，模型自身的不确定性可由6个坐标读出，而完整预测分布需要数百维；所需维度随深度增加。这种分离可利用：当只能保留少数维度时，在功能度量下训练的图表比基于方差或最优线性压缩的方法更能保持模型的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见问题“语言模型的计算到底用了多少维度”本身是病态的，因为除非先指明一个功能（functional），否则维度数量没有意义。作者希望通过提出任务加权图表，将维度度量与具体功能绑定，从而系统性地回答在不同功能下模型真正需要的表征维度，并揭示维度数与方差等常规指标之间的分离现象。

Method: 提出任务加权图表（task-weighted charts）这一方法：给定一个选定的功能，在这个功能自身的度量（如交叉熵等）下，对表征拟合低维坐标系，使蒸馏变成标准的最小二乘问题。以此对六个模型（覆盖70M至7B参数、三个模型族）进行系统测量，比较不同功能（next-token预测、模型自身不确定性、完整预测分布）所需的维度，并与激活方差分布、基于方差或用功能度量训练的压缩方法相对照。

Result: 跨六个模型，想要困惑度偏差保持在5%以内，下一词预测需要残差流宽度的70%-90%，该宽度主要被语言的长尾（稀有词）占用；激活方差分布无法预测这一需求（GPT-2的90%激活方差集中在两个方向，但几乎没有功能意义）。维度是依功能而定的：模型自身的不确定性仅需6个坐标，而完整预测分布需要数百维；所需维度随深度增加。当只能保留少数维度时，按功能度量训练的图表比基于方差或最优线性压缩的方法更能保留模型预测。

Conclusion: 语言模型的“实际维度”没有绝对答案，只有相对具体功能而言的答案。任务加权图表提供了一种良定义、可操作且可优化的维度测量与压缩方式，并证实方差和功能之间的系统性分离，说明高效压缩必须与任务功能对齐。这为理解大语言模型的内部结构、可解释性以及有损蒸馏/压缩提供了新视角与实用工具。

Abstract: How many dimensions does a language model's computation actually use? The question is ill-posed until one names a functional. Task-weighted charts make it well-posed: low-dimensional coordinate systems fit against a chosen functional of the representation, under the functional's own metric, turning distillation into plain least squares. Across six models from three families, spanning 70m to 7B parameters, next-token prediction needs 70--90% of the residual stream's width to stay within 5% of intact perplexity, a width consumed by the rare tail of language, and the variance profile predicts none of it: two directions carry 90% of GPT-2's activation variance and almost none of its function. Dimension is per-functional: the model's own uncertainty reads from six coordinates where the full predictive distribution needs hundreds; and it grows with depth. The dissociation is exploitable: when only a few dimensions can be kept, charts trained under the functional's metric preserve the model's predictions better than variance-based or optimal linear compression.

</details>


### [265] [Lost in Perception: Isolating Perceptual and Reasoning Failures in Multimodal Physics and Geometry Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18991)
*Raj Jaiswal,Sree Krishna Uppalapati,Dhruvkumar Patel,Ria Khatoniar,Tanuja Ganu,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出多模态大语言模型（LLMs）在科学推理基准上表现强势，但多数研究将感知与推理视为单一可测过程。作者设计了跨物理和几何基准的五任务诊断实验，旨在区分失败源于感知、推理或两者兼有。研究发现：错误的图表解读会降低模型即使在纯文本下能正确解题的表现；准确率通常从原始图像到人工撰写图文说明逐步提升；部分模型在获得正确图文说明后表现恢复较高，从而区分出感知受阻型失败与真正推理瓶颈型失败。感知失败后引发的推理错误类型因领域而异：物理领域表现为计算错误，几何领域表现为概念误用。此外，作者提到InternS1-mini尽管经过大量科学预训练并具备思考能力，但在每个任务上均低于实验中最弱模型，且其推理轨迹常在中途截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学推理基准将感知与推理混为一体，无法定位模型在科学题目上的失败究竟是源于视觉感知（图表解读）的缺失还是深层推理能力的不足，阻碍了针对性改进。

Method: 构建包含五个任务的诊断实验，涵盖物理与几何基准，通过对照原始图像与人工撰写的图文说明两种输入条件，隔离感知失败与推理失败；并进一步分析在纠正图文说明后模型的恢复程度，以及失败后所呈现的具体推理错误类型。

Result: 1) 错误的图表解读会损害模型从纯文本即可求解的问题表现；2) 从原始图像到人工图文说明，准确率普遍提升；3) 部分模型在正确图文说明下恢复度高，将感知受阻型失败从真正推理瓶颈中分离出来；4) 感知失败后的事后推理错误类型有领域差异：物理为计算错误，几何为概念误用；5) InternS1-mini尽管有科学预训练与思考能力，却在所有任务上低于实验最弱模型，且推理轨迹频繁中途截断。

Conclusion: 科学推理评估应解耦视觉感知与推理过程；模型能力差异可归结为感知阻断型失败与推理瓶颈型失败两类，其后续错误模式随领域而变化；同时提示大规模科学预训练与显式思考机制并不能保证推理可靠性与完整输出。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs report strong performance on scientific reasoning benchmarks, yet most treat perception and reasoning as a single measurable process. We introduce a five-task diagnostic experiment across physics and geometry benchmarks that isolates failures to perception, reasoning, or both. Incorrect diagram interpretation degrades performance even on problems models solve correctly from text alone, and accuracy generally rises from raw images to human-authored captions. Recovery under corrected captions is high for some models, separating perception-blocked failures from genuine reasoning bottlenecks. Which reasoning error follows a perception failure depends on domain: physics failures resolve into calculation errors, geometry into conceptual misapplication. As a discussion beyond our core experiments, InternS1-mini, despite heavy scientific pretraining and thinking capabilities, falls below the weakest model from experiments on every task, with reasoning traces frequently truncating before completion.

</details>


### [266] [Compiled Agency: Frontier General-Purpose Coding Agents Build Winning Game Players from Bare Interaction - from Flappy Bird to StarCraft II and Civilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18996)
*Joey Xiao,Haonan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Gauntlet 框架，用于测试前沿编码智能体能否仅凭原始游戏描述、观测/动作接口和空策略文件，在单次自主会话中独立构建完整的游戏控制器。实验表明最新一代系统能赢得星海争霸II和文明（Freeciv）等商业级完整游戏，无需逐回合调用模型，标志着语言智能体首次具备独立完成长时域策略游戏的能力，作者称之为"编译代理"（compiled agency）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM智能体在游戏应用上取得进展，但它们长期难以将游戏知识转化为实际游戏能力，即便研究者围绕模型构建感知、记忆、技能库、规划器等辅助组件。编码智能体的快速进步提出了两个更尖锐的问题：前沿模型是否能赢得游戏？是否能不借助任何辅助、完全独立地构建整个玩家程序？这些问题的回答对评估模型自主性至关重要。

Method: 引入 Gauntlet 框架，采用"开发-冻结-评估"（develop-freeze-evaluate）三段式流程：将从小型街机到完整商业规模的各种游戏统一转换到极简抽象接口（仅有游戏描述、原始观测/动作接口和空策略文件，无策略、无算法、无架构提示）。一般用途编码智能体在单一自主会话中与实时游戏交互实验，工程化出独立控制器；然后将控制器冻结并在隐藏的测试实例上计分，游戏过程中零模型调用。测试涵盖未公开的程序生成 roguelike、星海争霸II 及文明（Freeciv）完整游戏。

Result: 在未公开的程序生成 roguelike 上，保持验证成功率在0-86%之间，并呈现出鲜明的代际阈值：最新一代系统每次观测会话都优于前代的最佳会话。在全游戏规模下，编译出的原始API控制器击败了星海争霸II所有公平的内置AI及两个作弊变体；单次会话程序还能在保留的种子上通过全面征服赢得完整的文明（Freeciv）游戏。虽对新手AI胜率不高，但这是语言智能体首次无需逐回合模型调用和手工战术层即可独立赢得此类完整游戏。

Conclusion: 前沿编码智能体开始掌握长时域策略能力。冻结后的程序可被检查。作者将这一能力命名为"编译代理"（compiled agency）：即把开发经验编译进一个持久可执行智能体中，其架构完全由模型自行构建。这标志着从"辅助型智能体"向"自主构建型智能体"的范式转变。

Abstract: LLM agents have repeatedly struggled to convert knowledge of a game into competent play, even when researchers build the agent around the model - supplying perception, memory, skill libraries, planners, or executable-policy scaffolds. Rapid progress in coding agents raises two sharper questions: can frontier models now win games at all, and can they win them unaided, building the entire player themselves? We introduce Gauntlet, a develop-freeze-evaluate framework that ports games from small arcades to full commercial-scale titles, behind one deliberately bare contract: a general-purpose coding agent receives a game description, a raw observation/action interface, and an empty policy file - no strategy, no algorithm, no architecture. In a single autonomous session the agent experiments with the live game and engineers a standalone controller; we freeze the result and score it on held-out instances with zero model calls during play. On an unpublished procedural roguelike, held-out success spans 0-86 percent and exposes a sharp generational threshold: every observed session of a newest-generation system outperforms the best session of its predecessor. At full-game scale, a compiled raw-API controller defeats every fair StarCraft II built-in AI and two cheating variants, and single-session programs win complete games of Civilization (Freeciv) by total conquest on held-out seeds. Though at modest rates against novice AI, this is a first: no prior language-agent system had won full games of this genre standalone, without per-turn model calls and a hand-crafted tactical layer. Frontier coding agents begin to track long-horizon strategy. The frozen programs are inspectable. We call this capability compiled agency: development experience compiled into a persistent executable agent whose architecture is built by the model.

</details>


### [267] [MUSE: Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on Multi-Modal Understanding in Situated Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19088)
*Luyao Zhu,Xun Wei Yee,Wei Li,Mun Thye Mak,Wee Siong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MUSE基准，用于评估大型视觉语言模型在情境化教育应用中对艺术图像的理解能力。该基准将图像标注与问题生成解耦，包含十二个任务，涵盖视觉感知、语义与情感诠释、文化理解及组合推理，并特意选取以新加坡和东南亚多元文化语境为主的多种艺术图像。评估开源与专有模型发现，各能力维度存在显著差距，尤其在情感诠释和组合推理方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在多模态理解上取得显著进展，但其在教育场景中的能力尚未得到充分评估。现有基准主要聚焦真实世界图像或领域特定的教育推理，对艺术教育内容覆盖有限，无法全面评估模型在AI辅助语言学习中所需的艺术图像理解、语义/情感/文化内容解读及视觉情境推理能力。

Method: MUSE基准将图像标注与问题生成解耦，从而在降低标注成本的同时实现多样化任务与可控难度。其包含十二个任务，覆盖视觉感知、语义与情感诠释、文化理解及组合推理等能力维度；艺术图像经过精心挑选，以新加坡和东南亚多元文化语境为中心，同时涵盖西方艺术传统，并涉及多种主题与难度级别。通过评估开源及专有模型来测试该基准。

Result: 对开源与专有模型的评估显示，不同能力维度之间存在显著差距，尤其在情感诠释和组合推理方面表现薄弱。分析还识别出常见的失败模式，以及开发可信赖教育多模态模型所面临的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 作者希望MUSE能够作为情境化教育应用中多模态理解的标准基准，推动大型视觉语言模型在教育领域的发展，并为构建可信赖的教育多模态模型提供方向性指引。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable progress in multi-modal understanding, yet their capabilities in educational settings remain insufficiently evaluated. In AI-assisted language learning, models must interpret artistic imagery, understand its semantic, affective, and cultural content, and reason about visual context to support meaningful interaction. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on real-world images or domain-specific educational reasoning, providing limited coverage of artistic educational content. To address this gap, we introduce MUSE, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models on artistic image understanding in situated educational applications. MUSE decouples image annotation from question generation, enabling diverse tasks with controllable difficulty while reducing annotation effort. It comprises twelve tasks spanning visual perception, semantic and affective interpretation, culture understanding, and compositional reasoning, together with diverse artistic images deliberately curated to center Singaporean and Southeast Asian multicultural contexts alongside Western art traditions, covering multiple themes and difficulty levels. Evaluation of open-source and proprietary models reveals substantial disparities across capability dimensions, particularly in affective interpretation and compositional reasoning. Our analysis further identifies common failure modes and key challenges for developing trustworthy multi-modal models for education. We hope MUSE will serve as a standardized benchmark for advancing multi-modal understanding in situated educational applications.

</details>


### [268] [Flag Game: A Toy Model for Mechanistic Swarm Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19124)
*Elizabeth Pavlova,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出旗帜博弈（Flag Game）这一玩具模型来研究AI智能体集体信念形成的机制。该模型通过隐藏的国家旗帜定义地面真相，每个受限智能体仅观察私有裁剪图像但可交换信念、权衡同伴的社会证据。尽管简单，它重现了丰富的集体现象：性能随群体规模呈非单调缩放、社交感知提示与团队多样性带来准确性提升、组织结构产生强烈影响。研究发现集体信念崩溃在小群体中出现，随群体增大转化为集体信念极化，极化导致大群体性能下降但创造了信念多样性。作者用社会电路归因（预测哪个智能体、哪个观点对集体动力学最重要）和统计力学理论（用于大群体）两种互补方法剖析崩溃与极化机制，并验证了经验相图。这项工作朝着机制性群体可解释性（swarm interpretability）迈出第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体的涌现协调行为开始带来严重的安全风险，而驱动这些行为的关键现象是信念的快速形成与传播。为了集体对齐（collective alignment），需要对信念形成的机制有深入理解，但目前缺乏合适的工具模型来研究这一机制。

Method: 构建旗帜博弈（Flag Game）玩具模型：隐藏旗帜定义地面真相，每个受限智能体仅观察私有裁剪，但可交换信念并权衡来自同伴的社会证据。引入社会电路归因（social circuit attribution）技术预测对集体动力学最重要的智能体与观点，并通过因果干预（agent patching）验证预测；同时开发统计力学理论用于更大群体，与经验相图匹配。

Result: 模型重现了丰富的集体现象：性能随群体规模非单调缩放、社交感知提示和团队多样性带来准确性增益、组织结构有强烈影响。识别出集体信念崩溃在小群体中出现、随群体增大转化为极化；极化导致大群体性能下降但创造信念多样性。社会电路归因的预测可通过因果干预验证，但其效能随群体增大而降低，因此发展统计力学理论验证了经验相图。

Conclusion: 该工作朝向量子机制性群体可解释性（mechanistic swarm interpretability）——即研究个体智能体属性及其通信如何产生涌现集体行为——迈出了第一步，为集体信念形成的机制理解提供了理论框架和实证方法。

Abstract: Emergent coordinated behaviors of AI agents are starting to present critical safety risks. A key phenomenon driving these behaviors is the rapid formation and spread of beliefs about the world, and mechanistic understanding is crucial for collective alignment. To this end, we introduce the Flag Game, a toy model for studying the mechanisms of collective belief formation. Concretely, a hidden country flag defines the ground truth, and each bounded agent directly observes only a private crop but can exchange beliefs and weigh social evidence from peers. Despite its simplicity, the Flag Game reproduces rich collective phenomenology: non-monotonic scaling of performance with population size, accuracy gains from social-awareness prompting and team diversity, and strong effects of organizational structure. In particular, we identify that collective belief collapse at small population sizes turns into collective belief polarization as the population grows. This polarization causes the performance decline at large population sizes, but creates diversity in collective beliefs. Finally, we dissect the mechanisms underlying collective belief collapse and polarization with two complementary approaches. We first introduce social circuit attribution, a technique to predict which agent, and what view, matters most to collective dynamics, and verify its predictions by causal interventions on agents, tracing how agent patching changes collective outcomes. However, the efficacy of causal interventions on agents decreases as the population grows. We therefore develop a statistical mechanical theory for larger populations and verify that it matches the empirical phase diagram. Together, these results take a first step toward mechanistic swarm interpretability, a science of how the properties of individual agents and their communication give rise to emergent collective behavior.

</details>


### [269] [Cognitive Extensions for Dual-Process Language Agents: Memory and Self-Reflection in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19128)
*João Meneses dos Santos,Arlindo L. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于SwiftSage双过程智能体框架，提出了两个模块化认知扩展：用于显著性门控情景存储和触发驱动检索的自适应记忆模块（AMM），以及用于有界执行时验证与纠正干预的自我反思模块（SRM）。在ScienceWorld环境上的对照消融实验表明，完整系统取得最佳平均最终得分（64.62）、成功率（43.17%）和成功步效率（19.33步），其中SRM是贡献最大的独立模块；执行时控制是此场景中的主导瓶颈，而情景记忆主要在运行时循环稳定后发挥最大作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在交互式环境中仍然脆弱，其成功需要长视界状态追踪、合法动作执行以及从失败步骤中恢复的能力。现有方法在这类动态环境下缺乏对情景记忆的持久化利用和执行过程中的动态纠错，因此需要模块化的认知扩展来提升鲁棒性。

Method: 在SwiftSage双过程框架（快速动作提议者+慢速规划器）之上，以功能标志（feature-flagged）方式实现两个模块化扩展：(1) Adaptive Memory Module (AMM)，采用显著性门控的情景存储与触发驱动检索；(2) Self-Reflection Module (SRM)，提供有界执行时验证和纠正性干预。两者共享同一执行底座，便于在ScienceWorld上进行受控消融。

Result: 在四种配置（基线、基线+AMM、基线+SRM、完整系统）中，完整系统表现最佳：平均最终得分64.62，成功率43.17%，成功步效率19.33步；SRM是独立贡献最强的模块。

Conclusion: 执行时控制（SRM）是该场景下的主导瓶颈，是提升智能体性能的关键；情景记忆（AMM）的效用只有在运行时循环稳定后才充分显现，两者协同使用可取得最佳效果。

Abstract: Language agents remain brittle in interactive environments, where success requires long-horizon state tracking, valid action execution, and recovery from failed steps. We extend SwiftSage, a dual-process agent that combines a fast action proposer with a slower planner, using two modular cognitive extensions: an Adaptive Memory Module (AMM) for salience-gated episodic storage and trigger-driven retrieval, and a Self-Reflection Module (SRM) for bounded execution-time validation and corrective intervention. Both modules are implemented as feature-flagged extensions over the same execution substrate, enabling controlled ablations on ScienceWorld. Across four configurations---baseline, baseline+AMM, baseline+SRM, and the full system---the full system achieves the best mean final score (64.62), success rate (43.17%), and successful-step efficiency (19.33 steps), while SRM is the strongest standalone contributor. The results suggest that execution-time control is the dominant bottleneck in this setting, while episodic memory becomes most useful once the runtime loop is stabilized.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [270] [Pay Only for Disagreement: Certified No-Regression Verdicts for Model Updates with Matching Label-Complexity Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17560)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DISCERN协议，用于对模型更新进行认证式配对风险差审计，通过无标签分歧率实现零标签良性更新认证，并以任意有效置信序列在有标签的分歧样本上进行审计，提供有限样本有效性保证和标签复杂度上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产模型每次更新（重训、微调、量化或供应商更换）都可能比原模型更差，因此需要严格审计两个模型之间的风险差异。现有方法通常需要标注大量样本，成本高且无法实时监测。本文旨在利用模型间分歧样本无需标签即可观测的特性，降低审计成本并保证统计有效性。

Method: DISCERN采用顺序两层协议：第一层为零标签层，仅利用未标注流量估计分歧率，若低于容差则直接认证为良性更新；第二层为受审计层，仅在分歧样本上标注，并使用任意有效置信序列（anytime-valid confidence sequence）构造风险差置信区间，该序列在任何停止时间和任何标签路由规则下均有效，包括对抗性法官。还证明了有限样本有效性和标签复杂度界，并支持从单一误差预算中组合多个连续更新。

Result: 在超过14000条重放审计流、785对更新（含高达1.4B参数的LoRA微调语言模型）上，覆盖误差为0.0002（名义5%），功效0.986，零误报，56%的良性更新零标签认证成功。每个审计生成可机器核验的证据记录。

Conclusion: DISCERN利用免费的无标签分歧信息，在标签复杂度上比配对盲审计者节省1/rho的因子，同时保证严格的统计有效性，适合大规模模型更新的持续事后监测。

Abstract: Every production model is updated, by retraining, fine-tuning, quantization, or a silent vendor swap, and each update risks being worse than what it replaced. We formalize update promotion as certified paired risk-difference auditing. Our starting point is a support identity: the risk difference between two models lives on the inputs where they disagree, observable without labels. We build DISCERN, a sequential two-tier protocol. A zero-label tier certifies benign updates whose disagreement rate is below tolerance from unlabeled traffic alone. An audited tier labels only sampled disagreements through an anytime-valid confidence sequence, valid at every stopping time and under any label-routing rule, even an adversarial judge. We prove finite-sample validity and matching label-complexity bounds of order rho^2/eps^2 at the rate level, so exploiting free disagreement provably saves a factor 1/rho over any pairing-blind auditor, and the guarantee composes across an unbounded sequence of promotions from one error budget. Across 14,000+ replayed audit streams over 785 update pairs, including LoRA fine-tunes of language models up to 1.4B parameters, miscoverage is 0.0002 (nominal 5%), power 0.986 with zero false alarms, and 56% of benign updates certify with zero labels. Each audit emits a machine-checkable evidence record for post-market monitoring.

</details>


### [271] [Beyond Static RAG: An Adaptive, Tri-Metric Routing Framework for Efficient Long-Context Inference on Commodity GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17564)
*Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Ajay Kumar Boddepalli,Deep Narayan Mishra,Shashank Kapadia,Haoan Wang,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了一个名为"压缩悖论"的实践性失效模式：在T4这类商用GPU上部署RAG时，神经网络提示压缩引入的KV缓存争用和预处理延迟可能超过其生成阶段的收益，而跳过压缩又会在长上下文场景中引发OOM崩溃。作者提出Tri-Metric Router，一种确定性、免训练的调度策略，在Raw、神经压缩（LLMLingua-2）和词法压缩（BM25）三条流水线间选择，依据空间复杂度、句法密度和类型-标记比三个CPU侧信号，并结合VRAM余量和延迟交叉点进行硬件物理级调度。在LongBench qasper上校准阈值得到T4上约4332词的交叉点，在分布外数据上实现0% OOM失败、88.5±4.4%的oracle对齐率和49.3%的Combined F1，较恒定词法压缩提升5.2点且无额外VRAM或训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在商用GPU（如T4 16GB）上部署RAG时发现一个实践盲区：神经提示压缩在紧凑显存预算下与vLLM服务的LLM共存时，其KV缓存开销和预处理延迟可能抵消生成时间的节省（即"压缩悖论"）；而完全跳过压缩则会在长上下文中触发OOM。现有研究大多聚焦语义层面的压缩策略，忽略了硬件物理层面的约束，作者希望填补这一空白，提出一种考虑显存余量和延迟交叉点的确定性调度机制。

Method: 作者识别了vLLM型LLM与PyTorch压缩器在紧凑显存下协同部署的两种失效机制，并提出Tri-Metric Router——一种确定性、免训练的策略。它使用三个CPU侧信号（空间复杂度L、句法密度ρ_key、类型-标记比TTR）在Raw、Neural（LLMLingua-2）和Lexical（BM25）三条流水线中决策。与以往仅依赖语义的适配不同，其调度信号是硬件物理性的，基于VRAM余量和延迟交叉点；阈值通过对LongBench qasper的profiling校准，在T4上得到约4332词的运行交叉点。核心贡献在于校准方法本身，而非具体的硬件常数。

Result: 在分布外（holdout）数据上，该方法实现0% OOM失败、88.5±4.4%的oracle对齐率以及49.3%的Combined F1，较恒定开启的lexical压缩提升了5.2个点，且未增加额外VRAM或训练成本。

Conclusion: 通过将压缩调度的依据从纯语义转向硬件物理信号（显存余量与延迟交叉点），并采用可校准/可迁移的阈值方法而非硬件专属常数，能够在无需额外显存和训练开销的前提下，同时消除OOM失效并提升检索增强生成的压缩质量，为商用GPU上的RAG部署提供了实用且鲁棒的路线。

Abstract: Deploying retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) on commodity GPUs such as the NVIDIA T4 (16 GB VRAM) exposes a practical failure mode we call the Compression Paradox: neural prompt compression can add key-value (KV) cache contention and preprocessing latency that outweigh generation-time savings, while skipping compression can cause out-of-memory (OOM) failures on long contexts. We identify two distinct failure mechanisms when a vLLM-served LLM and a PyTorch-based compressor are co-deployed under tight memory budgets, and introduce the Tri-Metric Router, a deterministic, training-free policy that selects among Raw, Neural (LLMLingua-2), and Lexical (BM25) pipelines. The router uses three CPU-side signals: spatial complexity ($L$), syntactic density ($ρ_{key}$), and type-token ratio (TTR). Unlike prior semantic-only adaptation, our dispatch signal is hardware-physical, based on VRAM headroom and a latency crossover point. Thresholds are calibrated from profiling on LongBench qasper, yielding an operating crossover near 4,332 words on T4; our contribution is this calibration methodology rather than a hardware-specific constant. On out-of-distribution holdouts, the method achieves 0% OOM failures, 88.5 $\pm$ 4.4% oracle alignment, and 49.3% Combined F1, improving over always-on lexical compression by 5.2 points without additional VRAM or training cost.

</details>


### [272] [Where Grokking Happens: Distributed Utility and Fourier Recoding Without a Module Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17571)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过引入"转移博弈"（行为对齐的精确激活博弈配以成对的非泛化对照）来定位Transformer中记忆到泛化转变的功能性表达位置。结果显示：分布式效用增益带有前瞻性的block-0注意力偏置；选定的二阶模式在替换博弈中解释了加法对比的67-92%；不相交精确路径研究表明block-1 MLP在12/12对中比所有其他下游路径介导更多效应。"MLP记忆、注意力泛化"的预测被反转（在记忆锚点处为-.331 bits/example；预测方向上为0/12）；路由启动、全局秩坍缩及素数不变架构脊也均失败。最终将grokking识别为现有分布式电路的光谱重编码，而非模块切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在回答一个核心机制问题：Transformer中从记忆（memorization）到泛化（generalization）的转变究竟在哪里被功能性表达。作者试图区分各种假说（如"MLP记忆、注意力泛化"的模块分工论，以及路由、秩坍缩等机制），从而精确定位这一转变发生的神经结构位置。

Method: 作者提出"转移博弈"（Transition Games）方法：即与行为对齐的精确激活博弈（exact activation games），并配有成对的非泛化对照（paired non-generalizing controls）。通过该博弈框架测量分布式效用增益，并结合"不相交精确路径研究"（disjoint exact path study）来分解各下游路径（如block-1 MLP）对效应中介的贡献比例，同时检验了多个竞争性机制预测（路由启动、全局秩坍缩、素数不变架构脊以及"MLP记忆、注意力泛化"假说）。

Result: 结果发现：分布式效用增益带有一个前瞻性的block-0注意力偏置；选定的度二模式（degree-two modes）在替换博弈中解释了加法对比的67-92%。不相交精确路径研究确认在12/12对中，block-1 MLP对效应的中介作用超过所有被测试的其他下游路径。相反，"MLP记忆、注意力泛化"的预测被反转（记忆锚点处为-.331 bits/example；预测方向上0/12命中），且路由启动、全局秩坍缩、素数不变架构脊等机制均失败。结论将grokking认定为对既有分布式电路的光谱重编码，而非模块切换。

Conclusion: 作者否定了模块级分工假说（如"MLP记忆、注意力泛化"）以及路由、秩坍缩等结构切换机制，提出grokking的本质是"光谱重编码"（spectral recoding）——即对现存的分布式电路进行谱系层面的重编码，而非进行模块级的切换。这一结论强调了记忆到泛化转化是分布在整个电路上、由特定谱模式（degree-two modes）和block-1 MLP路径主导的渐进性重组织过程。

Abstract: Where in a Transformer is the change from memorization to generalization functionally expressed? We introduce Transition Games--behavior-aligned exact activation games with paired non-generalizing controls--and find distributed utility gain with a prospective block-0 attention bias; selected degree-two modes account for 67--92% of its addition contrast across replacement games, and a disjoint exact path study confirms that block-1 MLP mediates more of their effect than all other tested downstream paths in 12/12 pairs. The sharper "MLP memorizes, attention generalizes" prediction instead reverses (-.331 bits/example at the memory anchor; 0/12 in the predicted direction), while routing onset, global rank collapse, and a prime-invariant architecture ridge also fail, identifying grokking here as spectral recoding of an existing distributed circuit rather than a module switch.

</details>


### [273] [Disentangling Algorithmic Bias from Archival Artifacts: A Controlled Audit of Vision-Language Model Valuation in Metropolitan Museum Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17572)
*Manpreet Singh,Rhythm Bhatia,Rahul Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究审计了CLIP视觉语言模型在艺术品图像中的性别偏见，利用大都会艺术博物馆开放数据集（1,500件物品），通过零样本logit差分、等价性检验（TOST）和多元回归控制混杂因素。结果显示未发现统计显著的性别主效应，模型评分接近嵌入噪声下限，但模型对微观视觉特征的敏感性不足，且41.2%未署名作品造成生存偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计视觉语言模型（VLM）的社会偏见需要区分直接的算法价值差异与档案元数据中嵌入的混杂因素（confounders）。现有评估常将两者混淆，导致误判模型公平性。本研究旨在建立考虑混杂因素控制的定量审计框架，以准确评估CLIP模型在文化遗产场景中的性别偏见。

Method: 使用大都会艺术博物馆Open Access数据（1,500件物品，其中743件有署名：男性534、女性209，618件匿名），对CLIP模型（OpenAI CLIP与OpenCLIP）进行零样本审计。方法包括：(1) 三个语义提示对（masterpiece、quality、influence）的logit差分评分；(2) 基于Cohen's d >= 0.25界限的双单侧检验（TOST）确认统计等价性；(3) 控制媒介、创作时代、宽高比的多元OLS回归（R^2 < 0.02）。

Result: 未调整评估显示OpenAI CLIP（mu_F=-0.0067 vs mu_M=-0.0035, p=0.1829）与OpenCLIP（mu_F=0.0171 vs mu_M=0.0237, p=0.1224）均无统计显著的性别主效应；TOST确认等价（pTOST<0.005）。多元回归证实性别无显著条件效应（p>0.20），且高残差嵌入方差（R^2<2%）表明零样本评分接近嵌入噪声下限。

Conclusion: 宏观层面评分等价性反映的是度量工具对细粒度视觉语义特征不敏感，而非绝对模型公平性；仍需警惕微观层面的局部视觉偏见。剔除41.2%未署名藏品反映机构性生存偏差。研究强调评估AI公平性时必须进行多元混杂控制、等价性检验和档案来源审计。

Abstract: Auditing vision-language models (VLMs) for societal bias requires distinguishing direct algorithmic valuation disparities from confounders embedded within archival metadata. In this study, we audit Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models using historical artwork metadata from the Metropolitan Museum of Art Open Access collection (N = 1,500 total objects; N = 743 attributed works: Male n = 534, Female n = 209; n = 618 anonymous).
  We establish a quantitative audit framework evaluating zero-shot CLIP logit differential scores across three semantic prompt pairs (masterpiece, quality, and influence). Unadjusted evaluations demonstrate high score convergence without a statistically significant main gender effect under OpenAI CLIP (mu_F = -0.0067 vs mu_M = -0.0035, p = 0.1829) or OpenCLIP (mu_F = 0.0171 vs mu_M = 0.0237, p = 0.1224). Two One-Sided Tests (TOST) confirm statistical equivalence across Cohen's d >= 0.25 bounds (pTOST < 0.005).
  Multivariate OLS regression controlling for artwork medium, creation era, and aspect ratio (R^2 < 0.02) confirms that artist gender has no statistically significant conditional effect (p > 0.20). High residual embedding variance (R^2 < 2%) indicates that global zero-shot valuation metrics operate near an embedding noise floor, showing that broad zero-shot prompt logit differentials are a coarse measurement instrument rather than proving absolute model fairness.
  We highlight two key caveats: (i) macro-level score equivalence reflects metric insensitivity to fine-grained visual-semantic features and does not preclude localized micro-level visual biases, and (ii) excluding 41.2% unattributed holdings reflects institutional survival bias. These results demonstrate the necessity of multivariate confound control, equivalence testing, and archival provenance auditing when assessing AI fairness in cultural heritage collections.

</details>


### [274] [Temperon: Full-Time SAM Quality at a Third Less Wall-Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17575)
*Stamatis Mastromichalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Temperon训练策略：前43%的训练预算用普通SGD"探索"，随后一次性切换到SAM包裹的Muon优化器进行最后的余弦退火"精炼"，从而以接近普通SGD的成本获得全时段SAM的精度，并在多个数据集和预训练/微调任务上更快达标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM在每个训练步上成本翻倍，但其收益集中在训练末期，因此"全程使用SAM"是一种昂贵且低效的资源配置。作者试图回答：昂贵的训练模式应当花在哪里？能否只把SAM放在最受益的末尾阶段，其余时间用便宜的方法？

Method: 提出Temperon：前43%的epoch预算使用普通SGD作为"探索器"，之后通过一次预定切换将整个最后的余弦退火阶段交给"SAM包裹的Muon优化器"作为"精炼器"。采用对照实验与消融逐项归因各组件的贡献（Muon精炼器、探索器形状、重启等），并在匹配预算条件下重跑最接近的对手late-phase SAM以刻画方法边界。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100、SVHN、Tiny ImageNet上（5个随机种子），Temperon在精度上匹配最佳的全时SAM配方，并在三个数据集上分别提前35%、34%、32%达到最难的公共目标；精度归因精确：Muon精炼器值+0.85pp，探索器形状和重启无贡献。在匹配预算下，late-phase SAM最快达到所有中位目标，但Muon精炼器带来的精度档（CIFAR-100上0.83、CIFAR-10上0.97）没有任何SGD精炼方法能达；在Muon不带来档位收益的Tiny ImageNet上，对手胜出，这构成方法边界。该分配规律迁移到GPT-2预训练（-29%墙钟时间、全时SAM质量）和GLUE微调（1/3的SAM成本，从不差于全时SAM）。两个常量统摄经济学：早期跳过SAM获得固定信用，Muon一epoch成本为SAM+SGD的1.50x。切换无法从轨迹上计时——余弦调度下精度曲线呈"平台然后骤升"，信息存在于调度本身，因此定时切换是原则性的而非便利性的。

Conclusion: Temperon验证了"昂贵训练模式应集中在训练末期"的假设：把预算理性分配给末尾的SAM-Muon精炼器，能在成本大幅下降的同时保持甚至更快达到全时SAM的精度。方法的有效性边界清晰（在Muon不提供额外精度档的任务上对手胜出），切换时机的可行性依据是调度信息而非训练轨迹，并以两个经济常数支撑其可迁移性。代码与pip可安装实现已发布。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) doubles the cost of every training step, yet its benefit concentrates where training ends. We study where an expensive training mode should be spent and propose Temperon: a plain-SGD explorer for the first 43% of the epoch budget, then one scheduled hand-off that gives the entire final cosine anneal to a SAM-wrapped Muon refiner. On CIFAR-10/100, SVHN and Tiny ImageNet (five seeds, times reported as epochs-to-target times an idle-GPU-calibrated epoch cost), Temperon matches the best full-time-SAM recipe on accuracy everywhere while reaching the hardest common target 35%, 34% and 32% sooner on three of the four, and sits a tier above the published SAM+SGD recipe at level cost. Ablations make the attribution exact: the Muon refiner is worth +0.85pp with everything else fixed; the explorer's shape and its restarts are worth nothing, and we withdraw them as contributions. Re-running the closest rival, late-phase SAM, at matched budget shows the frontier: it is fastest to every mid-level target, but the tier the Muon refiner buys (0.83 on CIFAR-100, 0.97 on CIFAR-10) is reached by no SGD-refined method in any seed, and on Tiny ImageNet, where Muon buys no tier, the rival simply wins -- the measured boundary of the method. The allocation law transfers to GPT-2 pretraining (full-SAM quality at -29% wall-clock) and GLUE fine-tuning (never worse than full-time SAM at a third of its SAM cost). Two constants organize the economics: skipping SAM early buys a fixed credit, and a Muon epoch costs 1.50x a SAM+SGD epoch on all four datasets. Finally, the hand-off cannot be timed from the trajectory: under cosine schedules the accuracy curve is plateau-then-surge, so the information lives in the schedule, making the scheduled switch principled rather than convenient. Code and a pip-installable implementation are released.

</details>


### [275] [When the Gradient Sees Rank: Provable Necessity, Causal Recruitment, and Composition in Trained Matrix Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17594)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过实验探究梯度训练能否学习矩阵记忆存储K个键值绑定所需的秩。结果显示学习到的有效秩随K增加（d=16时Spearman ρ=1.0）；训练时的秩上限在k=K附近产生恢复率跃迁（d=8,K=4时秩3恢复率≤0.0004，秩4为0.97）；四个种子在21次自应用中保持≥0.9996恢复率；在实体子空间上学习算子有效秩接近K并逼近理想循环；单个收敛且秩被封顶的种子，其测量余弦可通过计算预测（误差0.008以内）；延长训练可解决部分初始失败，但固定编码器宽度下恢复率随矩阵维度增大而下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究仅在允许秩1解的任务上使用矩阵增强推理器，未回答梯度训练能否学会存储和组合关联所需的秩这一开放问题。本文旨在探究矩阵记忆在训练过程中能否学习到足以线性恢复K个键值绑定所需的最小秩。

Method: 在K个全新键值绑定上训练矩阵记忆，其精确的线性恢复需要rank(Z)≥K。采用固定线性读出，仅查询单个矩阵状态而无法访问原始绑定。以余弦相似度>0.9为恢复阈值（区别于数学相等）。实验测量学习到的有效秩随K的变化（Spearman相关性），并施加训练时秩上限以观察恢复率跃迁，同时测试训练算子的多次自应用稳定性及实体子空间的逼近性质。

Result: 学习到的有效秩随K严格递增（d=16时Spearman ρ=1.0）。秩上限产生接近k=K的恢复跃迁：d=8,K=4时秩3最大恢复0.0004，秩4达0.97。五个种子中四个在21次自应用后仍保持≥0.9996恢复。实体子空间上学习算子有效秩接近K并逼近理想循环。单个被封顶种子经计算（用实体子空间算子与理想循环）可在七次应用内将余弦预测误差控制在0.008内。延长训练解决若干初始失败，但固定编码器宽度时恢复率随矩阵维度增大而下降。

Conclusion: 研究表明梯度训练确实能学习矩阵记忆所需的秩，有效秩随绑定数K增加而增长，且秩上限在k=K处引发清晰的行为跃迁，印证了秩是存储与恢复能力的决定性因素。学习算子逼近理想循环，其行为可被理论计算准确预测。但存在尺度限制：固定编码器宽度时，恢复性能随矩阵维度增大而衰减，说明该机制在更大规模下的泛化与鲁棒性仍有局限。

Abstract: Can gradient-based training learn the rank needed to store and compose associations in a matrix memory? In our earlier study, we used a matrix-augmented reasoner on a task that admits a rank-1 solution, leaving this question open. We train matrix memories on $K$ fresh key-value bindings whose exact linear recovery requires $\mathrm{rank}(Z) \geq K$. A fixed linear readout queries a single matrix state without access to the original bindings. Experiments measure recovery by cosine similarity greater than 0.9, a threshold distinct from mathematical equality. Learned effective rank increases with $K$ across the tested grid (Spearman $ρ= 1.0$ at $d = 16$). Training-time rank caps produce a recovery transition near $k = K$: at $d = 8$, $K = 4$, rank 3 gives at most 0.0004 recovery and rank 4 gives 0.97. Four of five seeds retain at least 0.9996 recovery through 21-fold self-application of the trained operator. On the entity subspace, the learned operator has effective rank close to $K$ and approximates the ideal cycle. For the single converged seed capped below $K$, a calculation using the entity-subspace operator and ideal cycle predicts the measured cosine within 0.008 through seven applications. Extending training resolves several initial failures, but recovery still declines at larger matrix dimensions with encoder width fixed.

</details>


### [276] [Prior-Free Competitive Ratios for Improving Bandits: Scale, Curvature and Horizon Are Free, but Not Jointly Under Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17595)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究“改进型多臂老虎机”问题：每根臂有一条未知的非降、离散凹收益曲线 f_i，第 t 次拉动臂 i 得到 f_i(t)。作者证明此前 Blum 与 Ravichandran 结果中的 O(√k log k) 对数因子可以去掉：一个简单的“探测然后承诺”（probe-and-commit）算法无需知道最优收益尺度 m，即可在任意足够长地平线上达到 O(√k) 逼近；并给出了所有地平线上的最优竞争比 Θ(√k + k/T)，同样适用于未知地平线。在无噪声情形，一个同时不知道尺度 m、凹包络指数 β 和地平线 T 的“随机边缘探测”算法，也能对每个 β 与每个 T 同时达到最优 Θ(k^{β/(1+β)} + k/T)。在乘法噪声模型下，探测-承诺算法在未知噪声水平时仍保持 Θ(√k + k/T) 的全地平线阶。然而“先验的价格”会跳升：若算法既不知 m 也不知 β，则对固定噪声 ε 而言，最坏情况下的适应损失 φ_ε(k)=Θ_ε(√(log k/log log k))（由嵌套随机置换探测算法匹配），而只要知道 m 或 β 之一就能恢复到常数价格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在改进型多臂老虎机中，核心诉求是不依赖问题参数而获得接近最优臂的收益。此前方法在未知收益尺度时只能达到 O(√k log k)，对数因子是否必要、能否同时应对未知地平线与凹包络指数，以及噪声会如何放大对先验信息的依赖，都是悬而未决的问题，本文旨在给出精确的刻画。

Method: 主要方法包括三类算法与下界分析。(1) 探测-承诺算法：先以小规模探测估计各臂潜力，再承诺一个臂，达到常数倍 √k 的竞争比而不需要知道尺度 m；(2) 随机边缘探测算法：不读取 m、β 或 T，利用随机化在边缘收益上进行探测，统一实现 Θ(k^{β/(1+β)} + k/T)；(3) 嵌套随机置换探测算法：针对噪声情形，构造适应未知噪声与未知参数的策略，并匹配所刻画的价格上界；同时借助信息论/对抗式论证给出相应的下界。

Result: 得到一系列精确结果：无尺度信息时探测-承诺算法对 T≥2⌊√k⌋ 达到 4√3·√k 竞争比，消除了此前多余的对数因子；对每个地平线 T，最优竞争比为 Θ(√k + k/T)，且在未知 T 时同样成立。无噪声下，同时不知 m、β、T 的随机边缘探测算法对所有 β 与所有 T 达到最优 Θ(k^{β/(1+β)} + k/T)。含乘法噪声时，探测-承诺算法仍给出 Θ(√k+k/T) 的全地平线阶；但在固定噪声 ε∈(0,1/2] 下，若算法既不知 m 也不知 β，其价格 φ_ε(k)=Θ_ε(√(log k/log log k))（固定 ε 时对 k 渐近），而知晓 m 或 β 之一则价格回到常数。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在无噪声的确定性设定下，对先验信息（尺度、凹包络指数、地平线）的需求几乎可以完全消除，达到与已知全部参数时相同阶的最优竞争比；噪声则显著改变这一图景——对固定噪声水平，同时缺乏尺度与指数这两个参数会引入 √(log k/log log k) 量级的价格，但只要知晓其中一个参数，价格就恢复为常数。这给出了改进型多臂老虎机中“参数可知性—竞争比”之间精确的权衡。

Abstract: In the improving multi-armed bandits problem, each of $k$ arms has an unknown nondecreasing, discretely concave reward curve $f_i$, and pulling arm $i$ for the $t$-th time yields $f_i(t)$. For sufficiently long horizons, Blum and Ravichandran (ALT 2025) proved that randomized algorithms achieve an $O(\sqrt k)$ approximation to the best single arm when the scale $m=f^*(T)$ of the optimal arm is known ($T\ge2k$), and $O(\sqrt k\log k)$ when it is not ($T>4k$), against an $Ω(\sqrt k)$ lower bound. The logarithmic factor is unnecessary: a one-page \emph{probe-and-commit} algorithm achieves competitive ratio $4\sqrt3\,\sqrt k$ for $T\ge2\lfloor\sqrt k\rfloor$, without any knowledge of the scale, and we determine the optimal ratio for every horizon, $Θ(\sqrt k+k/T)$, also for unknown horizons. Without noise, \emph{no prior is needed at all}: a random-marginal probing algorithm reading neither the scale $m$, nor the concavity-envelope exponent $β$ of Blum, Garicano, Ravichandran and Sharma (UAI 2026), nor the horizon $T$, achieves the optimal $Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T)$ simultaneously for every $β$ and every horizon. Under the multiplicative noise model of Blum and Ravichandran, probe-and-commit keeps the same all-horizon order $Θ(\sqrt k+k/T)$ without knowing the noise level (and $Θ(\sqrt k)$ on the same range), but the price of priors jumps: for any fixed noise level $\varepsilon\in(0,1/2]$, the uniform price of adaptation $φ_\varepsilon(k)$ --- the worst case over horizons $T\ge16k$ of the loss relative to $k^{β/(1+β)}$ for algorithms knowing neither $m$ nor $β$ --- is $Θ_\varepsilon(\sqrt{\log k/\log\log k})$, the lower bound asymptotic in $k$ at fixed positive $\varepsilon$ and matched by a nested random-permutation probing algorithm, whereas knowing either $m$ or $β$ alone restores a constant price.

</details>


### [277] [Lecture notes on Physics Informed Neural Networks, Neural Operators, and their applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17638)
*Alessandro Bombini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是2025/2026学年在博尔扎诺大学开设的博士课程《物理信息神经网络》的讲义。课程旨在介绍物理信息深度神经网络（PINN）和神经算子（NOs）的概念，讲解如何从零开始用PyTorch实现，以及如何使用NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo等高级开源库解决工程、物理、石油储层等领域的实际问题，并涵盖混合模型、傅里叶神经算子、物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（PIKANs）等前沿主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物理信息神经网络（PINN）和神经算子（NOs）在科学计算与工程领域的广泛应用，学界需要一个系统化的课程与讲义来培养博士生掌握这些前沿方法，包括其理论原理、从零实现以及在实际场景中的落地应用。

Method: 课程采用理论与实践相结合的教学方式：一方面从原理层面讲解PINN和NOs的数学与深度学习基础；另一方面指导学生使用PyTorch从零实现这些网络，并借助NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo等专门开发的开源库解决真实世界问题（工程、物理、石油储层）。课程内容还覆盖了混合模型（Mixture-of-Models）、傅里叶神经算子（FNO）、物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（PIKANs）等近期热点。

Result: 本讲义系统化地整理了课程内容，涵盖PINN与NOs的基本概念、PyTorch从零实现方法、PhysicsNeMo等开源库的使用，以及FNO、PIKANs、Mixture-of-Models等前沿课题，为博士生提供了完整的学习材料。

Conclusion: 该课程讲义为物理信息深度学习领域提供了条理清晰的教学资源，兼顾理论深度与工程实践，帮助博士生掌握从基础实现到高级开源工具应用的全链路技能，助力其在多个科学与工程领域的实际研究。

Abstract: This is the set of lecture notes for the PhD course \href{https://www.unibz.it/en/faculties/engineering/phd-computer-science/study-course-offering/2025/36967}{\textit{Physics Informed Neural Network}, held at the University of Bozen/Bolzano} in the academic year 2025/2026.
  The goal of the course was to introduce the concept of Physics Informed Deep Neural Networks (PINN) and Neural Operators (NOs), discuss their implementation from scratch in PyTorch and using advanced ad-hoc developed open-source libraries such as NVIDia PhysicsNeMo to address real-world problems in various fields (engineering, physics, petroleum reservoir). We discuss recent topics such as Mixture-of-Models, Fourier Neural Operators, Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (PIKANs) and Fourier Neural Operators.

</details>


### [278] [Fathom: Per-Query Read Depth for Sparse Decoding over Offloaded KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17652)
*Vivek Kalyanarangan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fathom是一种面向百万级token agentic会话的关键词扫描（key scan）方法，利用4-bit K缓存按通道主序（channel-major）存储为位平面（bit plane），让每个查询通过反向注水（reverse water-filling）按通道方差加权重要性分配比特预算。相比136-bit扫描（Double Sparsity、Loki、SparQ r=32），其GPU解码速度快1.67倍；与SparQ r=16的68-bit读取相比，在七分之六的模型和上下文设置中读取字节少18%且注意力误差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当agentic会话扩展到百万token且多个会话同时驻留时，KV缓存和排名索引存于主机内存（host memory），对全部n个键进行top-k步排名的扫描成为限制解码吞吐的流量瓶颈。现有方法（Double Sparsity、Loki、SparQ）使用固定比特数的扫描（如136-bit或68-bit），未根据查询和通道的重要性自适应分配读取量，导致流量开销大而精度不足。

Method: Fathom的关键创新在于：(1) 将4-bit K缓存按通道主序存储为位平面，使得t个平面的前缀恰好是该通道的t-bit量化器；(2) 每个查询通过反向注水算法，依据各通道方差加权的重要性，在其比特预算内决定读取每个键通道的比特数；(3) 复用量化服务栈已有的4-bit K副本作为存储，不额外增加内存开销。该方法针对KV索引驻留主机内存的场景优化，而非GPU内存驻留场景。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上处理一百万个token时，Fathom的GPU解码时间比136-bit扫描（Double Sparsity、Loki、SparQ r=32）快1.67倍；在与SparQ 68-bit读取（r=16）相同的GPU时间内，Fathom少读18%字节且在七个模型/上下文设置中有六个注意力误差更低。在RULER风格任务中，每个token的扫描均与精确top-k解码一致；在真实编码agent会话中，Fathom在92位比特数下即达到最精确136-bit扫描的步骤一致率。

Conclusion: Fathom表明按查询自适应分配通道读取比特数（而非固定比特扫描）能在保持精度的同时大幅降低主机内存到GPU的流量，成为百万token级agentic解码中索引扫描的高效方案。但其优势仅限于KV索引驻留主机内存的场景，当索引位于GPU内存时该方法并无加速收益。

Abstract: When agentic sessions run to a million tokens with many sessions resident at once, the KV cache and the index that ranks it live in host memory, and the scan that ranks all n keys for a top-k step becomes the traffic that bounds decoding. We present Fathom, a key scan in which each query decides how many bits of each key channel to read. The 4-bit K cache is stored channel-major as bit planes, so a prefix of t planes is exactly the channel's t-bit quantizer, and the query spends its bit budget by reverse water-filling over the variance-weighted importance of its channels. At one million tokens on Qwen3-8B a decode step is 1.67x faster in GPU time than with the 136-bit scans of Double Sparsity, Loki and SparQ r=32, and in the same GPU time as SparQ's 68-bit read (r=16) Fathom reads 18% fewer bytes with lower attention error on six of seven model and context settings. On RULER-style tasks every per-token scan matches exact top-k decoding, and on real coding-agent sessions Fathom reaches the step agreement of the most accurate 136-bit scan at 92 bits. The store is the 4-bit K copy a quantized serving stack already holds, and the method is not faster when the index is resident in GPU memory.

</details>


### [279] [Reflect, Revise, Reuse: Training-Free Skill Evolution for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17653)
*Bofan Chen,Boxuan Zhang,Fei Tang,Zhengxi Lu,Yong Du,Tongbo Chen,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的GUI智能体技能演化框架EvoSkill-GUI，通过反射-修订-复用循环在部署时从执行反馈中动态修订技能，提升多个基础模型在三大GUI基准上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体技能框架将技能视为部署前生成的静态工件，未针对GUI执行动态特性（弹窗、延迟加载、控件移动）设计，无法在运行时改进。作者认为需要能根据执行反馈在部署时修订的技能，而非更优的静态技能。

Method: EvoSkill-GUI采用训练自由框架，每个技能为结构化多文件包（含检索元数据、可执行计划、备份定位、失败恢复规则、辅助工具、失败案例）。核心是reflect-revise-reuse循环：执行器进行即时修订，隔离的批评者在严格信息隔离下诊断失败轨迹，执行器通过受限工具接口编辑特定技能文件。

Result: 在MobileWorld、AndroidWorld、OSWorld三大GUI基准上，EvoSkill-GUI无需训练即可持续提升多个基础模型，最大提升分别为+16.2%、+6.0%、+10.5%；演化后的技能库可复用于相关任务，无需从头构建。

Conclusion: 验证了部署时技能演化作为训练自由、跨平台有效的范式，优于静态技能，为GUI智能体长效自适应提供新方向。

Abstract: GUI agents execute long-horizon tasks on dynamic graphical user interfaces, where pop-ups, delayed loads, and relocated widgets routinely invalidate plans fixed before execution. Recent agent-skill frameworks encapsulate reusable procedural knowledge to mitigate this, yet existing skill designs are largely developed without targeting GUI execution dynamics and treat skills as static artifacts produced before deployment rather than living procedural knowledge that improves through it. We argue that what GUI agents need is not better static skills, but skills that can be revised from execution feedback at deployment time, without additional training. We propose \textbf{EvoSkill-GUI}, a training-free framework in which each skill is a structured multi-file package containing retrieval metadata, executable plans, backup localization, failure-recovery rules, accessibility utilities, and failure cases. EvoSkill-GUI operates through a \textbf{\emph{reflect-revise-reuse}} loop: the executor performs instant in-rollout revisions, an isolated critic diagnoses failed trajectories under strict information isolation, and the executor edits specific skill files through a restricted tool interface. Across MobileWorld, AndroidWorld, and OSWorld, three mainstream GUI benchmarks spanning mobile and desktop platforms, EvoSkill-GUI consistently improves multiple base models without any training, with maximum gains of $+16.2\%$, $+6.0\%$, and $+10.5\%$ respectively, and evolved skill libraries continue to benefit related tasks rather than being rebuilt from scratch. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/EvoSkill-GUI.

</details>


### [280] [Regularized Least Squares Training of Quadratic Neural Networks with Applications to System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17654)
*Luis Rodrigues,Zachary Yetman Van Egmond,Mohammad R. Amiri Fard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带正则化的二次神经网络最小二乘训练方法，在正则化系数为正时给出训练优化问题的最优解下界，并提供近似解及其灵敏度的闭式表达式；当下界紧且正则化系数为零时近似解即为最优解，该方法相比反向传播等迭代数值方法显著减少计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络训练依赖反向传播等迭代数值方法，计算耗时且易陷入局部极小值。作者希望通过最小二乘方法为二次神经网络建立解析（闭式）的训练与灵敏度表达式，从而克服迭代方法的固有缺陷，并建立与核范数最小化之间的联系。

Method: 采用带正则化的最小二乘框架训练二次神经网络。该方法推导出训练优化问题在正正则化系数下的下界，并给出近似权重及权重对数据误差灵敏度的闭式表达式；同时将求解下界的优化问题与核范数最小化建立联系。在正则化系数为零时，下界紧且近似解即最优解。

Result: 所提方法可显著降低计算时间（相比反向传播），并成功应用于非线性系统辨识实例，在所提下界与最优值之间进行对比验证。

Conclusion: 该最小二乘训练方法具有三大贡献：权重解析表达式、权重对数据误差的解析灵敏度、以及下界优化与核范数最小化之间的关联。它为二次神经网络的快速、可解析训练提供了新途径，并在正则化系数为零时达到最优。

Abstract: This paper proposes a least squares approach for the training of quadratic neural networks with regularization. The proposed methodology yields a lower bound on the solution of the training optimization problem for the case where the regularization coefficient is positive. Moreover, it yields closed-form expressions for the approximate solution and its sensitivity The lower bound is tight and the approximate solution is the optimal solution when the regularization coefficient is zero. Having a closed-form expression for the weights reduces considerably the computational time when compared with iterative numerical methods such as backpropagation that can get stuck in local minima. The proposed approach has three main contributions, namely, (i) it yields an analytical expression for the weights, (ii) an analytical expression for the sensitivity of the weights to errors in the data is also provided, (iii) it establishes a connection between the optimization to compute a lower bound and nuclear norm minimization. The proposed least squares training is successfully applied to a nonlinear system identification example where the proposed lower bound is compared with the optimal value.

</details>


### [281] [DSD: Learning Diverse and Reusable Motor Skills via Diffusion Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17682)
*Sun Woo Kim,Xue Bin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出扩散技能发现（DSD）方法，利用扩散模型通过分数匹配近似策略诱导状态分布的熵梯度，从而在高维人形控制任务中发现更广泛的、可复用的运动技能，支持分层控制和零样本控制等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过复用丰富的运动技能库高效学习新任务，模拟角色也应如此。现有技能发现方法通过最大化技能潜在变量与状态之间的互信息来学习多样技能，其中边际状态熵促进行为覆盖、条件熵保证行为一致性。然而，高维控制问题中边际状态熵的直接估计不可行，先前方法依赖潜在空间近似或粗糙的状态分布估计器，未能有效促进状态空间的广泛覆盖，导致技能行为多样性有限、下游任务效用降低。

Method: 提出DSD方法，使用扩散模型通过分数匹配来近似策略诱导状态分布的熵梯度，以此构建目标函数，鼓励在高维人形控制中发现更广泛的行为范围。学到的技能在两种下游控制设置中复用：分层控制（任务特定的高层策略）和零样本控制（从离线轨迹中进行潜在选择）。

Result: 实验表明，与先前的技能发现方法相比，DSD发现了更广泛的、可复用的运动技能库，涌现出复杂而敏捷的行为，并能在下游任务中有效复用。

Conclusion: 通过扩散模型对边际状态熵梯度的近似，DSD有效克服了高维控制中熵估计不可行的难题，显著提升了技能发现的多样性与下游任务的可复用性，为模拟角色技能学习提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Humans efficiently learn new tasks by reusing a rich repertoire of motor skills across different goals and contexts. A similar strategy can also be used to enable simulated characters to efficiently perform new tasks by leveraging reusable motor skills. To support a wide range of downstream tasks, the learned repertoire should be diverse, consisting of distinct behaviors as well as spatial and temporal variation within each behavior. A commonly used method for learning diverse skills is by maximizing the mutual information between skill latents and the states produced by a policy. The marginal state entropy promotes broad behavioral coverage, while the conditional entropy encourages consistent behaviors from each latent. However, directly estimating the marginal state entropy is intractable in high-dimensional control problems. Prior methods therefore rely on indirect latent-space approximations or coarse estimators of the state distribution. These approximations may not effectively promote broad coverage of the state space, resulting in skills with limited behavioral diversity and reduced utility for downstream tasks. In this work, we propose Diffusion Skill Discovery (DSD), a skill discovery method that uses a diffusion model to approximate the entropy gradient of the policy-induced state distribution through score matching. The resulting objective encourages the discovery of skills that produce a broader range of behaviors for high-dimensional humanoid control. The learned skills are reused in two downstream control settings: hierarchical control with a task-specific high-level policy and zero-shot control through latent selection from offline trajectories. Our experiments show that DSD discovers a broader repertoire of reusable motor skills than prior skill discovery methods, leading to the emergence of complex and agile behaviors that can be reused across downstream tasks.

</details>


### [282] [The Missing "I Don't Know": Why Three Reasoning-Reliability Findings Converge on Calibrated Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17686)
*Srijith Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出三项看似无关的LLM可靠性研究（工具可靠性表征崩溃、安全约束下模型对违规片段的处理差异、以及无“我不知道”函数时必然幻觉的理论证明）共同指向一个缺失能力：校准式弃答（calibrated abstention）。尽管三者所揭示的缺口来源不同（能力缺口、策略缺口、递归论缺口），但对策一致。作者进一步指出，当前主流基准对拒绝回答赋予零奖励，导致选择该校准函数的“排行榜梯度”不存在，虽然诚实性后训练缩小了部署模型中的差距，但理论上彻底闭合仍需基准改革。作者提出四项评估改革：三重评分、弃答率报告、能力分层评估和强制校准指标，并强调基准改革是必要但非充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和三项看似无关的LLM可靠性问题，揭示其共同根因，并指出当前基准设计中的关键盲区——排行榜对弃答不提供选择压力，从而阻碍理论要求的校准式弃答能力的形成。

Method: 通过综合三项实证与理论结果（Yin等、Suleymanov等、Bastounis等），进行归因分析，识别共同缺失能力；进而从训练信号角度论证基准激励缺失的机制，并提出四项具体的评估改革方案。

Result: 三项研究发现均指向“校准式弃答”这一单一干预；同时证实主流基准对弃答赋予零奖励，导致选择该功能的排行榜梯度不存在；提出四项评估改革（三重评分、弃答率报告、能力分层评估、强制校准指标）。

Conclusion: 基准改革是缩小Bastounis定理所揭示差距的必要但非充分条件；若要原则上闭合该类问题，必须引入校准式弃答功能，并调整评估体系以提供相应的选择压力。

Abstract: Three recent results describe what look like unrelated LLM reliability problems. Yin et al. (2026) show reasoning RL collapses tool-reliability representations. Suleymanov et al. (2026) show that under safety-constrained generation, large models rewrite flagged spans while small models truncate. Bastounis et al. (2024) prove any consistent-reasoning system without an implicit "I don't know" function must hallucinate infinitely often on broad problem classes. We argue these findings converge on a single intervention: calibrated abstention is what each independently identifies as the missing capability, even though the unavailability they document, a capability gap, a policy gap, and a recursion-theoretic gap, has a different source in each case. Honesty post-training has narrowed the gap in deployed models, but principled closure of the class Bastounis identifies requires a calibrated abstention function whose training signal at the leaderboard level is absent: dominant benchmarks assign zero reward to decline, so the leaderboard gradient that would select for the function does not exist. We propose four changes to evaluation: triple-scoring, abstention-rate reporting, capability-stratified evaluation, and mandatory calibration metrics. Benchmark reform is necessary, not sufficient, for closing the gap the theorem identifies.

</details>


### [283] [Accelerating Diffusion Sampling via Speculative Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17691)
*Marcello Bullo,Yanxiao Liu,Öykü Sıla Güner,Arpan Mukherjee,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将扩散模型中的投机采样（speculative sampling）与相对熵编码（REC）联系起来，指出已有的前瞻草稿无需局限于线性链式结构，从而提出"草稿树"（draft trees）方法丰富每轮候选状态，降低目标函数的计算代价；同时采用REC中的贪婪拒绝采样作为草稿-目标耦合，在保证精确目标采样的前提下提升接受率。实验表明在多种目标与草稿模型组合下，相对反射最大耦合基线最高可加速8.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机采样通过草拟廉价候选状态并在保持目标分布精确的耦合下修正，可减少昂贵的扩散模型目标评估次数。然而现有基于反射最大耦合的扩散采样器在拓扑上受限：其前瞻草稿构成单一线性链式图（chain graph），本质上限制了每次目标评估的接受率。

Method: 将投机采样与相对熵编码（REC）建立理论联系，论证前瞻草稿不必是线性的；据此提出核心贡献"草稿树"（draft trees），使每轮考虑的候选状态更加丰富，从而降低目标函数评估次数。同时采用REC中的贪婪拒绝采样（greedy rejection sampling）作为草稿-目标的耦合机制，在提升接受率的同时保证目标样本的精确性。

Result: 在多种目标模型与草稿模型组合的实验中，相比反射耦合基线，该方法在实际设置下最高可获得8.3%的加速效果。

Conclusion: 草稿树结合贪婪拒绝采样可突破现有扩散投机采样器的拓扑限制，在保证精确目标采样的前提下有效地提高接受率与采样加速比，为扩散模型加速生成提供了新的REC视角与实用方案。

Abstract: Speculative sampling accelerates diffusion model generation by drafting inexpensive candidate states and correcting them under a coupling that preserves the target distribution exactly, reducing the number of expensive target evaluations. Existing diffusion samplers, notably those based on reflection maximal coupling, are topologically constrained: their lookahead drafts form a chain graph, a single linear sequence, which inherently limits the acceptance rate per target evaluation. We connect speculative sampling in diffusion models to relative entropy coding (REC). This perspective shows the lookahead need not be linear and motivates our central contribution, draft trees, which enrich the candidates considered per round and lower the target function evaluations. We further adopt greedy rejection sampling, an REC algorithm, as the draft-target coupling, improving acceptance while guaranteeing exact target samples. Experiments across diverse target and draft models demonstrate up to 8.3% acceleration over the reflection coupling baseline in practical settings.

</details>


### [284] [Composite-Gradient Learning for Shared Control Authority Between Deep Reinforcement Learning and Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17697)
*Giray Önür,Azita Dabiri,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种复合梯度学习（CGL）方法，将MPC控制器显式整合进DRL学习过程，通过将DRL与MPC的控制输入表示为联合动作并在更新DRL智能体时考虑二者的交互作用，从而改进DRL-MPC共享控制框架下的学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在DRL-MPC共享控制框架中，DRL智能体与MPC控制器各自决定部分控制输入。然而现有学习范式往往将MPC视为环境的一部分，未显式考虑MPC对控制的贡献及其与DRL智能体之间的交互作用，导致DRL智能体的策略更新不准确。

Method: 提出复合梯度学习（CGL）方法：将DRL与MPC的控制输入表示为一个联合动作空间，在训练过程中更新DRL智能体时显式纳入MPC的贡献及其与DRL动作的交互梯度，从而将MPC整合进学习过程。

Result: 在两个具有不同交互强度的多类别高速公路交通网络上进行评估，并与将MPC视为环境的一部分或仅部分纳入MPC的替代方法对比。结果显示：在弱交互下CGL收益有限；在强交互下，CGL在部分训练运行中能学习到性能更优的控制策略，但平均控制性能提升仍然有限。

Conclusion: CGL方法通过显式整合MPC交互改进了DRL学习过程，但其收益依赖于DRL与MPC控制输入的交互强度，在强交互场景下具有潜力，整体平均性能增益却不显著，需结合具体场景权衡使用。

Abstract: Integrated deep reinforcement learning (DRL) and model predictive control (MPC) methods are increasingly used to control autonomous systems by combining their complementary capabilities. DRL learns control policies through interaction with the environment. MPC uses a system model to optimize control inputs while accounting for constraints. In DRL-MPC frameworks with shared control authority, both the DRL agent and the MPC controller each determine part of the control inputs. However, common learning formulations treat MPC as part of the environment and therefore do not explicitly account for MPC's contribution to control or its interaction with the DRL agent. This paper proposes a novel composite-gradient learning (CGL) method that integrates the MPC controller into the learning process by representing the DRL and MPC control inputs as a joint action and accounting for their interaction when updating the DRL agent during training. CGL is evaluated on two multi-class freeway traffic networks with different strengths of interaction between the DRL and MPC control inputs and it is compared with alternative methods that treat MPC as part of the environment or that only partially incorporate MPC into learning. The results show that CGL offers limited benefit under weak interaction, but learns higher-performing control policies than the alternative methods in a subset of training runs under strong interaction, although the average control-performance gains remain modest.

</details>


### [285] [REVERSAL-BENCH: A Reversibility Axis and Reset Oracle for Measuring the Reset-Free RL Cliff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17745)
*Riyaaz Shaik,Chandru Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REVERSAL-BENCH基准，通过连续参数ρ∈[0,1]控制环境可逆性并提供重置预言机（reset oracle）和可恢复性真值验证机制，在五个物理引擎的八种操作场景中系统评估策略。研究发现存在尖锐的"可逆性悬崖"：随ρ增加，无重置智能体（reset-free）被持续吸收进不可恢复状态，而回合制智能体则能稳定学习。这种吸收现象在真实物理仿真和学习的操作策略下依然存在，且通过几何相同的可逆对照证实该失效由不可逆性而非障碍复杂度因果驱动。此外，针对可恢复性预测与安全护栏（safety shield）的评估表明，可恢复性可被准确预测，但主动恢复仅在智能体能物理规避陷阱时才能成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主强化学习的核心目标是在无外部重置的情况下持续训练策略。然而现有范式高度依赖环境内在的可逆性，这在真实世界操作任务（如把物体推下桌、撒出颗粒物质）中并不存在。作者旨在量化可逆性对无重置学习的影响，并揭示现有reset-free框架在不可逆环境中的失效机制。

Method: 构建REVERSAL-BENCH基准：用连续参数ρ∈[0,1]控制可逆性，提供重置预言机作为真值验证机制，覆盖五个物理引擎中的八种操作设置。系统评估标准actor-critic算法、安全强化学习（safe RL）以及专门的reset-free框架，并设计几何相同的可逆对照环境以分离不可逆性与障碍复杂度的因果作用。同时评估可恢复性预测模型和在不可逆失效前干预的安全护栏。

Result: 发现"可逆性悬崖"：随ρ增大，reset-free智能体（包括autonomous reset-free基线及约束RL）持续被吸收进不可恢复状态且永久被困，学习停止；而回合制智能体保持稳定学习。该吸收现象在完整物理仿真和学习的操作策略下持续存在。与几何相同可逆对照相比，确认失效由不可逆性因果驱动而非障碍复杂度。可恢复性可被准确预测，但主动恢复仅在智能体能物理规避陷阱时成功。

Conclusion: 由于缺乏外部重置，reset-free智能体一旦进入不可恢复状态即被永久吸收，导致学习停滞，这是现有无重置训练范式的根本性缺陷。论文发布包含大规模多模拟器数据集（带可恢复性标签）、重置预言机的基准套件，并指出可恢复性预测虽可行，但单纯依靠主动恢复不足以解决该问题，需要智能体能够物理规避陷阱。

Abstract: A central goal of autonomous reinforcement learning is continuous policy training without external resets. However, existing paradigms largely depend on underlying environmental reversibility, a property absent in real world manipulation, where events such as pushing objects off tables or spilling granular substances cannot be undone. We introduce REVERSAL-BENCH, a benchmark that controls reversibility via a continuous parameter $ρ\in [0, 1]$ and provides a reset oracle, a ground-truth verification mechanism to test state recoverability across eight manipulation settings in five physics engines. Evaluating a broad spectrum of policy architectures, including standard actor-critic algorithms, safe RL, and specialized reset-free frameworks, reveals a sharp reversibility cliff: reset-free agents are consistently absorbed into irrecoverable states as $ρ$ increases, whereas episodic agents maintain steady learning. We see this failure mode across autonomous reset-free baselines and constrained RL. Because reset-free agents lack external resets, any transition into an irrecoverable state results in permanent absorption, leaving the agent trapped where further learning halts. We show that this absorption phenomenon persists in full physics simulations under learned manipulation policies. By evaluating against geometrically identical reversible counterparts, we confirm that this breakdown is causally driven by irreversibility rather than obstacle complexity. We release the benchmark suite, a large multi-simulator dataset labeled with recoverability and a reset oracle. We also evaluate a safety shield that intervenes before irreversible failures occur, showing that while recoverability can be predicted accurately, active recovery primarily succeeds only when the agent can physically steer clear of the trap

</details>


### [286] [SAM-on-the-Curve: Sharpness-Aware Mode Connectivity for Robust Weight-Space Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17748)
*Alejandro Calatrava,Xu Zhang,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出尖锐模式连通性（Sharp Mode Connectivity, SMC），将模式连通性（MC）从一维低损失路径的几何标准重构为邻域鲁棒的路径优化问题。SMC对极小极大功能应用一阶锐度感知近似，使整条路径及其邻域保持平坦。在CIFAR-10-C严重模糊损坏下，SMC相比标准MC实现高达6.09%的绝对精度提升，并能产生负损失障碍（路径内部点的性能优于端点平均损失），在ResNet-18、VGG16-BN和ViT-Tiny上均验证了路径级平坦性作为鲁棒权值空间插值原则的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模式连通性（MC）仅沿一维轨迹控制损失，但未约束轨迹周围的权值空间邻域，导致优化出的曲线可能穿越尖锐脊线，在分布偏移下变得脆弱。作者认为低损失连通性是不完整的几何准则，亟需将平坦性纳入连通性优化目标以增强鲁棒性。

Method: 将模式连通性重新表述为邻域鲁棒的路径优化问题，寻求整条曲线局部邻域都保持低损失的路径。对得到的极小极大泛函应用一阶锐度感知近似（sharpness-aware approximation），强制沿整条曲线（而非仅曲线本身）保持平坦，并据此推导出实用的连通路径优化算法。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C的严重模糊损坏下，SMC较标准MC获得最高6.09%的绝对精度提升；且SMC产生负损失障碍，即路径内部点的模型性能优于端点平均损失。该结果在ResNet-18、VGG16-BN和ViT-Tiny上结合CIFAR-10与ImageNet-100数据集得到验证。

Conclusion: 路径级平坦性是实现鲁棒权值空间插值的实用原则。SMC通过将锐度感知融入连通性优化，不仅改善了分布偏移下的鲁棒性，还揭示了平坦连通路径可产生优于端点的插值模型，为权值平均、模型集成与合并等下游技术提供了更稳健的几何基础。

Abstract: Deep neural networks that are independently trained to similar performance can be connected by low-loss parametric curves in weight space, a phenomenon known as Mode Connectivity (MC). This geometric property underpins practical techniques such as weight averaging, model ensembling, and model merging. We argue that low-loss connectivity is an incomplete geometric criterion: it controls loss only along a one-dimensional trajectory while leaving the surrounding weight-space neighborhood unconstrained, so the optimized curve may traverse sharp ridges that become fragile under distribution shift. We therefore reformulate mode connectivity as a neighborhood-robust path optimization problem, seeking a curve whose entire local neighborhood maintains low loss. We propose Sharp Mode Connectivity (SMC), which applies a first-order sharpness-aware approximation to the resulting minimax functional, enforcing flatness along the entire curve rather than only on it. We derive a practical optimization algorithm for connectivity paths under this sharpness-aware objective. Under severe blur corruptions from CIFAR-10-C, SMC achieves up to 6.09\% absolute accuracy improvement over standard MC. Remarkably, SMC produces negative loss barriers, meaning that models obtained at interior points of the optimized path can outperform the average endpoint loss. These results, validated across ResNet-18, VGG16-BN, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-100, establish path-wise flatness as a practical principle for robust weight-space interpolation.

</details>


### [287] [Modular Deep Learning Mechanisms for Auditable Next-Day Wildfire Spread Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17763)
*Miguel Esparza,Aydin Ayanzadeh Ahmad Mousavi,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种模块化增强方法（风向/坡度条件注意力偏置、物理特征检索增强输出校正、火灾条件双流门控），用于次日活跃火灾预测。在Next Day Wildfire Spread基准上对五个骨干网络进行了分阶段消融、方向审计、检索扰动、校准度量和计算成本比较。SwinUNETR融合三种增强后三种子平均F1为0.4216、AUC-PR为0.3673；混合集成模型达到0.4292和0.3790。研究发现风向偏置与输入风方向高度一致但与观测到的次日火灾位移对齐较弱，区分了先验可检测性与预测物理保真度。成果表明预测性能、运维可信度与计算实用性可兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次日野火预测需要模型能在计算过程中同时评估假设与历史证据。尽管深度学习能从遥感数据学习空间模式，但仅靠预测性能无法建立物理保真度和运维可信度。本研究旨在通过模块化增强来暴露和评估域信息组件，使预测结果可审计。

Method: 提出三种模块化增强：1) 风向与坡度条件注意力偏置，用于显式表达方向偏好；2) 物理特征检索增强输出校正，利用九维环境-火灾状态描述符选择历史瓦片，并对冻结模型的logits施加学习校正；3) 火灾条件双流门控。在Next Day Wildfire Spread基准上对五个骨干网络进行分阶段消融、方向审计、检索扰动、校准度量和计算比较评估。

Result: SwinUNETR融合三种增强后三种子平均F1为0.4216、AUC-PR为0.3673；混合集成（两个增强架构与一个非增强架构）达到F1 0.4292、AUC-PR 0.3790。增强收益因架构而异，检索相关的AUC-PR提升未必转化为更高的F1。构建的风向偏置与输入风向密切对齐，但与观测的次日火灾位移对齐较弱。

Conclusion: 该研究构建了一个暴露与评估野火预测模型中选定域信息组件的框架。结果表明预测性能、运维可信度与计算实用性并非相互竞争的目标，可以同时兼顾。

Abstract: Next-day wildfire prediction requires models whose forecasts can be evaluated alongside the assumptions and historical evidence used in their computation. Although deep learning can learn spatial patterns from remote-sensing data, predictive performance alone does not establish physical fidelity or operational trustworthiness. This study investigates three modular augmentations for next-day active-fire prediction: wind- and slope-conditioned attention biases, physics-feature retrieval-augmented output correction, and fire conditioned dual-stream gating. The attention biases expose prescribed directional preferences, while the retrieval module selects historical tiles using a nine-dimensional environmental and fire-state descriptor and applies a learned correction to a frozen model's logits. The modules are evaluated across five backbones on the Next Day Wildfire Spread benchmark, using staged ablations, directional audits, retrieval perturbations, calibration measures, and computational comparisons. The three-seed mean F1 score and area under the precision--recall curve (AUC-PR) of a SwinUNETR model with all three augmentations are 0.4216 and 0.3673. Then, a mixed ensemble (two augmented architectures and one non-augmented architecture) model achieves 0.4292 and 0.3790. Benefits vary across architectures, and retrieval-related improvements in AUC-PR do not consistently translate into higher F1. The constructed wind bias aligns closely with input wind, but its alignment with observed next-day fire displacement is much weaker, distinguishing prior inspectability from predictive physical fidelity. The study contributes a framework for exposing and evaluating selected domain-informed components within wildfire prediction models. Together, the results presented show that predictive performance, operational trustworthiness, and computational practicality need not be competing objectives.

</details>


### [288] [Principled Koopman Representations with Kalman Inference for Efficient Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17815)
*Ruiquan Li,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出K²SVD方法，通过优化Hilbert-Schmidt目标显式学习Koopman算子的前导奇异函数，构造数学上一致的Koopman空间，并配合线性高斯状态空间模型与卡尔曼滤波进行预测，在多个数据集上超越现有最优方法，同时显著降低维度与计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于神经网络的潜在Koopman空间学习方法往往没有构造数学上有效的Koopman空间，这些表示与算子理论公式不一致，且未能捕捉动力系统的内在低秩结构，从而影响预测性能。

Method: K²SVD通过优化Hilbert-Schmidt目标显式学习Koopman算子的前导奇异函数，得到算子良好的低秩近似和可解释的线性组合；在学得的Koopman空间内，用线性高斯状态空间模型刻画时间演化，并通过卡尔曼滤波进行推断，以缓解多步预测中的噪声累积。

Result: 所学的紧凑潜在空间维度仅为先前工作的不到10%；在多个数据集上超越现有最优方法，预测速度显著更快、计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 有原则的低秩Koopman表示能带来显著的性能与效率优势，为更广泛的应用开辟了潜力。

Abstract: The Koopman operator has been widely used for time-series prediction in dynamical systems. However, prior work that learns latent ``Koopman spaces'' using neural networks often did not construct a valid Koopman space for forecasting, as these representations may be mathematically inconsistent with the operator-theoretic formulation and fail to capture the intrinsic low-rank structure of system dynamics. To address this issue, we introduce K$^2$SVD, a method that explicitly learns the leading singular functions of the Koopman operator by optimizing a Hilbert-Schmidt objective. This yields a well-defined low-rank approximation of the Koopman operator with an interpretable linear combination, featuring a compact latent space with less than $10\%$ of the dimensions used in previous work. In the learned Koopman space, K$^2$SVD further captures temporal evolution with a linear Gaussian state-space model and performs inference via Kalman filtering, mitigating noise accumulation during multi-step prediction. Empirical results show that K$^2$SVD outperforms state-of-the-art methods across multiple datasets, with significantly faster prediction speeds and lower computational cost than previous efficiency-focused models. This highlights the benefits of principled low-rank Koopman representations and opens up broader potential for applications.

</details>


### [289] [The Free Inference Dimension: Complexity Measure for Zero-Collision Navigation under Hypothesis Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17816)
*Luiz Carlos Castro Guedes,Edward Hermann Haeusler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在有限元强化学习环境下研究嵌套约束族中的智能体行为，发现值混合（VM）智能体无需识别真实环境即可实现近最优零碰撞导航（称为"自由推断"现象），该现象在密度阈值处发生突变。作者形式化了"自由推断维数" dFI，证明其严格小于 VC 维数并与 Natarajan 维相关，给出了 PAC 风格泛化界，并证明"先平均后切换"的混合策略在 Littlestone 型维度下是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solomonoff 归纳法通常将预测建模为对可计算假设的混合，往往能识别真实环境。但作者在先前的元强化学习工作中发现一个不同机制：值混合智能体在不识别真实环境的条件下仍能达到近最优导航效果。作者希望形式化这一"自由推断"现象的边界条件与复杂度度量，解释密度阈值处的性能突变及何时应改用后验众数选择（PMS）策略。

Method: 作者引入自由推断维数 dFI(S,N)，一种组合度量，刻画值混合智能体在保持轨迹一致性前提下可处理的环境复杂度。通过证明其严格小于 VC 维数并与 Natarajan 维数相差一个路径长度因子，捕捉不可分解损失的成本；采用 PAC 风格松弛推导出由 dFI^(epsilon,delta) 驱动的泛化界；同时定义互补的 PMS 识别维数，并论证"先平均至首次碰撞、再切换为选择"的混合策略最优，与 Littlestone 型维度建立联系并通过网格世界实验验证。

Result: 理论结果包含：自由推断维数 dFI 严格小于 VC 维，与 Natarajan 维相差路径长度因子；基于 dFI 的 PAC 泛化界成立；PMS 识别维数被定义；混合（先平均后选择）策略可证明为最优。实验上，网格世界实验支持了与 Littlestone 型维度的联系，验证了密度阈值处性能突变与策略切换的合理性。

Conclusion: 自由推断是元强化学习中一种无需环境识别即可实现近最优性能的机制，其适用范围由 dFI 维数刻画并在密度阈值处结束。混合策略（初始平均值混合、碰撞后切换至众数选择）在理论上最优，统一了 VM 与 PMS 两种范式，并通过与 VC、Natarajan、Littlestone 类学习维度的联系将自由推断纳入经典学习理论框架。

Abstract: Solomonoff induction frames prediction as a mixture over computable hypotheses, typically leading to identification of the true environment. In a finite meta-reinforcement learning setting with nested constraint families, in our previous work, we observe a different regime: a value-mixture (VM) agent achieves near-optimal, zero-collision navigation without identifying the true environment, a phenomenon we call Free Inference. This regime persists up to a sharp density threshold, beyond which performance degrades and posterior-mode selection (PMS) becomes preferable.
  We formalize this behavior via the Free Inference dimension dFI(S,N), a combinatorial measure of the environmental complexity a VM agent can handle while preserving trajectory coherence. We prove dFI is strictly smaller than the VC-dimension and relates to the Natarajan dimension up to a path-length factor, capturing the cost of non-decomposable loss. A PAC-style relaxation yields generalization bounds driven by dFI^(epsilon,delta). We also define a complementary PMS identification dimension and show that a hybrid strategy---averaging until the first collision, then switching to selection---is optimal, with links to Littlestone-type dimensions supported by grid-world experiments.

</details>


### [290] [NObSP: Functional Decomposition of Neural Networks via Oblique Subspace Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17825)
*Alexander Caicedo,Víctor De La Hoz,Santiago Alférez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 NObSP（非线性斜子空间投影）框架，利用训练好的深度神经网络线性末层，通过样本空间中的斜投影将预测分解为显式逐特征贡献函数与交互残差，从而避免特征子空间重叠时的重复计数，同时支持局部解释与全局功能分析。论文建立了与函数式 ANOVA 和 Kolmogorov-Arnold 表示定理的关联，并推导出用于样本外评估的高效偏回归算法。针对卷积网络，NObSP-CAM 经一次性校准后无需反向传播即可生成类别激活图。实验表明其在表格与视觉基准上忠实度与既有归因方法相当；在已知组件函数的合成基准上，NObSP 的函数复现得分为 0.989（KernelSHAP 为 0.966，Integrated Gradients 为 0.922）；在 TinyImageNet 上，贡献向量嵌入将平均最近邻类别纯度从原始激活的 0.654 提升至 0.713，并将平均最近邻距离缩小超过一半。结果表明 NObSP 通过恢复具有可分离正负证据的函数贡献画像，对标量归因方法形成互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度神经网络如何做出决策仍是一项根本性挑战。现有归因方法大多只提供标量重要性分数，难以刻画特征的具体功能贡献形式，且在特征子空间重叠时存在重复计数（double counting）问题。

Method: NObSP 利用已训练网络末层的线性结构，在样本空间中使用斜投影（oblique projections）将预测分解为显式的逐特征贡献函数与交互残差；斜投影避免了特征子空间重叠导致的重复计数。方法上：与函数式 ANOVA 及 Kolmogorov-Arnold 表示定理建立理论联系；推导出用于样本外评估的高效偏回归算法；针对卷积网络提出 NObSP-CAM，经一次性校准后无需反向传播即可生成类别激活图。

Result: 在表格与视觉基准上，NObSP 的忠实度与既有归因方法相当。在具有已知组件函数的合成基准上，NObSP 的函数复现得分（Function Reproduction Score）为 0.989，优于 KernelSHAP（0.966）与 Integrated Gradients（0.922）。在 TinyImageNet 上，贡献向量嵌入将平均最近邻类别纯度从 0.654 提升至 0.713，并将平均最近邻距离降低一半以上。

Conclusion: NObSP 能够恢复具有可分离正负证据的函数贡献画像，以功能性贡献分析补充传统标量归因方法，从而在保持忠实度的同时提供更丰富的解释信息。

Abstract: Understanding how deep neural networks make decisions remains a fundamental challenge. We present NObSP (Nonlinear Oblique Subspace Projections), a framework that decomposes predictions into explicit per feature contribution functions and an interaction residual. NObSP exploits the linear final layer of a trained network and uses oblique projections in sample space to reduce double counting when learned feature subspaces overlap, thereby supporting both local explanations and global functional analysis. We establish connections to functional ANOVA and the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem and derive an efficient partial regression algorithm for out of sample evaluation. For convolutional networks, NObSP-CAM produces class activation maps without backward passes after a one time calibration. Experiments on tabular and vision benchmarks show faithfulness comparable to established attribution methods. On a synthetic benchmark with known component functions, NObSP obtains a Function Reproduction Score of 0.989, compared with 0.966 for KernelSHAP and 0.922 for Integrated Gradients. On TinyImageNet, contribution vector embeddings improve mean nearest neighbor class purity from 0.654 for raw activations to 0.713 and reduce mean neighbor distance by more than half. These results indicate that NObSP complements scalar attribution methods by recovering functional contribution profiles with separable positive and negative evidence.

</details>


### [291] [Procedural Pretraining for Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17831)
*Moritz Friedemann,Zachary Shinnick,Philip Torr,Bruno Andreis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三阶段训练流程（程序性预训练→SMILES分子预训练→下游微调），通过在抽象的程序性生成数据上预训练来为分子性质预测提供可迁移的归纳偏置，实验表明程序性预训练可在分子预训练之后进一步提升性能（Lipophilicity上测试误差降低4.8%），且其收益在数据稀缺时最强、依赖程序性数据的结构而非表层统计特征、并存在最优的程序性训练预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测常受限于下游标注数据集规模小，已有工作通常在大规模无标注分子语料上预训练。作者提出新问题：能否在模型看到任何分子数据之前，从抽象的、程序化生成的数据中学习有用的归纳偏置，以提供与基于分子数据的预训练互补的迁移学习路线。

Method: 采用由程序性预训练、SMILES分子预训练和下游微调组成的三阶段训练流程，系统评估多种程序性任务，涵盖序列结构（如Reverse）、元胞自动机和图推理。通过控制程序性训练预算、分析数据结构影响、并分别检验注意力层与前馈层的贡献，探究迁移的机制与边界。
