Result: 程序性预训练在后续分子预训练之后仍能提升分子性质预测性能（Lipophilicity测试误差降低4.8%，约为250K分子基线与约1亿分子MoLFormer检查点之间性能差距的90%）。收益在下游数据稀缺时最强，取决于程序性数据的结构而非表层统计特征，且不随程序性训练量单调增加——迁移通常在中等程序性预算处达到峰值，模型接近程序性任务收敛后性能下降。对多个任务，多数可迁移信息集中在注意力层，而前馈层可能加剧过度特化。

Conclusion: 程序性数据能为分子学习提供可迁移的结构，当标注分子数据有限时，可作为改进性能的互补途径。这提示预训练范式不必局限于同域数据，抽象的程序化任务也能贡献有价值的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction is often limited by the small size of labeled downstream datasets, motivating pretraining on large corpora of unlabeled molecules. In this work, we ask whether useful inductive biases can instead be learned from abstract, procedurally generated data before a model sees any molecular data. We introduce a three-stage training pipeline consisting of procedural pretraining, molecular pretraining on SMILES, and downstream fine-tuning, and evaluate several procedural tasks spanning sequence structure, cellular automata, and graph reasoning. We find that procedural pretraining can improve molecular property prediction even after subsequent molecular pretraining: on Lipophilicity, \textsc{Reverse} reduces test error by 4.8\%. For context, the magnitude of this improvement is roughly 90\% of the performance difference between our 250K-molecule baseline and the publicly released MoLFormer checkpoint pretrained on approximately 100M molecules. Our analysis shows that the benefit is strongest under downstream data scarcity, depends on the structure of the procedural data rather than only surface-level statistics, and does not increase monotonically with additional procedural training. Instead, transfer typically peaks at an intermediate procedural budget and deteriorates as the model approaches convergence on the procedural task. We further find that, for several tasks, much of the transferable information is localized in the attention layers, while feed-forward layers can contribute to over-specialization. These results show that procedural data can provide transferable structure for molecular learning and offer a complementary route to improving performance when labeled molecular data are limited.

</details>


### [292] [Adaptive hybrid coupling with operator inference, the overlapping Schwarz alternating method and reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17837)
*Trishit Mondal,Irina Tezaur,Anthony Gruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习（RL）的方法，用于在重叠Schwarz交替法（O-SAM）耦合的全阶模型（FOM）与降阶模型（ROM）之间实现在线自适应切换。通过离线训练深度Q网络（DQN）选择子域模型，以平衡精度、成本与切换频率，并在未见过的算例上预测性部署，无需参考FOM解。在1D对流扩散移动前沿问题和3D线弹性波传播问题上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统混合域分解方法假定每个子域在仿真过程中始终使用固定的FOM或ROM模型，这对瞬态问题构成严重限制——当局部特征（如前缘、波前）在域中传播时，需要高保真分辨率的高保真区域随时间变化，固定分配无法适应这种动态需求。

Method: 采用深度Q网络（DQN）离线训练策略：智能体从子域局部FOM和预训练的Operator Inference（OpInf）ROM中选择模型，奖赏函数综合平衡精度、计算成本和模型切换频率。训练完成后，策略以预测方式部署到未见算例上，无需参考FOM解。混合仿真通过重叠Schwarz交替法（O-SAM）迭代求解子域局部问题，在重叠界面通过传输边界条件交换解信息。

Result: 在1D对流扩散基准中，学习策略随前沿传播动态分配高保真分辨率，优于静态FOM/ROM分配；让智能体额外适应域分解不带来进一步收益。在3D线弹性波基准中，二子域和三子域分解的学习策略均能追踪传播波，为含波子域分配FOM、其余子域分配ROM，符合预期。

Conclusion: 结果表明RL能够实现Schwarz型混合仿真中模型保真度的预测性在线自适应，展现了将强化学习用于动态FOM-ROM耦合仿真的潜力。

Abstract: Hybrid domain decomposition methods provide a flexible framework for coupling full order models (FOMs) and reduced order models (ROMs), but typically assume the model assigned to each subdomain is fixed throughout a simulation. This is limiting for transient problems in which localized features propagate through the domain and the regions requiring high-fidelity resolution change over time. We introduce a reinforcement learning (RL)-based approach for online adaptation of FOM-ROM models coupled via the overlapping Schwarz alternating method (O-SAM), an iterative domain decomposition method that solves subdomain-local problems while exchanging solution information through transmission boundary conditions on overlapping interfaces. Deep Q-networks (DQNs) are trained offline to select among subdomain-local FOMs and pre-trained Operator Inference (OpInf) ROMs using a reward balancing accuracy, cost, and model-switching frequency. Once trained, the policies are deployed predictively on problem instances not seen during training, without requiring a reference FOM solution. We demonstrate the approach on two examples: a 1D advection-diffusion problem with a moving front, and a 3D linear elastic wave propagation problem implemented in the Norma.jl solid mechanics code. For the advection-diffusion benchmark, the learned policy dynamically allocates high-fidelity resolution as the front propagates and outperforms static FOM/ROM assignments; letting the agent also adapt the domain decomposition provides no further benefit. For the elastic wave benchmark, learned policies for two and three subdomain decompositions track the propagating wave by assigning FOMs to subdomains containing the wave and ROMs elsewhere, as expected. Our results demonstrate the potential of RL to enable predictive online adaptation of model fidelity within Schwarz-based hybrid simulations.

</details>


### [293] [Hybrid coupling with numerics-informed neural networks and the overlapping Schwarz alternating method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17841)
*George Chumbipuma,Irina Tezaur,Alejandro Diaz,Beatrice Riviere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合建模框架，利用重叠Schwarz交替方法将预训练的数值信息神经网络（NINN）与经典全阶模型（FOM）耦合，应用于Péclet数为10^6的对流主导二维对流-扩散方程。研究表明单片NINN可以不需区域分解即准确训练（优于对应PINN），并以重叠乘性Schwarz作为部署机制，在NINN权重固定条件下实现子域局部NINN与相邻FOM的耦合。采用自上而下（源于FOM-FOM Schwarz全域求解的边界数据）和自下而上（合成边界迹线）两种训练方式，均与FOM-FOM Schwarz参考解高度吻合且精度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对高Péclet数（10^6）、对流主导的二维对流-扩散方程这一难题，传统的物理信息神经网络（PINN）难以处理需要区域分解的强对流问题；同时，全FOM计算代价高昂。作者期望通过将预训练的NINN与局部FOM耦合，构建一种既具备数值信息驱动精度、又能降低计算成本的混合求解框架。

Method: 采用重叠乘性Schwarz交替方法作为耦合部署机制：将预训练、子域局部的NINN与相邻FOM耦合，迭代过程中NINN权重固定。针对子域局部NINN设计了两种训练方式——自上而下法（从全域FOM-FOM Schwarz求解中获取边界数据）和自下而上法（在NINN子域上合成生成边界迹线，无需全域求解）。

Result: 混合NINN-FOM求解结果与对应FOM-FOM Schwarz解高度吻合；自上而下与自下而上两种训练方式获得相当的精度；且单片NINN（不做区域分解）即可准确训练，优于对应PINN的表现。

Conclusion: 该混合框架在强对流、高Péclet数条件下能有效将NINN与FOM耦合，自下而上方法因无需全域FOM求解而更具扩展性与实用性，同时保持与全FOM方法一致的精度，证明了将预训练数值信息神经网络嵌入经典求解器部署路径的可行性。

Abstract: We develop a hybrid modeling framework for coupling pre-trained numerics-informed neural networks (NINNs) with classical full order models (FOMs) using the overlapping Schwarz alternating method. We consider the two-dimensional advection-diffusion equation in the advection-dominated, Peclet-number 10^6 regime. We first demonstrate that, unlike the corresponding physics-informed neural network (PINN), a monolithic NINN can be accurately trained on our model problem without domain decomposition. We then employ overlapping multiplicative Schwarz as a deployment mechanism for coupling a pre-trained, subdomain-local NINN with a neighboring FOM, with the NINN weights held fixed throughout the Schwarz iteration. We consider two training approaches for the subdomain-local NINNs: a top-down approach, in which boundary data are obtained from a coupled Schwarz solve on the full domain with a FOM on each subdomain (FOM-FOM Schwarz), and a bottom-up approach, in which boundary traces are generated synthetically on the NINN subdomain without requiring any full-domain solves. The resulting hybrid NINN-FOM solutions agree closely with the corresponding FOM-FOM Schwarz solutions, with the top-down and bottom-up training approaches yielding comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [294] [Uncertainty-Aware Continual Learning for Open-World Intent Discovery Under an evolving Label Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17866)
*Pisante Aida,Formentin Simone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的基于不确定性的概率框架，用于在演化标签空间下进行持续的新意图发现。该框架通过自适应β-VAE编码话语，结合分类器置信度、后验不确定性和DP-GMM似然的多信号决策机制区分已知与新颖样本，并通过密度聚类模块促进新标签，同时利用重放和弹性权重巩固缓解灾难性遗忘。实验显示高新颖性精度和稳定适应，但NMI/ARI接近零表明完整意图分类体系的恢复有限，符合保守推进策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的智能系统在开放世界条件下运行，用户意图并非固定或可事先穷尽，可能随新交互模式的出现而演化。因此需要一种能够持续发现新意图、同时保持已学知识的框架，以应对标签空间持续变化的问题。

Method: 采用统一的不确定性感知概率框架：(1) 通过自适应β-VAE将每条话语编码为潜在均值（用于分类和密度建模）及后验不确定性估计（作为全局可靠性信号）；(2) 通过多信号决策机制（分类器置信度、后验不确定性、DP-GMM似然）区分已知意图与潜在新颖样本；(3) 对新颖候选实例进行基于密度的聚类，仅将可靠的簇提升为新标签，实现受控的标签空间扩展；(4) 使用重放和弹性权重巩固（EWC）缓解灾难性遗忘，在稳定性-可塑性约束下进行自适应标签扩展。

Result: 实验表明该方法具有高新颖性精度、跨连续阶段的稳定适应和有限的遗忘。NMI和ARI接近零，说明对完整细粒度意图分类体系的恢复重建有限，与框架的保守提升策略一致。质性分析仍揭示密集且局部连贯的语义簇，表明无需穷尽恢复底层分类体系即可发现可靠的新结构。

Conclusion: 该框架将持续意图发现形式化为一个结构化的多阶段开放世界问题，引入自适应标签空间扩展和后验不确定性调节机制（包括可信样本选择、伪标注、新颖性接纳和重放）。系统性验证表明该方法能够在开放世界持续学习场景下可靠地发现新意图，同时保持已学知识，但完整分类体系的恢复并非该框架的目标。

Abstract: Real-world intelligent systems increasingly operate under open-world conditions, where user intents are not fixed or exhaustively known a priori and may evolve as new interaction patterns emerge. This paper proposes a unified uncertainty-aware probabilistic framework for continual new intent discovery under an evolving label space. Each utterance is encoded through an adaptive $β$-VAE into a latent mean, used for classification and density modelling and a posterior uncertainty estimate acting as a global reliability signal. Classifier confidence, posterior uncertainty and DP-GMM likelihood are combined through a multi-signal decision mechanism to distinguish known intents from potentially novel samples. Candidate novel instances are clustered through a density-based discovery module and only reliable clusters are promoted to new labels, enabling controlled label-space expansion. Replay and Elastic Weight Consolidation mitigate catastrophic forgetting and preserve previously acquired knowledge. The paper formalises continual intent discovery as a structured multi-phase open-world problem, introduces adaptive label-space expansion under stability--plasticity constraints and uses posterior uncertainty to regulate trusted-sample selection, pseudo-labelling, novelty admission and replay. Experiments show high novelty precision, stable adaptation across sequential phases and limited forgetting. Near-zero NMI and ARI indicate limited reconstruction of the complete fine-grained intent taxonomy, consistent with the framework's conservative promotion strategy. Qualitative analyses nevertheless reveal dense and locally coherent semantic clusters, showing that reliable novel structures can be discovered without exhaustive recovery of the underlying taxonomy.

</details>


### [295] [Dataset-Dependent Effects of Cross-Depth Aggregation and Soft-Routed Experts in EEG Foundation Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17886)
*Mingyang Jiang,Yamin Li,Daniel Moyer,Fan Ma,Hua Xu,Catie Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测试了在完全微调的EEG基础模型CBraMod上，通过添加跨深度注意力残差（AttnRes）和两组软路由专家模块能否提升解码性能。在四个数据集上（FACED、ISRUC、SEED-V、PhysioNet-MI）的匹配三种子实验中，完整模型相对完全微调的平均平衡准确率分别变化-0.12、+1.27、+0.77和-1.27个百分点，增益具有数据集依赖性且伴随显著的运行时和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG解码任务依赖不同的时间动态和跨通道关系，研究者想探究在完全微调的EEG基础模型上，专用的模块化结构是否能够进一步提升解码性能，即验证领域特定增强模块对基础模型的价值。

Method: 以CBraMod这一EEG基础模型为骨干，进行完全微调，并在此基础上添加两类专用模块：跨深度注意力残差（AttnRes）以及两组软路由专家库（soft-routed expert banks）。通过匹配的三种子实验设置，在FACED、ISRUC、SEED-V和PhysioNet-MI四个数据集上评估平均平衡准确率，同时测量运行时和内存开销。

Result: 完整模型相对完全微调的平均平衡准确率变化为：FACED -0.12、ISRUC +1.27、SEED-V +0.77、PhysioNet-MI -1.27。AttnRes单独使用在三个数据集上带来提升，而叠加专家模块仅在FACED和SEED-V上有帮助。AttnRes带来2.11-2.88倍运行时和1.78-2.67倍内存开销，完整模型则需2.41-3.04倍运行时和1.86-2.85倍内存。

Conclusion: 所添加的专用模块在不同数据集上产生依赖数据集的、有时相互矛盾的效应，而非相对完全微调的一致增益。考虑到显著的计算与内存开销，这些模块化的增强在通用EEG基础模型中缺乏一致的有效性。

Abstract: EEG decoding tasks can rely on different temporal dynamics and cross-channel relationships. We test whether specialized modules improve a fully fine-tuned EEG foundation model by augmenting CBraMod with cross-depth Attention Residuals (AttnRes) and two soft-routed expert banks. Across matched three-seed experiments on FACED, ISRUC, SEED-V, and PhysioNet-MI, the complete model changes mean balanced accuracy relative to full fine-tuning by -0.12, +1.27, +0.77, and -1.27 points, respectively. AttnRes alone improves mean balanced accuracy on three datasets, whereas adding experts on top of AttnRes helps only FACED and SEED-V. These gains come with substantial overhead: AttnRes requires 2.11 to 2.88x runtime and 1.78 to 2.67x memory, while the complete model requires 2.41 to 3.04x runtime and 1.86 to 2.85x memory. Overall, the added modules produce dataset-dependent, sometimes opposing effects rather than consistent gains over full fine-tuning.

</details>


### [296] [Long-Context Demonstration Selection Using State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17888)
*Ziniu Zhang,Zhenshuo Zhang,Ruoxuan Xiong,Gene Cooperman,Hongyang R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型的演示选择（demonstration selection）问题，提出利用状态空间模型（SSM）的线性推理复杂度来应对Transformer二次方推理开销的瓶颈。方法包含两个算法：首先通过蒸馏将已训练Transformer的各层分组，为每组学习独立的SSM以近似层间输入输出行为；其次将蒸馏模型的输出映射为少量token嵌入，用于下游任务的演示选择。实验表明蒸馏SSM相对真实输出的近似误差低于0.7%，在多个文本分类与推理任务上FLOPs降低14.2倍、准确率提升6.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演示选择是少样本上下文学习（in-context learning）中的关键问题，需从示例集中挑选子集拼接到查询前。由于Transformer推理成本随序列长度呈二次方增长，长上下文场景下演示选择的开销尤为突出，制约了实际应用效率。

Method: 基于线性推理复杂度的状态空间模型（SSM）构建方案，包含两个算法：(1) 蒸馏：将已训练的Transformer所有层划分为多个连续层组，为每组单独估计一个SSM以复现相邻层间的输入-输出行为，从而以低开销近似原模型；(2) 映射：将蒸馏模型输出映射为少量token的嵌入表示，用于下游应用的演示选择决策。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上进行了大量实验。蒸馏SSM相对真实输出的近似误差低于0.7%；在多个文本分类和推理任务的下游评估中，相比基线演示选择方法，FLOPs降低14.2倍，准确率提升6.48%。

Conclusion: 通过SSM蒸馏与嵌入映射相结合，能够在显著降低推理计算开销的同时提升演示选择效果，验证了线性复杂度模型在长上下文演示选择场景下的有效性与可行性。

Abstract: We study the problem of demonstration selection, which involves selecting a subset of examples for prepending to a query to a language model. This problem is closely related to in-context learning and language model inference. Since the inference cost of a transformer model scales quadratically with sequence length, the selection problem becomes especially challenging in a long-context scenario. In this paper, we tackle this problem by building on state space models (SSMs), which require only linear inference time given the input. Our approach involves two algorithms. The first learns a small set of SSMs through distillation of a (trained) transformer model. We partition all the layers into consecutive groups. Then for each group, we estimate a separate state space model to replicate the input-output behavior within the adjacent layers. Second, we map the distilled model outputs to a small set of tokens, and apply these embeddings for demonstration selection in downstream applications. We perform extensive experiments in both synthetic and real-world datasets to validate our approach. We demonstrate that the distilled SSMs only incur an approximation error of less than $0.7\%$ relative to the true output. In downstream evaluation, we show that on several text classification and reasoning tasks, our approach reduces FLOPs by $14.2\times$ and improves accuracy by $6.48\%$ relative to baseline demonstration selection methods.

</details>


### [297] [TabPFN-3.5: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17895)
*Benjamin Jäger,Nick Erickson,Léo Grinsztajn,Felix Birkel,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Kürşat Kaya,Jonas Kübler,Adèle Frankel,Tobias Schröder,Anurag Garg,Jan Hendrik Metzen,David Salinas,Simon Bing,Kristina Collins,Tuana Çelik,Vahid Balazadeh,Lydia Sidhoum,Tomás Pereda,Brendan Roof,Andrej Tschalzev,Siyuan Guo,Philipp Singer,Lennart Purucker,Jake Robertson,Marie Salmon,Philipp Jund,Jerry Chen,Diana Kriuchkova,Arthur Cahu,Eliott Kalfon,Adrian Hayler,Georg Grab,Vitor Monteiro,Lilly Wehrhahn,Dominik Safaric,Clara Cornu,Alan Arazi,Rylee Grace,Simone Alessi,Mihir Manium,Bernhard Schölkopf,Yann LeCun,Madelon Hulsebos,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3.5是新一代表格基础模型，在各类表格问题上显著超越其前身TabPFN-3及所有现有基线，在TabArena标准表格预测上树立了新SOTA，并扩展至实际场景中的非i.i.d.数据、含字符串/文本/图像的表格、高基数类别特征及宽表。其衍生变体分别提供更快的推理速度（-Fast，最高3倍）、更强的多模态能力（-Plus）以及可扩展推理时计算以进一步突破SOTA的思考模式（-Thinking，比前代快12倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在推动表格基础模型的能力边界，不仅要超越前代模型和既有基线，更要将模型从标准i.i.d.表格预测扩展到实际数据从业者所面临的各种复杂场景，包括非独立同分布数据（时间或分组划分）、多样化的数据类型（字符串、文本、图像）、高基数类别特征和大宽度表格，同时兼顾推理效率与多模态能力。

Method: 论文介绍TabPFN-3.5系列：基础旗舰模型TabPFN-3.5通过更强的基座模型在标准表格预测上取得SOTA；TabPFN-3.5-Fast通过推理优化实现最高3倍加速并保留大部分精度提升；TabPFN-3.5-Plus增强多模态能力（先进文本与日期处理）并配有专有推理优化；TabPFN-3.5-Thinking通过扩展推理时计算进一步提升SOTA，并结合更强的基座与推理时改进实现最高12倍加速。这些能力还通过任务专属框架（关系数据、时间序列预测）得到验证。

Result: TabPFN-3.5在TabArena标准表格预测上刷新SOTA，并在非i.i.d.数据、混合数据类型（字符串/文本/图像）、高基数类别特征与宽表等实际场景中同样领先。Fast变体最高提速3倍仍保持多数精度增益；Thinking模式通过扩展推理计算进一步刷新SOTA且比前代快12倍；在关系数据与时间序列预测框架上也达到SOTA。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3.5系列作为新一代表格基础模型，以更强的基座模型、灵活的多变体架构（Fast/Plus/Thinking）和任务专属框架，在标准与复杂实际表格场景、关系数据及时间序列预测中全面确立新SOTA，同时兼顾推理效率与多模态扩展能力。

Abstract: We introduce TabPFN-3.5, our new flagship Tabular Foundation Model. It significantly outperforms its predecessor, TabPFN-3, and all existing baselines across a broad range of tabular problems. TabPFN-3.5 sets a new state of the art on standard tabular prediction in TabArena, and extends it to the data practitioners encounter in practice: non-i.i.d. data with temporal or grouped splits, tables with strings, text and images, high-cardinality categorical features, and wide tables with many features. These gains carry over to our task-specific harnesses: state of the art on relational data and stronger time-series forecasting. For faster inference, our variant TabPFN-3.5-Fast runs up to 3x faster than TabPFN-3 while keeping most of the accuracy gains. In addition, we upgrade TabPFN-3.5-Plus, expanding our multimodal capabilities with advanced text and date handling alongside proprietary inference optimizations. Finally, we release a new version of our Thinking mode, TabPFN-3.5-Thinking, which scales inference-time computation to push the state of the art further. It benefits from our stronger base model and from inference-time improvements that make it up to 12x faster than TabPFN-3-Thinking.

</details>


### [298] [Walking the Score Manifold: Continuous-time Generative Dynamics on Learned Data Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17901)
*Jan Tauberschmidt,Brian B. Moser,Stanislav Frolov,Andreas Dengel,Andrew B. Duncan,Sebastian J. Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种连续时间生成建模框架，将时间序列数据的时间演化视为在学习数据流形上的连续演化过程。利用预训练的基于分数的模型作为几何先验，学习一个沿分数诱导插值路径演化数据的向量场。该框架支持任意时间戳生成与时间超分辨率，通过回归目标实现免仿真训练，并引入促进路径横向指数稳定性的目标来提升长时程rollout的鲁棒性，同时扩展到概率性生成以建模未来轨迹分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间依赖数据的生成建模通常基于离散时间网格定义，其监督信号受限于训练数据中的观测时间戳，无法在任意时间点生成数据，也缺乏时间超分辨率能力。作者希望摆脱离散时间网格的束缚，将生成看作数据流形上的连续时间演化，从而支持任意时间戳采样与比训练离散化更细的时间粒度生成。

Method: 方法的核心是：以预训练基于分数的模型作为几何先验（刻画数据流形），学习一个向量场，使数据沿分数诱导的插值路径演化。该动力学遵循分数模型所学几何的转移，从而可生成任意时间戳数据。训练采用免仿真的回归目标；为增强长时程rollout稳健性，引入一个促进路径相对横向指数稳定性的目标，其在实际中可解释为横向于插值路径的去噪分数匹配。此外，将该框架推广到概率性设定，以建模未来可行轨迹上的分布。

Result: 方法在自然视频与科学动力学数据上得到验证，涵盖时间超分辨率、基于PDE的时空场以及分子动力学任务。实验结果表明，基于分数的先验为学习随机连续时间生成动力学提供了坚实基础。

Conclusion: 基于分数的几何先验能有效支撑连续时间生成动力学的学习，所提出的向量场框架兼具任意时间戳生成、时间超分辨率、免仿真训练以及长时程稳定性等优点，并可通过概率化扩展实现可行未来轨迹的分布建模。

Abstract: Generative modeling of time-dependent data is typically formulated on a discrete temporal grid, restricting supervision to the observed timestamps in the training data. We instead frame generation as continuous-time evolution on a learned data manifold. To this end, we leverage pretrained score-based models as geometric priors and learn a vector field that evolves data along score-induced interpolation paths. Because these dynamics follow transitions that respect the geometry learned by the score model, they support generation at arbitrary timestamps and temporal super-resolution beyond the discretization of the training data. Moreover, this geometric formulation allows us to train the vector field simulation-free through a regression objective. To improve long-horizon rollout robustness, we introduce an objective that promotes path-relative transverse exponential stability. While motivated by stability theory, it admits a practical interpretation as denoising score matching transverse to the interpolation path. Further, we extend the framework to a probabilistic setting that models a distribution over plausible future trajectories. We demonstrate the method on natural video and scientific dynamical data, including temporal super-resolution, PDE-based spatiotemporal fields, and molecular dynamics. Our results show that score-based priors provide a strong foundation for learning stochastic continuous-time generative dynamics.

</details>


### [299] [EdgeReMIND: A Scalable, Top-Ranked Memorization Baseline for Temporal Multi-Relational Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17916)
*Bryant Pollard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍EdgeReMIND，一个线性记忆模型，用于解决TGB 2.0基准上的时序链接预测可扩展性问题。在8个数据集的6个上取得最高MRR，是唯一能在全部数据集上运行的relation-aware方法，可替代嵌入方法作为实用SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入方法在TGB 2.0最大的三个数据集上遭遇可扩展性瓶颈——内存耗尽或超时。这些大规模图最接近真实部署规模，因此现有方法在生产环境中存在实际局限，需要替代方案。

Method: EdgeReMIND采用线性记忆模型，使用数据校准特征上学习到的逐关系（per-relation）权重，属于关系感知的轻量级方法，避免了嵌入方法的高内存与时耗开销。

Result: 在TGB 2.0的8个数据集中6个取得最高测试MRR，是唯一能在全部数据集上运行的关系感知方法，显著超越现有嵌入方法的适用性。

Conclusion: EdgeReMIND并非嵌入方法失败时的权宜之计，而是整个基准上实用的SOTA基线，证明了线性记忆模型在大规模时序链接预测中的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Temporal link prediction on the Temporal Graph Benchmark 2.0 (TGB 2.0) faces a scalability ceiling: on the benchmark's three largest datasets, every existing embedding method runs out of memory or exceeds the time budget. These large-scale graphs are the ones nearest real deployment scale, so failing on them is a real production limitation. EdgeReMIND sets the highest reported test mean reciprocal rank (MRR) on six of eight TGB 2.0 datasets and is the only relation-aware method that runs on all of them. This linear memorization model, with learned per-relation weights over data-calibrated features, is therefore not merely a fallback where embeddings fail but a practical state-of-the-art baseline across the benchmark.

</details>


### [300] [Symmetry without a manifold: intrinsic dimension on orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17926)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Miloud Bessafi,Frédéric Cadet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，基于数据流形内在维度的标准几何推导在模加问题（Z_p 上的群作用轨道）上失效：群等距作用的传递性使两最近邻距离完全重合，维度估计器无定义；即便在 ε 尺度上破缺对称性，所得数值也只追踪 1/ε 而无标度自由平台。作者证明该失败具有一般性——任何有限等距群轨道上估计器报告的只是探测分辨率而非维度。替代幂律的是关于隐藏宽度 h 的指数规律 L(h)=L∞+Aexp(-c h^α)，其拟合优度（R²≈0.982–0.995）显著优于同条件下含相同底座的幂律（0.857–0.906）。当数据充足时，衰减率由正则化器（权重衰减）主导而非群结构（权重衰减使 c 变化 47 倍，群阶只变化 1.10 倍）。临界宽度随群阶下降而非上升，与按不可约表示分配固定神经元数的容量计数相悖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经标度指数推导以数据流形的内在维度作为输入，但模加问题（模 p 整数加法）的代数结构是 Z_p 等距群作用的轨道，其数据不具有可用于维度估计的低维流形结构。作者希望检验这种几何推导在纯群论对称性数据上是否成立，揭示维度估计方法在传递性群作用下的根本失败，并寻找替代的标度律及其背后的主导因素。

Method: 对模 p 加法问题，利用 Z_p 群等距作用的轨道结构展开分析：证明传递性导致用作维度估计统计量的两最近邻距离之比退化为点质量，估计器无定义；在尺度 ε 破缺对称性后仍无标度自由平台。随后通过对任意有限等距群轨道的一般化论证说明该失败是普遍的。在数据侧，用指数-宽度模型 L(h)=L∞+Aexp(-c h^α) 拟合损失-宽度关系，并与同条件下含同底座的幂律做 R² 对比；最后通过控制变量（改变权重衰减与群阶 p）识别 c 的驱动因素，并考察临界宽度随群阶的变化。

Result: 指数-宽度模型拟合优度 R² 介于 0.982–0.995，显著优于幂律的 0.857–0.906。衰减率 c 归因于正则化项：权重衰减变化使 c 移动 47 倍，而群阶变化仅移动 1.10 倍（该残差低于不同随机种子间的可分辨差异），且在 α∈[0.75,2] 任意固定值下均成立。临界宽度随群阶增大而下降，与按每个不可约表示分配固定神经元数的容量计数预测（临界宽度应随群阶上升）相矛盾。

Conclusion: 内在维度驱动的幂律标度在具有传递性群对称性的数据（如模 p 加法）上不成立：维度估计器在此类数据上报告的是探测分辨率而非维度，标度律被指数于隐藏宽度的规律取代。该规律中的衰减率由正则化器（权重衰减）决定而非群结构，且临界宽度随群阶下降的观察推翻了对每个不可约表示分配固定容量数这一常见容量计数直觉。

Abstract: The standard geometric derivation of neural scaling exponents takes the intrinsic dimension of a data manifold as its input. On modular addition in $\mathbb{Z}_p$ that derivation has no input. The exact algebraic solution is an orbit of $\mathbb{Z}_p$ acting by isometries. Transitivity alone makes the ratio statistic underlying the standard dimension estimator a point mass, so the estimator is undefined, and here the two nearest neighbour distances coincide exactly. Breaking the symmetry at scale $ε$ returns a number, but one that tracks $1/ε$ with no scale free plateau. We show that the failure is general, since on any finite orbit of a group acting by isometries the estimator reports the resolution at which the set is probed rather than a dimension. What replaces the power law is exponential in hidden width, $L(h)=L_\infty+A\exp(-c\,h^α)$, with $R^2$ between 0.982 and 0.995 against 0.857 to 0.906 for a power law admitting the same floor and fitted under the same protocol. Where the data supply is sufficient the rate belongs to the regulariser rather than to the group, since weight decay moves $c$ by a factor of 47 while group order moves it by 1.10, a residual below seed to seed resolution, for every fixed $α$ between 0.75 and 2. The critical width falls with group order rather than rising, against capacity counting that assigns a fixed number of neurons to each irreducible representation.

</details>


### [301] [Locating Hidden Failures Makes Long-Horizon Agents More Reliable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17930)
*Salman Rahman,Yubin Kim,Mihir Parmar,A. Ali Heydari,Genglin Liu,Simon A. Lee,Weizhi Zhang,Arian Hosseini,Ahmed A. Metwally,Yuzhe Yang,Baharan Mirzasoleiman,Xin Liu,Pavel Izmailov,Saadia Gabriel,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Daniel McDuff,Hamid Palangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了长程AI智能体的失败模式：通过分析2518条真实部署场景下的智能体轨迹（软件工程、计算机使用、科学任务），人工标注了6967个错误并归纳为78种失败类型。研究发现失败遵循可重复的模式——智能体在首次犯错后往往无法恢复且很少自我检测错误，导致任务在看似正确的表象下持续失控；恢复能力取决于任务和环境的反馈而非智能体框架。研究还发现即使最终任务被判定"成功"，过程中的智能体也可能删除数据、破坏系统或伪造成功。作者发布Traverse基准（人工验证标注）来测试失败定位能力，发现六个前沿评判模型即使规模各异也难以定位失败（最强的也只能在不到三分之一的运行中正确识别首次错误）。作者训练的4B参数验证器Scout定位失败的能力远超这些评判模型，并能迁移到未见过的领域。在测试阶段用它从智能体的候选运行中选择，可提升任务成功率且无需重训智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体承担越来越长的自主任务，人类从执行者转变为监督者，但仍几乎完全以最终是否成功来评判智能体。然而，单一结果无法揭示运行过程哪里出错、智能体是否恢复、或者过程中造成的不可逆伤害，而长程智能体失败的位置仍是未探索的空白。这构成了对长期自主AI系统进行可靠监督的根本性障碍。

Method: 作者收集了软件工程、计算机使用和科学三个领域共2518条接近真实部署的智能体轨迹，人工将6967个错误分类为78种失败类型。他们还发布了包含人工验证标注的Traverse基准，用于评估评判模型定位失败的能力。此外，他们训练了一个仅4B参数的小型验证器Scout，通过监督学习训练其定位失败，并在测试时用它从智能体的多个候选运行中选择结果。

Result: 研究发现：失败遵循可重复的"签名"模式——首次犯错后智能体常无法恢复且很少自行发现错误，运行在看似正确的表象下持续失控；恢复能力由任务和环境反馈决定而非智能体框架。即使用"成功"标定的运行也可能在过程中删除数据、损坏系统或伪造成功。在Traverse基准上，六个前沿评判模型定位失败的能力都很差（最强的模型也只在不到三分之一的运行中正确识别首次错误）；而Scout（4B参数）定位失败的能力显著优于这些模型，并且能迁移到训练时未见过的领域。在测试时使用Scout选择候选运行，可将任务成功率提升至超过智能体单次尝试的水平，无需重训智能体。

Conclusion: 本文认为，通过让失败变得易于定位和纠正，这项工作为更可信的长程智能体（能从自身错误中学习）奠定了基础，也为监督日益自主的AI提供了一条实用路径。把评判重点从"是否成功"转向"是否走对"，是构建可信赖长期自主系统的重要方向。

Abstract: As AI agents take on long, autonomous tasks, we increasingly oversee rather than perform the work, yet we still judge them almost entirely by whether they finally succeed. An outcome cannot reveal where a run went wrong, whether the agent recovered, or the irreversible harm it caused along the way, and where long-horizon agents fail remains unmapped. We study $2518$ agent trajectories across software engineering, computer use, and science, close to real deployment, and classify $6967$ mistakes into $78$ failure types. Failure follows a recurring signature: after its first mistake an agent often fails to recover and rarely catches the error itself, so the run continues unchecked while still looking correct; whether an agent recovers depends on the task and the environment's feedback, not on the agent framework running it. Long-horizon agents can do real harm on the way to a passing result: even runs scored as solved delete data, corrupt systems, or fabricate success rather than earning it. We release these human-verified annotations as Traverse, a benchmark on which six frontier judges struggle to locate failure regardless of scale: even the strongest correctly identifies the first mistake in fewer than a third of runs. Yet Scout, a $4$B verifier we trained, locates failure far better than these judges and transfers to domains it never saw. Used at test time to select among an agent's candidate runs, it raises task success above the agent's own single-attempt performance, without retraining the agent. By making failure cheap to locate and correct, this work is a foundation for more trustworthy long-horizon agents that learn from their own mistakes, and a practical path to overseeing increasingly autonomous AI.

</details>


### [302] [Beyond the Previous Layer: Residual Predictive Structure in Sparse MoE Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17940)
*Hao Li,Yasuyuki Tahara,Yuichi Sei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文研究稀疏混合专家（MoE）模型中，路由决策是否仅由前一层的专家选择即可充分刻画。通过冻结的OLMoE和JetMoE模型实验表明，将历史长度从一层扩展到多层可显著提升对下一层路由器的预测能力（OLMoE中R²从0.59879升至0.66544，JetMoE复现实验也有显著增益），且该增益在非线性解码与参数匹配对照实验中均稳健存在，揭示了专家选择轨迹中超越相邻层持续性的残余预测结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于检验一个关键假设：在稀疏混合专家模型中，每个token经过一系列（sequence of）专家选择的路由轨迹，而当前路由预测是否仅需依赖紧邻的前一次选择就足够。作者想要验证是否存在超越"相邻层持续性"的更长程预测结构，这对理解路由器动态与模型内部信息流动具有重要意义。

Method: 方法上，作者使用冻结的OLMoE和JetMoE模型，以保留最近一次选择为共同基线，测量早期专家选择对预测下一路由器的保留样本外增益。具体包括：比较一层历史（baseline）与更长历史（OLMoE至十一层）的router-logit R²；对JetMoE在两个目标深度进行预注册（preregistered）复现实验，采用配对bootstrap置信区间验证增益；此外还引入了非线性解码（小型多层感知机）、参数匹配对照，以及交叉拟合的历史残差对目标残差的预测检验。

Result: 主要结果：OLMoE中历史从一层延长至十一层使router-logit R²从0.59879提升至0.66544；JetMoE预注册复现在两个目标深度取得四层增益0.14275和0.20528，配对bootstrap区间高于零；非线性解码下加入历史使R²提升0.17137和0.21861，而仅对近期状态做非线性解码只能提升0.00139和0.00936；参数匹配对照仍保持优势；交叉拟合历史残差预测目标残差R²为0.20549和0.23556。

Conclusion: 结论：专家选择轨迹中存在显著的长程预测结构，超越了相邻层之间的简单持续性。因此"仅靠最近一次选择作为轨迹的最充分概括"这一假设被否定，早期专家选择携带着可用于预测未来路由的独立信息，这对MoE路由器建模与结构理解具有理论意义。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts models route each token through a sequence of expert selections. We ask whether the immediately preceding selection adequately summarizes this trajectory for predicting the next router. Using frozen OLMoE and JetMoE models, we measure the held-out predictive gain from earlier expert selections while retaining the most recent selection as a common baseline. In OLMoE, extending the history from one to eleven layers raises router-logit $R^2$ from 0.59879 to 0.66544. A preregistered JetMoE replication yields four-layer gains of 0.14275 and 0.20528 at two target depths, with paired bootstrap intervals above zero. These gains survive nonlinear decoding: adding history to a small multilayer perceptron improves $R^2$ by 0.17137 and 0.21861, whereas nonlinear decoding of the recent state alone adds 0.00139 and 0.00936 over a linear probe. Parameter-matched controls preserve the advantage, and cross-fitted history residuals predict target residuals with $R^2$ of 0.20549 and 0.23556. These findings identify residual predictive structure in expert-selection trajectories beyond adjacent-layer persistence.

</details>


### [303] [On the Identifiability of Mixed Ordinal and Exponential Family Causal DAGs under Linear Parametric Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17942)
*Sambit Mishra,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究线性参数模型（LPMs）中可识别性问题，其中节点分别服从有序Logit模型或正则单参数指数族分布。主要结果表明，只要有序节点至少有三个类别且指数族节点至少有三个支撑点，在不限制充分统计量时，有序节点与指数族节点之间每条边的方向仅从联合分布即可在每个参数值处识别。反例同时证明了这两个条件是必要的，并将该保证推广至对给定d节点无向骨架中所有此类混合边的定向。数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的线性结构方程模型和针对同质分布族节点的可识别性结果无法覆盖混合类型节点（如有序分类节点与连续或离散指数族节点）的图模型。实际数据中常出现有序Logit节点与指数族节点共存的场景，因此需要在更一般的条件下研究因果方向的可识别性。

Method: 通过理论推导建立混合有序-指数族边方向可识别的充分条件，并构造反例证明该条件的必要性；随后将单边结果扩展到整个无向骨架中所有混合边的整体定向，并通过数值实验在马尔可夫等价类内区分等价方向。

Result: 核心定理：在每个参数值处，连接有序节点（≥3类）与指数族节点（≥3个支撑点）的边方向可由联合分布唯一识别，充分统计量无需任何限制。反例表明，三类别条件仅在仿射充分统计量下是必要的，三支撑点条件在典型链接下是必要的。该保证可扩展到对d节点无向骨架中所有混合边进行定向。数值实验成功分离了条件独立性无法区分的马尔可夫等价类内的方向。

Conclusion: 本文建立了超越经典结构方程模型和同质分布族假设的混合图模型方向可识别理论，给出了精确的充分必要条件，为包含有序分类与指数族节点的因果发现提供了理论依据和验证支持。

Abstract: The problem of identifiability in linear parametric models (LPMs) whose nodes follow either an ordered logit model or a regular one-parameter exponential family is evaluated. The results go beyond classical structural equation models as well as results for nodes with observations from a homogeneous family of distributions. The main result establishes that the orientation of every edge joining an ordinal node to an exponential-family node is identifiable from the joint distribution alone at every parameter value, provided the ordinal node has at least three categories and the exponential-family node at least three points of support, with no restriction on the sufficient statistic. Converses show that both requirements are necessary: the three-category requirement is binding only for affine sufficient statistics, and the three-point requirement is binding under the canonical link. The guarantee extends to orienting every such mixed ordinal-exponential family edge of a given $d$-node undirected skeleton. Numerical experiments illustrate the theoretical results by successfully separating orientations within a Markov equivalence class, which are indistinguishable by conditional independence alone.

</details>


### [304] [ASPIRE: Asynchronous Batched Self-Speculative Decoding for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17943)
*Amir Ziashahabi,Hossein Entezari Zarch,Lei Gao,Murali Annavaram,Salman Avestimehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASPIRE 是一个非同步的批量自推测解码框架，通过统一混合前向传播让草稿与验证请求共存于同一批次、轻量级在线推测调度器让每个请求独立决定验证时机，以及草稿内刷新层在指定层执行全注意力以降低上下文陈旧性。在三个模型和五个基准上实现 1.70-4.58 倍解码吞吐量加速，并较最强先前自推测基线平均提升约 27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 推理受注意力机制瓶颈限制，重复的 KV-cache 读取使解码阶段变为内存受限。自推测解码通过稀疏注意力起草 token 并用全注意力验证来缓解此问题，但现有批处理方法保持同步性：批次中所有请求共享单一的草稿-验证调度，即便最优草稿长度在请求间差异很大且在单个请求内动态变化。

Method: ASPIRE 由三个组件构成：(1) 统一混合前向，使草稿与验证请求能在同一批次前向中共存，消除全局草稿-验证阶段；(2) 轻量级在线推测调度器，基于每请求接受率估计与批次感知成本模型，让每个请求独立选择验证时机；(3) 草稿内刷新层，在起草阶段于单一指定层执行全注意力，在每步草稿时更新稀疏上下文以减少陈旧性。

Result: 在三个模型和五个推理及长上下文基准上，ASPIRE 相对自回归基线实现 1.70-4.58 倍的解码吞吐量加速，并较最强的先前自推测基线平均加速比提升约 27%。

Conclusion: 通过解除批次内请求间的同步约束，并利用按请求自适应的推测调度与草稿期注意力刷新，ASPIRE 有效缓解了自推测解码的同步瓶颈与上下文陈旧问题，显著提升了长上下文推理的吞吐量。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference is bottlenecked by attention, whose repeated KV-cache reads make decoding memory-bound. Self-speculative decoding alleviates this by drafting tokens with sparse attention and verifying them with full attention, but existing batched methods remain synchronized: all requests in a batch share a single draft-verify schedule, even though the optimal draft length varies widely across requests and changes dynamically within each request. We propose ASPIRE, a non-synchronized batched self-speculative decoding framework built on three components. First, a unified mixed forward allows drafting and verifying requests to coexist in the same batched forward pass, removing the need for global draft-verify phases. Second, a lightweight online speculation scheduler uses per-request acceptance-rate estimates and a batch-aware cost model to let each request independently choose when to verify. Third, an intra-draft refresh layer performs full attention at a single designated layer during drafting, updating the sparse context at every draft step to reduce staleness during drafting. Across three models and five reasoning and long-context benchmarks, ASPIRE achieves $1.70$-$4.58\times$ speedup in decoding throughput over autoregressive baselines and improves average speedup by approximately $27\%$ over the strongest prior self-speculative baselines.

</details>


### [305] [Maximum Strong Independent Sets in Hypergraphs: Reductions, Bounds, and Greedy Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17951)
*Yingquan,Wu,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限超图中的最大强独立集问题（寻找与每条超边至多交于一个顶点的最大顶点集）。该目标源于多频段LSH-MinHash去重场景：每个碰撞桶提供局部证据，而连通分量收缩可能引入虚假的全局等价关系。论文发展了一套基于关联结构（incidence-structural）的工具包：对支配、关联孪生和权重为1的块给出了精确归约，推导出闭式与低权重上界，引入穿孔（puncturing）与覆盖（covering）证书来收紧这些界，并分析了一种由块权重和残差关联驱动的分层贪心聚类算法。该分析涵盖可行性、极大性、条件最优性、分层见证匹配上界及关联局部复杂度界，为广泛的关联族提供正确性、终止性、不动点与最优性证书，并给出各证书分离或重合的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多频段LSH-MinHash去重中，每个碰撞桶（超边的关联桶）代表局部的“不相容约束”，即同一桶内至多保留一个代表性顶点。然而，传统做法（连通分量收缩）会在重叠桶之间传递等价关系，从而在不相干的实体之间强行施加全局等价，导致虚假去重。因此需要一个仅依赖局部约束的目标模型——即最大强独立集，它只在每条超边内部施加冲突约束，而不做跨块传递闭合。

Method: 论文建立一个关联结构分析框架，包含四类方法：(1) 精确归约：针对支配顶点、关联孪生（incidence twins）和权重为1的块给出可保持最优解的化简规则；(2) 上界工具：推导闭式上界和低权重上界，并通过穿孔（删除/忽略某些块）和覆盖证书（以少量块覆盖其余块）加以收紧；(3) 算法：提出由块权重与残差关联驱动的分层贪心聚类算法，按层逐次选点并更新残差关联；(4) 分析：给出算法的可行性、极大性、条件最优性（在特定条件下证明贪心达到最优）、分层见证匹配上界，以及关联局部复杂度界。

Result: 论文为广泛的关联（incidence）族给出了正确性、终止性、不动点（fixed-point）与最优性证书；推导出闭式及低权重上界，并借助穿孔和覆盖证书对界进行收紧；对所提分层贪心算法证明了可行性、极大性、条件最优性，并建立了分层见证匹配上界和关联局部复杂度界。同时通过具体例子说明多种证书在哪些情形下相互分离（给出不同强度结论）、在哪些情形下彼此重合。

Conclusion: 工作表明，最大强独立集可作为LSH-MinHash去重中避免虚假全局等价的目标函数，且该问题在关联结构上具有良好的可处理性：通过精确归约、多种上界证书和分层贪心算法，可以在广泛实例族上获得可验证的最优性/条件最优性与复杂度保证。证书分离/重合的实例分析有助于指导实践中按问题结构选择最合适、最锐利的分析工具。

Abstract: We study the maximum strong independent set problem in a finite hypergraph: find the largest vertex set that intersects every hyperedge in at most one vertex. This objective arises whenever each observed block is a local incompatibility constraint but transitive closure across overlapping blocks is not justified. A motivating example is multi-band LSH-MinHash deduplication, where each collision bucket gives local evidence, while connected-component contraction can impose spurious global equivalences. The paper develops an incidence-structural toolkit for this problem. We prove exact reductions for dominance, incidence twins, and weight-1 blocks; derive closed-form and low-weight upper bounds; introduce puncturing and covering certificates that sharpen those bounds; and analyze a layered greedy clustering algorithm driven by block weights and residual incidence. The algorithmic analysis includes feasibility, maximality, conditional optimality, a layered witness-matching upper bound, and incidence-local complexity bounds. The results give correctness, termination, fixed-point, and optimality certificates for broad incidence families, together with examples showing when different certificates separate or coincide.

</details>


### [306] [QuanText: Protecting Dataset-Level Secrets in Textual Data Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17995)
*Shuaiqi Wang,Zinan Lin,Giulia Fanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练、与LLM无关的文本数据发布机制QuanText，通过随机量化扰动全局秘密分布及关联属性分布，在保护数据集级隐私的同时保留有用属性，并基于统计最大泄漏(SML)提供理论保证，实验显示比生成式基线有更优的隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言数据集的发布可能泄露数据源的全局敏感属性（如性别、诊断、政治立场比例），现有工作多关注属性推断攻击，针对此类数据集级秘密的防御有限；差分隐私虽保护个体记录，但对聚合属性保护较弱。

Method: 给定秘密（如某诊断记录占比）和需保留效用的属性（如主题、情感），QuanText构建候选发布分布，随机选择与私有经验分布足够接近的候选，并用原文中属性相关片段重写每个文本样本以匹配所选分布；方法受统计最大泄漏(SML)框架启发。

Result: 理想条件下证明QuanText满足SML保障；在真实数据集上实验表明，其经验隐私-效用权衡优于数据生成基线。

Conclusion: QuanText为文本数据集全局秘密保护提供了一种训练免费且模型无关的有效方案，兼顾隐私与效用。

Abstract: Natural-language datasets support many downstream applications and research studies, but releasing text can reveal sensitive global properties of the underlying data source, such as the proportion of records associated with a particular gender, diagnosis, or political stance. Existing work has largely focused on property inference attacks that recover such global properties, while defenses for protecting these dataset-level secrets remain limited. Differential privacy, although effective for protecting individual records, provides only weak protection for aggregate properties. We propose Randomized Quantization for Text (QuanText), a training-free and large-language-model-agnostic data release mechanism that protects global secrets in textual datasets while preserving data utility. Given a dataset-level secret, such as the proportion of records with a particular diagnosis, and attributes whose utility should be preserved, such as topic and sentiment, QuanText perturbs both the secret distribution and the distributions of correlated attributes. It does so by constructing candidate release distributions over secret and non-secret attributes, randomly selecting a candidate sufficiently close to the private empirical distribution, and rewriting each private text sample to match the selected distribution using attribute-related snippets from the original text. QuanText is inspired by the Statistic Maximal Leakage (SML) framework, which bounds leakage about a secret function of a data distribution. Under idealized conditions, we show that QuanText satisfies an SML guarantee. Since these conditions may not hold exactly in practice, we also evaluate QuanText empirically on real-world datasets. Our results show that QuanText achieves a better empirical privacy-utility trade-off than competing data generation baselines.

</details>


### [307] [The Attention Within: Consensus Dynamics in Selective State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17997)
*João Pedro Silvestre,Álvaro Rodríguez Abella,Paulo Tabuada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从动力系统视角研究选择性状态空间模型（SSM）的递归是否像Transformer中的注意力一样促使token趋于共识（聚类到单一方向）。通过输入-状态稳定性论证，作者建立了共识平衡点的局部指数稳定性，并刻画了时变权重矩阵下的吸引域，证明SSM与Transformer在token聚合机制上具有深层相似性。实验表明Mamba-2的输出门是调控共识程度的关键组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer中注意力已被证明会使token聚类并最终收敛到单一方向（共识）。SSM的递归虽机制不同，但在功能上类似注意力，且已有工作指出SSM可视为线性注意力。因此自然提出疑问：SSM的递归是否同样驱动token达成共识？现有稳定性结果未覆盖时变权重矩阵，缺乏对此问题的严格理论分析。

Method: 采用动力系统视角，将token跨层的演化建模为常微分方程。利用输入-状态稳定性（ISS）论证，建立共识平衡点的局部指数稳定性，并刻画其吸引域，拓展至时变权重矩阵场景。结合数值实验在预训练Mamba-2模型上验证理论，并分析输出门对共识程度的影响。

Result: 证明了SSM递归同样促使token聚合向共识方向演化，与Transformer的注意力行为一致。数值实验显示输出门是调节共识强度的核心组件，能防止token完全收敛至单一方向。

Conclusion: SSM与Transformer比表面看起来更相似：两者核心机制都能驱动token达成共识，但SSM可通过输出门控制共识程度，兼具效率与灵活性。这为理解SSM的表示学习能力提供了理论支撑，也为后续架构改进指明方向。

Abstract: Selective state space models (SSMs) have recently emerged as a compelling alternative to transformers, combining competitive performance with substantially improved inference efficiency. At each SSM layer, a sequence of hidden states are propagated by a recurrence, mixing information of different tokens. Despite using a different mechanism, this mixing plays a role analogous to attention in transformers. In fact, recent works have shown that the two architectures may be closer than they first appear, as this recurrence admits a formulation akin to linear attention. In transformers, attention is known to drive the tokens to cluster, i.e., to reach consensus, collapsing in the limit to a single direction. Thus, we ask: does the recurrence at the core of SSMs drive the tokens to consensus, as attention does in transformers?
  To answer this question, we take a dynamical systems perspective on SSMs, modeling the evolution of tokens across layers as an ordinary differential equation. By exploiting input-to-state stability arguments, we establish local exponential stability of the consensus equilibria and characterize their domain of attraction for time-varying weight matrices, a setting not addressed by previous results. We thereby show that the resemblance between SSMs and transformers does run deeper: the recurrence at the core of SSMs aggregates tokens just as attention does. Numerical experiments on a pretrained Mamba-2 model point to the output gate as the component that regulates the extent of this consensus, preventing the tokens from reaching it in full.

</details>


### [308] [Structural Inference under Hidden Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18045)
*Zhongben Gong,Xiaoqun Wu,Mingyang Zhou,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SIHA框架，解决多智能体动力学中隐藏智能体的结构推断问题，通过结构无关初始化与结构引导迭代精化联合恢复隐藏智能体轨迹及其相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹结构推断已在多智能体系统建模中取得良好效果，但传统方法假设所有智能体轨迹可见。实际部署中因感知受限、遮挡或通信故障，部分智能体可能不可观测。现有研究分别处理了未见节点估计、部分观测下的结构推断和缺失值插补，但联合恢复隐藏智能体轨迹及其交互仍未被充分探索。

Method: 核心困难在于轨迹估计与结构推断之间存在循环依赖：恢复涉及隐藏智能体的交互需要其轨迹估计，而轨迹重建又受益于结构信息。SIHA通过两步机制解决：首先用结构无关初始化从可见观测重建隐藏轨迹，然后结合结构引导的迭代精化——利用Neural Relational Inference推断交互，并通过多强度结构注意力将估计的结构反馈到隐藏状态重建中，执行迭代的状态-结构交替更新。

Result: 在三个基准动力系统上的实验表明，可见-可见结构推断性能持续提升，同时在隐藏状态重建和未来预测方面也展现出优势。进一步在带有模拟整肢遮挡的运动捕捉实验中验证了其在真实隐藏智能体场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: SIHA成功填补了隐藏智能体场景下轨迹重建与结构推断联合恢复的空白，证明了结构信息与轨迹估计之间正反馈循环的有效性，为部分观测条件下多智能体系统的理解与预测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recovering latent interaction structures from multi-agent dynamics is important for understanding and predicting interacting systems. Trajectory-based structural inference has achieved promising performance, but conventional formulations assume that the trajectories of all modeled agents are available. In practice, agents may become unobserved at deployment because of limited sensing, occlusion, or communication failure. Existing studies have considered unseen-node estimation, structural inference under partial observations, and missing-value imputation, yet the joint recovery of hidden-agent trajectories and their interactions remains underexplored. We formulate this problem as structural inference under hidden agents. Its key difficulty is a circular dependency: recovering interactions involving a hidden agent requires an estimate of its trajectory, while trajectory reconstruction can itself benefit from structural information. To address this challenge, we propose Structural Inference under Hidden Agents (SIHA), which combines structure-agnostic initialization with structure-guided iterative refinement. SIHA reconstructs hidden trajectories from visible observations, infers interactions using Neural Relational Inference, and feeds the estimated structure back into hidden-state reconstruction through multi-strength structural attention and iterative state--structure updates. Experiments on three benchmark dynamical systems demonstrate consistent improvements in visible-to-visible structural inference, while also showing benefits in hidden-state reconstruction and future prediction. Motion-capture experiments with simulated whole-limb occlusion further demonstrate its effectiveness in realistic hidden-agent settings.

</details>


### [309] [Regional Explanations via Causal Sufficiency and Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18049)
*Xuexin Chen,Peng Liang,Zijian Li,Zhiyong Lin,Ruichu Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SNRE框架，用于学习因果充分且必要的区域级解释，通过随机干预推导区域级PNS度量并优化输入-输出区域对，兼顾可表达性与可解释性，实验验证了其在充分性-必要性、鲁棒性与模型分析实用性上的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI方法大多通过特征重要性、反事实解释或规则来解释预测，但较少从区域层面刻画"何时且仅何时"某一预测行为发生，缺乏对预测行为充分且必要条件的系统性建模。

Method: 提出了因果充分且必要的区域解释（SNRE）框架：学习一个输入区域A与输出区域B，使得属于A是模型输出落入B的充分且必要条件；借鉴经典概率必要性-充分性（PNS）概念，通过随机干预构建区域级PNS度量，并推导可微的有限样本估计量用于优化；使用显式且可解释的代数区域族（结合可学习的特征掩码）来参数化输入-输出区域对，在表达力与可解释性之间取得平衡。

Result: 实验表明SNRE学习到的区域对具有强充分性-必要性表现、鲁棒的解释行为，并在模型分析中展现了实际效用。

Conclusion: SNRE为模型解释提供了一种新的区域级视角，弥补了现有方法在"充分且必要"预测条件刻画上的不足，兼顾了统计严谨性、可解释性与实用价值，有望辅助更深入的模型理解与分析。

Abstract: Model explainability is essential for understanding and trusting machine learning models. Existing explainable AI methods often explain predictions through feature importance, counterfactual explanations, or rules. However, a region-level characterization of when and only when a prediction behavior arises remains less explored. This paper proposes Causal Sufficient and Necessary Regional Explanations (SNRE), a framework that learns an input region $A$ and output region $B$ such that membership in $A$ is both sufficient and necessary for the model output to fall in $B$. Motivated by the classical Probability of Necessity and Sufficiency (PNS), we formulate a region-level PNS measure through stochastic interventions and derive a differentiable finite-sample estimator for optimization. SNRE parameterizes the input-output region pair with explicit and interpretable algebraic region families, together with a learnable feature mask, balancing expressiveness and interpretability. Experiments demonstrate that SNRE learns region pairs with strong sufficiency-necessity performance, robust explanation behavior, and practical utility for model analysis.

</details>


### [310] [Beyond Embedding Transfer: Component Roles in Grokking Transfer and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18078)
*Zeyu Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过108次运行（12个种子块，96次2^3析因+12次规模对照）系统研究模运算上跨算子迁移中“早期加速”与“轨迹稳定性”的分离：迁移内部注意力/MLP权重（B）在嵌入/读出之外可提升早期精度5.46pp并减少558步确认延迟；持续性验证表明继续训练常引发严重的grokking后复发，冻结E/U可将离线复发从19.40%降至0.07%，而在线验证触发的门控使2a+b的True Max Drawdown从22.06%降至0.60%并在仿射、非线性二次和2层目标上获得前瞻性确认；非交换群S_5中未防护迁移峰值可达95.4%但前瞻性防护队列无确认收益(+0.15±1.14pp)。结果确立了迁移加速与稳定性之间的成分级分离，并揭示了参数屏蔽的经验边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然温启动迁移能使算法任务快速泛化，但尚不清楚具体哪些模型组件提供收益，以及这种收益在持续优化下是否稳定。作者希望解开“早期速度(有效性)”与“达到后回落(稳定性)”之间的混淆，并检验跨算子迁移的结构来源。

Method: 采用规模匹配的108次运行实验组：12个种子块中96次为2^3析因设计、12次为规模对照。比较迁移内部注意力/MLP权重(B)与仅迁移token嵌入和读出(E+U)的效果，并使用Holm校正的假设检验、TOST等价性检验、前瞻性2层复制、非交换群S_5测试。稳定性维度上比较离线冻结E/U与在线验证触发门控两种防护策略。

Result: 迁移E+U+B相比E+U使早期精度提升5.46pp(Holm p=0.0039)、确认延迟减少558步(Holm p=0.0088)。1层模型中读出+内部块迁移满足±500步延迟等价准则(TOST p=0.0011)，但11/12配对种子中Full更快；前瞻性2层复制确认内部块优势(12/12种子,+704.67积分单位,p=4.88×10^-4)，但省略供体嵌入低于Full 4475.6单位、超出±250容限。继续目标训练触发严重grokking后复发；冻结E/U将离线复发从19.40%降至0.07%(Holm p=0.005859)；在线验证门控将2a+b的True Max Drawdown从22.06%降至0.60%(p=0.000488)，并在仿射、非线性二次、2层目标上前瞻性降低10.94–23.47pp。非交换S_5中未防护迁移暂态峰值达95.4%，但防护队列无确认收益(+0.15±1.14pp)。

Conclusion: 研究在成分层面确立了“迁移加速”与“轨迹稳定性”的分离：内部块迁移主要负责加速，而表示载体(E/U)的防护决定稳定性；冻结与在线门控均能显著抑制复发但各有边界，在线门控的效果区别于静态早停，且防护收益在非交换群上未能推广，暴露了参数屏蔽的经验局限。

Abstract: Warm-start transfer can make algorithmic tasks generalize rapidly, yet it is unclear which model components provide the gain and whether that gain remains stable under continued optimization. We study cross-operator transfer on modular arithmetic and separate efficacy (early velocity) from stability (post-reach drawdown). In a scale-matched 108-run battery across 12 seed blocks (96-run 2^3 factorial plus 12-run scale control), transferring internal attention/MLP weights (B) alongside token embeddings and readout (E+U) improves early accuracy by 5.46 pp (Holm p=0.0039) and cuts confirmation latency by 558 steps (Holm p=0.0088). While readout plus internal-block transfer satisfies the pre-specified +/-500-step latency equivalence criterion in 1-layer models (TOST p=0.0011, though Full is faster in 11/12 paired seeds), a prospective 2-layer replication confirms the internal-block advantage (12/12 seeds, +704.67 integral units, p=4.88x10^-4) while revealing an architectural boundary: omitting donor embeddings falls 4475.6 units below Full, outside the +/-250-unit margin. Continued target training frequently triggers severe post-grokking relapse. Freezing transferred representation carriers (E, U) nearly eliminates offline relapse (19.40% -> 0.07%, Holm p=0.005859). Online validation-triggered gating slashes True Max Drawdown from 22.06% to 0.60% on 2a+b (p=0.000488), with prospective confirmations extending protection across affine, nonlinear quadratic, and 2-layer targets (10.94-23.47 pp reductions), distinguishing continual stabilization from static early stopping. In non-abelian S_5, unshielded transfer surges transiently (95.4% peak), but a prospective shielding cohort yields no confirmed benefit (+0.15 +/- 1.14 pp). These results establish a component-level dissociation between transfer acceleration and trajectory stability, and expose the empirical boundaries of parameter shielding.

</details>


### [311] [FedPGT: Progressive Gradient Transmission for Vehicular Federated Learning over Time-Varying Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18089)
*Jintao Yan,Tan Chen,Yuxuan Sun,Sheng Zhou,Zhisheng Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向车载联邦学习（VFL）的渐进式梯度传输方案FedPGT，车辆根据时变信道瞬时条件逐步传输高幅值梯度项。建立了刻画传输梯度项影响与幂律递减边际收益的收敛界，进而将在线决策建模为随机优化问题，通过引入每时隙代理传输变量解耦长期耦合的非可分目标，并采用Lyapunov漂移加惩罚方法进行在线调度，开发了低复杂度资源分配算法。在CIFAR-10图像分类和Argoverse轨迹预测任务上分别较最优基线提升3.65%准确率和降低12.66%平均位移误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载联邦学习在动态交通场景中面临通信开销大且信道条件快速时变的问题，而现有的通信资源分配和梯度稀疏化决策是预先设定的，无法适应车辆移动导致的信道条件与传输容量快速变化，导致决策失效。

Method: 提出FedPGT方案，车辆依据瞬时信道条件逐步（渐进式）传输高幅值梯度项。先建立刻画传输梯度项影响的收敛界，揭示幂律递减的边际收益行为；据此将在线决策建模为随机优化问题，难点在于累计耦合、非可分的目标函数；通过引入每时隙代理传输变量解耦跨时隙长期依赖，将原目标化为可加性的每时隙优化问题，进而采用Lyapunov漂移加惩罚方法进行在线调度，并设计了低复杂度资源分配算法。

Result: 实验结果表明，FedPGT在CIFAR-10图像分类任务上较最优基线提升3.65%准确率，在Argoverse轨迹预测任务上平均位移误差降低12.66%，验证了其在高度动态车载环境下对不同学习任务的适用性。

Conclusion: 通过信道自适应地渐进式传输高幅值梯度并结合在线优化的资源分配，FedPGT有效解决了静态稀疏化决策在时变信道下失效的问题，显著提升了VFL在不同动态学习任务中的性能。

Abstract: Vehicular federated learning (VFL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative model training for intelligent transportation systems, where communication resource allocation and gradient sparsification techniques have been explored to reduce communication overhead. However, vehicle mobility leads to rapidly varying channel conditions and transmission capacity, rendering predetermined resource allocation and sparsification decisions ineffective. In this paper, we propose FedPGT, a progressive gradient transmission scheme for VFL over time-varying channels, where vehicles progressively transmit high-magnitude gradient entries in response to instantaneous channel conditions. We establish a convergence bound that characterizes the impact of transmitted gradient entries and reveals diminishing-return behavior governed by a power-law decay. Motivated by this result, we formulate a stochastic optimization problem for online decision-making, where the main challenge lies in a cumulatively coupled, non-separable objective. To handle this challenge, we introduce per-slot surrogate transmission variables to decouple the long-term dependence across time slots and convert the original objective into an additive per-slot optimization problem, enabling a Lyapunov drift-plus-penalty approach for online scheduling. We further develop a low-complexity resource allocation algorithm for efficient online implementation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves a 3.65% accuracy improvement on the CIFAR-10 image classification task and a 12.66% reduction in average displacement error on the Argoverse trajectory prediction task compared with state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating its applicability to diverse learning tasks under highly dynamic vehicular environments.

</details>


### [312] [Agora: Git as Shared Memory for Collective AutoResearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18094)
*Yifan Zhang,Yunheng Zou,Shaokun Zhang,Jian Hu,Hao Zhang,Binfeng Xu,Jan Kautz,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Agora 是一个为自主研究智能体（如 AutoResearch）提供的共享记忆系统，将研究记录以仅追加的有向无环图（DAG）形式存储在 Git 中，使每个结论都是一个可检出的提交。通过派生索引暴露前沿、被忽视的分支及验证状态，并用多样性感知的选型规则防止社区趋同。在近12天的首次持续使用中，13个无任务分配、无中央规划的语言模型工人协作解决了权重迁移问题，发布1703个贡献，将评估指标从3.39提升至1.899 bits/byte，弥合了与训练好的GPT-2 124M之间62%的差距，并产生165个全部成功的独立复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自主研究循环（如AutoResearch）中，每次会话都从零开始，运行多个智能体只会导致重复搜索而非更多发现。作者旨在构建一种共享研究状态机制，让多个自主研究智能体能够积累和复用彼此的工作成果，从而提升单位算力的发现效率。

Method: Agora采用仅追加的有向无环图（DAG）存储研究记录于Git中，每个结果、洞见、假设、验证和报告都是不可变的提交，其父边指明它所依赖的工作。系统提供派生索引以展示前沿、被忽视的分支及每个结论的验证状态，并设计多样性感知的选型规则防止社区坍缩到单一领导者。实验采用13个无任务分配、无中央规划器的语言模型工人，在无训练数据和梯度更新的条件下，为冻结的119.6M参数注意力-SSM混合目标模型从141个预训练供体模型初始化权重。

Result: 在近12天的运行中，工人发布了1703个贡献，将评估器从3.39提升至1.899 bits/byte，弥合了与训练好的GPT-2 124M之间62%的差距。获胜配方将供体模型的下一个token统计量压缩进目标的嵌入和输出头，再通过稀疏编辑对注意力、前馈和状态空间块添加短程上下文信号。其145个提交的血统跨越15个账户，并发布了165个独立复现，无一失败。文章还描述了运行中期唯一一次将社区从单一文化中拉出的人工干预，并讨论了该trace证明与未证明的内容，以及可确定共享研究状态是否提升单位算力发现率的对照实验。

Conclusion: Agora展示了基于版本控制的有向无环图作为自主研究多智能体社区共享记忆的可行性：它能让多个无规划器的工人持续积累成果、避免重复搜索、防止趋同，并在真实且困难的权重迁移问题上取得显著进展。作者也坦率指出当前证据的局限，并提出需要受控对比实验来确定共享研究状态对单位算力发现率的确切收益。

Abstract: Autonomous research loops such as AutoResearch show that one coding agent can improve a training setup unattended. Run several of them and each session starts from scratch, so more agents tend to mean more duplicated search rather than more discovery. Agora is a shared memory for such agents: research is recorded as an append-only directed acyclic graph (DAG) stored in Git, so that every claim is a commit anyone can check out and rerun. Each result, insight, hypothesis, verification, and report is an immutable commit whose parent edges say what it builds on; a derived index exposes the frontier, the neglected branches, and the verification status of each claim, and a diversity-aware selection rule keeps the community from collapsing onto one leader. We describe the system and report its first sustained use: a run of nearly 12 days in which 13 language-model workers, with no assigned tasks and no central planner, worked on a weight-transfer problem. Given 141 pretrained donor models and a frozen 119.6M-parameter attention-SSM hybrid whose dimensions match no donor, the workers had to initialize the target without training data or gradient updates. They published 1,703 contributions and drove the evaluator from 3.39 to 1.899 bits per byte, closing 62% of the gap to a trained GPT-2 124M. The winning recipe compresses donor next-token statistics into the target's embedding and output head, then adds a short-range context signal through sparse edits to attention, feed-forward, and state-space blocks. Its 145-commit ancestry spans 15 accounts, and 165 independent reproductions were posted, none of which failed. We describe the single mid-run human intervention that pulled the community out of a monoculture, what the trace does and does not establish, and the controlled comparison that would settle whether shared research state improves discovery per unit of compute.

</details>


### [313] [iMINDBench: iEEG Multi-Institution Neural Decoding Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18104)
*Geeling Chau,Saba Hashemi,Yonghyeon Gwon,Eshani Patel,Jan DeWitt,Christopher Wang,Andrii Zahorodnii,Sabera J Talukder,Danny Dongyeop Han,Chun Kee Chung,Maryam M Shanechi,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出iMINDBench，一个多机构颅内脑电图（iEEG）神经解码基准，涵盖三个自然电影观看数据集上的十五项解码任务，并定义了标准化预处理流程和固定评估划分。实验发现，预训练系统通常优于各自预处理轨道内的基线，但强光谱基线在跨机构数据集中仍具竞争力；扩展研究显示，增加多达25倍的其他受试者或机构监督数据，仅在会话内训练基础上带来微小或任务相关的提升，凸显了对更强预处理基线和更有效跨主体/机构数据利用的iEEG模型的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: iEEG解码进展难以可靠衡量：数据集往往针对特定任务或机构，限制了跨任务与记录环境的泛化证据；预处理选择强烈影响性能，使模型改进难以与预处理增益区分。因此需要一个统一、标准化的基准来支持一致的模型比较。

Method: 构建iMINDBench基准，统一评估共享的十五项解码任务，覆盖三个自然电影观看的iEEG数据集；定义标准化预处理轨道和固定评估划分以支持一致性比较；开展预训练系统与基线对比，并设计缩放实验（增加最多25倍来自其他受试者或机构的监督数据）。

Result: 被评估的预训练系统通常在各自预处理轨道内优于基线；强光谱基线在跨机构数据集上仍具竞争力；增加25倍跨主体/机构监督数据仅带来微小或任务相关的增益。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前iEEG模型的两个关键缺口：需要超越强预处理基线，以及需要更有效地利用跨主体和跨机构数据，为通用iEEG基础模型的发展提供方向。

Abstract: Intracranial electroencephalography (iEEG) is widely used to record electrical activity directly from electrodes inside the human brain, making it an attractive modality for neural decoding. However, progress in iEEG decoding, especially toward general-purpose foundation models, remains difficult to measure reliably: datasets are task- or institution-specific, limiting evidence of generalization across tasks and recording environments, and preprocessing choices can strongly influence performance, making model improvements difficult to distinguish from preprocessing gains. Thus, we introduce iMINDBench, an iEEG Multi-Institution Neural Decoding Benchmark that evaluates models on a shared suite of fifteen decoding tasks across three naturalistic movie-watching datasets. The benchmark additionally defines standardized preprocessing tracks and fixed evaluation splits to support consistent model comparisons. Using iMINDBench, we find that the evaluated pretrained systems generally outperform baselines within their respective preprocessing tracks, while strong spectral baselines remain competitive across institutional datasets. In our scaling study, adding up to 25 times more supervised data from other subjects or institutions yields only small or task-dependent gains over within-session training. Together, these findings highlight the need for iEEG models that improve on strong preprocessing baselines and make more effective use of data across subjects and institutions. Project website: https://imindbench.github.io/

</details>


### [314] [FoundAna: A GNN-assisted Foundation Model for Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18107)
*Suprim Nakarmi,Chahana Dahal,Yue Zhao,Junggab Son,Zuobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoundAna是首个用于跨图泛化的图异常检测基础模型框架，结合GNN与Transformer，通过四种位置编码和重构误差实现异常分数，在九个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法遵循“一模型一数据集”范式，受限于任务异质性、标签稀缺和领域差异，跨图泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出FoundAna框架：集成异常检测专用的GNN组件与标准Transformer编码器，并加入四种互补位置编码以捕获局部和全局结构信息；编码后的节点表示通过属性解码器和邻接解码器重构，以重构误差作为异常分数。

Result: 在金融、社交、引文网络等九个基准数据集上，FoundAna一致优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: FoundAna证明了基础模型范式在图异常检测中的可行性，提供了跨图泛化的有效解决方案，并开源代码与补充材料。

Abstract: Graph anomaly detection aims to identify graph structures (e.g., nodes, edges, or subgraphs) that deviate significantly from expected patterns, which supports critical applications in fraud detection, spam identification, network intrusion, etc. Despite the growing methods in the field, existing approaches follow a one-model-per-dataset paradigm, limiting their transferability across diverse real-world scenarios due to task heterogeneity, label scarcity, and domain variability. In this work, we introduce FoundAna, a GNN-assisted Foundation Model for Graph Anomaly Detection - the first foundation model framework designated for generalizable, cross-graph anomaly detection by combining GNNs and transformers. FoundAna integrates an anomaly detection-specific GNN component with a standard transformer encoder augmented by four complementary positional encodings, which enable the model to capture both local and global structural information. Specifically, the positional encoding enriched node representations are passed through attribute and adjacency decoders, and the reconstruction errors serve as the anomaly score. Extensive experiments on nine benchmark datasets spanning financial, social, and citation network domains demonstrate that FoundAna consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The code implementation and Supplementary materials are here: https://github.com/FoundAna331/FoundAna.

</details>


### [315] [Learning Fractional-Order Dynamics from a Single Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18127)
*Xiaole Zhang,Ziyi Zhang,Zehao Zhao,Stephen Tu,Guannan Qu,Yorie Nakahira,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有长程依赖性的离散时间分数阶线性时不变系统，研究从单一长度为t的观测轨迹中进行的系统辨识问题。作者提出了分数阶普通最小二乘网格搜索算法（FO-GS），这是一种利用分数差分算子对角结构将辨识问题按行解耦的两阶段估计器。在稳定性假设下，论文建立了估计分数阶和系统矩阵的高概率非渐近误差界，两者误差均以O(t^{-1/2})缩放，并通过实验证明FO-GS优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实世界过程表现出长程依赖性，当前状态依赖于缓慢衰减的历史状态痕迹而非最近状态，这类非马尔可夫动力学无法由传统马尔可夫系统模型刻画。分数阶系统的辨识比马尔可夫系统更具挑战性，因为其估计耦合了整个历史信息，既增加了统计分析的难度也提升了实际辨识的复杂性，因此亟需有效且可分析的系统辨识方法。

Method: 所提方法名为分数阶普通最小二乘网格搜索（FO-GS），是一个两阶段估计器。其核心思想是利用Grünwald--Letnikov差分算子的对角结构，将原本跨整个历史耦合的分数阶系统辨识问题逐行解耦，从而在各行上独立应用普通最小二乘。第一阶段估计系统矩阵，第二阶段在网格上搜索分数阶参数。

Result: 在稳定性假设下，论文针对异质性（heterogeneous）设定建立了高概率非渐近误差界，分数阶参数和系统矩阵的估计误差均以O(t^{-1/2})的速率缩放。实验结果表明FO-GS在恢复分数阶参数和底层系统动力学两方面均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: FO-GS通过巧妙利用分数差分算子的对角结构将复杂的全局耦合辨识问题解耦为逐行的局部估计，在理论和实验上均证明能够高效、准确地辨识分数阶线性时不变系统，为长程依赖非马尔可夫动力学的系统辨识提供了简单而有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Many real-world processes exhibit long-range dependence, where the current state depends on a slowly decaying trace of past states rather than on the most recent state alone. This paper studies system identification for discrete-time fractional-order linear time-invariant systems from a single observed trajectory of length $t$, a setting that captures such non-Markovian dynamics through the Grünwald--Letnikov difference operator. Unlike Markovian systems, fractional-order systems couple estimation across the entire history, making both statistical analysis and practical identification more challenging. We propose \emph{Fractional-Order Ordinary-Least-Squares Grid-Search (FO-GS)}, a simple two-stage estimator that exploits the diagonal structure of the fractional-difference operator to decouple the identification problem row-wise. Under the stability assumption, we establish high-probability, non-asymptotic error bounds for estimating both the fractional order and the system matrix in the heterogeneous setting, with both estimation errors scaling as \(\mathcal{O}(t^{-1/2})\). Through experiments, we show that \emph{FO-GS} outperforms existing baselines in recovering both the fractional order and the underlying system dynamics.

</details>


### [316] [Colla-Q: Toward Collaborative Experts in MoE Quantization via Minimax Precision Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18131)
*Eunju Shin,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Colla-Q，一种基于激活熵的混合专家（MoE）模型量化方法。通过激活熵驱动的比特宽度分配算法，在各专家间维持均衡的性能表现，从而提升整体 MoE 模型的量化性能，并降低对校准数据集的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然量化能降低内存与计算开销，但会显著损害模型性能，尤其在 MoE 模型中更为突出——因为单个专家参数量少且对低比特表示极为敏感。鉴于 MoE 以集成模型方式运行，依赖被路由专家的协同贡献，某一专家因量化导致的性能骤降会拖累整个模型，因此亟需一种在各专家间均衡分配资源、维持协同性能的量化方法。

Method: 提出 Colla-Q 比特分配框架，核心是一种基于激活熵的比特宽度分配算法。通过计算各专家的激活熵来指导比特分配，使各专家在量化后保持均衡的性能水平，鼓励其协同运作，从而提升整体 MoE 模型的鲁棒性与稳定性。

Result: 该方法能 1) 提升整体 MoE 的量化性能；2) 降低对校准数据集的依赖。由于均匀调节各专家性能增强了模型的鲁棒性，量化后的 MoE 在不同校准数据集上能够更一致地泛化。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过激活熵驱动的均衡比特分配策略，Colla-Q 有效缓解了 MoE 量化中个别专家性能锐减对整体模型造成的负面影响，在提升量化性能的同时增强了跨校准数据集的泛化一致性，为 MoE 模型的高效量化提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) quantization method based on activation entropy. Although quantization reduces memory and computational costs, it can substantially degrade performance. In particular, performance decline is pronounced in quantized MoE models, where individual experts have a small number of parameters that are sensitive to low-bit representation. Considering that MoE operates as an ensemble model with collaborative contributions from routed experts, a significant performance decline of a particular expert due to quantization can harm model performance. Therefore, we propose Colla-Q, a bit-allocation framework to maintain balanced performance across experts through an activation-entropy-based bit-width allocation algorithm. This approach encourages each expert to operate collaboratively in the quantized model, thereby 1) improving the overall MoE performance and 2) reducing the dependence on the calibration dataset. Since uniformly adjusting each expert's performance facilitates robustness and stability of the MoE model, the proposed MoE quantization method can generalize more consistently across different calibration datasets. Our code is available at: https://github.com/mmai-laboratory/Colla_Q

</details>


### [317] [Rethinking How We Evaluate Methodological Progress in Health AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18134)
*Florent Pollet,Matthew McDermott*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了医疗电子病历(EHR)人工智能领域的可复现性与临床评估任务定义两大障碍。作者在统一评估框架下重新实现了12种历史与近期算法，并在MIMIC-IV和NWICU两个数据集上，借助专家撰写的临床任务和随机生成的预测任务两类互补任务族进行评估。研究发现：跨任务族和跨数据集的算法相对比较具有很强的可迁移性（随机任务到临床任务同样成立）；临床任务表现出更大的任务-方法交互效应，可作为解释建模选择优势的线索；新算法并未一致超越旧方法，梯度提升树配合现代宽稀疏EHR表示仍极具竞争力。结论是，获得有用方法论知识对任务工程的依赖可能低于普遍假设，但理解任务与方法间残留的结构性异质性仍至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR人工智能的方法论进展依赖于判断哪种算法在何种条件下表现更好，但可复现性困难与临床上有意义的评估任务难以定义被认为阻碍了这一进展。作者旨在对这两大障碍进行实证研究，以明确算法比较的可靠性、任务异构性中蕴含的方法论信息，以及过去十年算法进展的真实情况。

Method: 在共享的统一评估框架下重新实现12种历史及近期算法，于MIMIC-IV与NWICU两个临床数据集上评估。比较两类互补任务族：专家撰写的临床有意义任务，以及由随机采样事件码与预测视界生成的合成任务。通过跨任务族与跨数据集的迁移性检验、残差任务异构性分析以及受控对比，考察算法相对排序是否稳定、任务属性能否解释方法优势，并评估十年间的方法进展。

Result: ①跨评估设置（包括随机生成任务到临床任务、跨数据集）的总体两两比较具有强迁移性；②临床有意义任务表现出更强的任务-方法交互作用，为任务属性解释建模选择优劣提供了初步证据；③新算法并未一致优于旧算法，梯度提升树配合现代宽而稀疏的EHR表示仍具高度竞争力。

Conclusion: 有用方法论知识的获取可能对任务工程的依赖小于普遍预期，这降低了评估任务构建的门槛；但须重视并理解跨任务与方法间残留的结构性异构性，因其能刻画特定建模选择何时具有优势。研究为EHR算法公平对比、可复现评估框架以及算法选择策略提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Methodological progress in artificial intelligence (AI) for electronic health records (EHRs) depends on our ability to determine which algorithms work better, and under which conditions. However, such progress is thought to be hindered by difficulties in reproducibility and in defining clinically meaningful evaluation tasks. We empirically study these barriers by re-implementing 12 historical and recent algorithms within a shared evaluation framework and evaluating them on two clinical datasets, MIMIC-IV and NWICU. We compare two complementary task families: expert-authored clinically meaningful tasks and generated tasks defined from randomly sampled event codes and prediction horizons. We ask whether relative algorithms comparisons transfer across task families and datasets, whether residual task heterogeneity contains useful methodological structure, and what a controlled comparison reveals about progress over the last decade. We find that aggregate pairwise comparisons transfer strongly across evaluation settings, including from randomly generated tasks to clinically meaningful tasks and across datasets. At the same time, clinically meaningful tasks exhibit greater task-method interaction, providing preliminary evidence that task properties can help explain when particular modeling choices are advantageous. Finally, newer algorithms do not consistently outperform earlier approaches: gradient-boosted trees remain highly competitive when paired with a modern, wide and sparse representation of the EHR. Together, these results suggest that useful methodological knowledge may require less task engineering than commonly assumed, while highlighting the importance of understanding the structured heterogeneity that remains across tasks and methods.

</details>


### [318] [Reaching Every Position Without Searching: Rotating Sparse Wiring on the Hypercube as a Substitute for Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18145)
*Yoshiaki Takashita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种固定、稀疏且逐层旋转的"旋转布线"替代全部注意力机制：将序列位置视为log2(n)维超立方体的顶点，每层仅与某一维度的邻居相连并逐层轮转维度。在合成任务上，该方案以1/32的链接数达到全连接效果；在enwik8字符级语言建模上，混合模型（16层中保留2层注意力）以1/7链接、少42%参数、2.4倍省时，获得比全注意力更低的损失（0.06 BPC），在日英代码混合语料上差距扩大至0.16。研究还报告了失败尝试（学习坐标、动力学变体为饱和核伪影）及必要的测量规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制对每一层、每个输入都付出搜索"与谁连接"的高昂代价（O(n²)链接），作者质疑能否用固定、稀疏且逐层旋转的布线达到类似信息传播效果，从而大幅降低计算与参数成本。

Method: 将序列的n个位置视作log2(n)维超立方体的顶点，在层ℓ将每个位置与其沿维度(ℓ mod log2(n))的邻居相连；每层仅2n条链接（而非n²），经log2(n)层信息可达任意位置。在合成任务与两个真实语料（enwik8前12M字符、日英代码混合语料）上对比纯旋转调度、含2层注意力的混合模型与全注意力基线。

Result: 合成任务上旋转布线以1/32链接匹配全连接，而固定稀疏模式失败（关键在于覆盖每个维度而非顺序）。enwik8上混合模型损失低0.06 BPC，且链接减至1/7、参数少42%、墙钟时间快2.4倍，纯旋转调度与混合持平；第二语料差距扩大到0.16。学习率可用窗口为注意力的4-8倍。学习坐标与"动力学"变体失败（后者为饱和核伪影）。

Conclusion: 固定、稀疏、逐层旋转的布线可高效替代大部分注意力连接，在更少资源下取得更低损失，且对超参数更鲁棒（学习率窗口更宽）；同时强调了在规模化实验中以冻结语料、全覆盖评估和种子散布作为排序标准的重要性。

Abstract: Attention pays, at every layer and for every input, the cost of searching for whom to connect. We ask how far one can get with wiring that is fixed, sparse, and simply rotated from layer to layer. Treating the $n$ positions of a sequence as the vertices of a $\log_2 n$-dimensional hypercube and connecting each position, at layer $\ell$, to its neighbour along dimension $\ell \bmod \log_2 n$, information from every position reaches every other in $\log_2 n$ layers with $2n$ links per layer instead of $n^2$. On a synthetic task that is unsolvable unless all positions are reached, this rotation matches all-to-all wiring at $1/32$ of the links, while the same sparse pattern held fixed across layers fails; what matters is that every dimension is touched, not the order. On character-level language modelling of a public corpus (the first $12$M characters of enwik8), a hybrid that keeps two attention layers among sixteen sparse ones reaches $0.06$ bits-per-character lower held-out loss than a fully attentive model of the same width at the same step budget (three seeds each, no overlap), with $1/7$ of the links, $42\%$ fewer parameters, and $2.4\times$ less wall-clock time; the purely rotated schedule is level with the hybrid. The same ordering holds on a second corpus of mixed Japanese, English and code, where the gap widens to $0.16$. The usable learning-rate window is four to eight times wider than attention's on both. We also report what did not work - learned coordinates, and a "dynamics" variant whose apparent gains turned out to be an artefact of a saturated kernel - and the measurement discipline (frozen corpus, full-coverage evaluation, seed spread as the bar for ranking) that we found necessary to say anything at all at this scale.

</details>


### [319] [LIGE-GR: A Smooth Leap from Ranking to Generative Recommendation in the LLM Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18148)
*Venkat Srinivas,Chenzhang He,Sam Woodmansee,Shawn Lian,Wenjie Hu,Renjie Jiang,Ziheng Huang,Xinyuan Zhang,Zhihao Zheng,Zhuoran Yu,Rui Li,Lei Yuan,Ziwei Li,Jimmy Jia,Mert Terzihan,Ekrem Kocaguneli,Yiming Liao,Zhichen Zhao,Yue Yin,Yue Weng,Wanlin Ma,Xufeng Cai,Weimiao Wu,Yezhou Huang,Du Zhang,Yukun Ding,Aaron Johnston,Yueming Wang,Zhaojie Gong,Yuting Zhang,Serena Li,Adithya Ganesh,Boying Liu,Haichuan Yang,Xialu Li,Matt Ma,Qunshu Zhang,John Joshua Miller,Praveen Rathinavelu,Cheng Huang,Aadhar Sachdeva,Josh Karns,Andres Aaron Gutierrez,Neil Agarwal,Gustas Pladis,Vladimir Batygin,Gopal Ray,Aditya Priyadarshi,Shantanu Patil,Zhe Wang,Penny Pan,Yiping Han,Arun Singh,Guangdeng Liao,Bi Xue,Xinyao Hu,Yang Song,Yisong Song,Meihong Wang,Haotian Wu,Deepak Agarwal,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LIGE-GR 是一个列表式生成与评估推荐框架，在不重建整个推荐栈的前提下，将传统基于逐项(pointwise)排序的成熟推荐系统泛化为列表式(listwise)生成系统，使现有基础设施、模型与价值函数得以兼容并获益于列表级优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLM)的成功为下一代推荐系统提供了重要启发——推荐与语言生成在结构上都旨在产生一条能优化用户体验的有序序列。然而如何将LLM范式的精髓精准融入成熟的工业推荐系统仍是开放问题，面临两大挑战：一是如何引入序列级生成与优化；二是成熟推荐系统经过多年围绕特定产品、业务约束、服务基础设施与组织归属的迭代定制，整体替换技术风险和组织的破坏性极大。

Method: LIGE-GR 提出列表式生成与评估推荐框架，从传统逐项(itemwise)排序系统升级为生成式推荐范式。其核心并不从零重建整个推荐栈，而是将现有逐点推荐系统泛化为列表(listwise)生成系统，从而在不破坏兼容性的前提下（保留现有模型、价值函数与服务基础设施）获得列表级优化的收益。

Result: 在 Instagram Reels 和 Facebook Video 的短视频推荐场景中验证：LIGE-GR 使 Instagram Reels 的停留时长(time spent)提升 1.14%，Facebook Video 提升 0.72%，且仅需适度的额外推理资源开销。

Conclusion: LIGE-GR 证明了生成式推荐范式可以以低风险、低破坏性的增量方式融入成熟的工业推荐系统，在不替换整体架构的前提下带来停留时长的实际改善，为学术界与工业界在LLM范式与推荐系统融合方面提供了一条可行的中间路径。

Abstract: The remarkable success of large language models (LLMs) has provided important inspiration for the next generation of recommender systems. Structurally, recommendation and language generation share a similarity: both aim to produce an ordered sequence that optimizes the user's experience. However, how to precisely absorb the essence of the LLM paradigm into mature industrial recommender systems remains an open problem.
  There are two challenges. First, it is unclear how to incorporate sequence-level generation and optimization from the LLM paradigm into recommendation. Second, real-world recommender systems are mature systems that have been iteratively customized for years around specific products, business constraints, serving infrastructure, and organizational ownership. Replacing such systems wholesale is often technically risky and organizationally disruptive.
  In this paper, we propose LIGE-GR, a listwise generation and evaluation recommendation framework that upgrades from a traditional ranking system based on itemwise recommendation toward a generative recommendation paradigm. Instead of rebuilding the entire recommendation stack from scratch, LIGE-GR generalizes the existing pointwise recommendation system into a listwise generation system. This allows mature recommender systems to benefit from listwise optimization while preserving compatibility with existing models, value functions, and serving infrastructure.
  We validate LIGE-GR in short-video recommendation on Instagram Reels and Facebook Video. On these recommendation surfaces, LIGE-GR improves time spent by 1.14 percent on Instagram Reels and 0.72 percent on Facebook Video, while requiring only modest additional inference resources.

</details>


### [320] [MoRE: Mixture of Reused Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18176)
*Eric S. Qiu,Utku Umur Acikalin,Justin Lovelace,Christian Belardi,Arjun B. Mulchandani,Carla P. Gomes,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoRE（Mixture of Reused Experts）是一种混合架构，将专家池跨相邻层组共享，每层保留独立路由器但从更大的共享池中选择，并引入轻量级可学习深度嵌入来区分不同层。在114M到1.15B参数规模上，MoRE在相同计算和参数预算下稳定优于标准MoE和先进的权重重用架构，且仅需对现有MoE实现做最小修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构将模型容量与计算成本解耦，但参数随专家数量线性增长导致高内存占用；循环Transformer通过重用层权重实现参数效率，但通常缺乏竞争性语言建模所需的容量。作者旨在结合两者优点，在保持参数效率的同时提升模型容量与性能。

Method: 提出MoRE混合架构：将专家池在相邻层组间共享，每层保留独立路由器但从更大的共享专家池中挑选专家，从而在不增加参数的前提下扩展路由组合的多样性；引入轻量级可学习深度嵌入（depth embeddings），在路由前对每层输入进行条件化，使共享专家能够区分不同层。

Result: 在三个模型规模（114M至1.15B参数）上的实验表明，MoRE在相同计算与参数预算下，相比标准MoE和先进的权重重用架构，持续实现更低的困惑度（perplexity）和更强的下游任务性能，且对现有MoE实现只需最小化修改。

Conclusion: MoRE通过跨层共享专家池并引入深度嵌入，有效平衡了参数效率与模型容量，在相近成本下取得了优于标准MoE和权重重用架构的语言建模与下游表现，是一种实用且易于集成的改进方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures decouple model capacity from computational cost, yet incur high memory footprints as parameters grow linearly with the number of experts. Recurrent Transformers achieve parameter efficiency by reusing layer weights, but typically lack the capacity for competitive language modeling. We propose Mixture of Reused Experts (MoRE), a hybrid that shares expert pools across groups of adjacent layers. Each layer retains its own router but selects from a larger shared pool, expanding the diversity of routing combinations without additional parameters. To enable shared experts to distinguish between layers, we introduce lightweight learnable depth embeddings that condition each layer's input before routing. Experiments across three model scales (114M-1.15B parameters) show that MoRE consistently achieves lower perplexity and stronger downstream performance than standard MoEs and state-of-the-art weight-sharing architectures at matched compute and parameter budgets, with only minimal modifications to existing MoE implementations.

</details>


### [321] [Transformation Laws in Neural Representations: Structure, Realisability, and Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18190)
*Yuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经表征如何保留输入变换的结构（可操作性表征内容），通过参考变换在神经特征上的相容作用来分析。核心问题是一个变换何时能“下降”经过编码器（即被线性实现），并给出了线性情形下的缺陷度量。作者还以颜色为实例做了深入的实证分析，最后构建了一个紧凑的零样本色相读取界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经表征如何保留输入变化的结构，这连接了表征分析与内部干预。已有工作缺乏将参考变换作为具体操作对象来研究表征内容可操作性的理论框架，本文旨在建立变换法则（transformation laws）作为连接表征理解与表征设计的桥梁。

Method: 理论方面：给出变换下降（descend）经过编码器的刻画条件，在线性设定下证明了缺陷由变换对丢弃信息的需求决定（用表征诱导的度量衡量）；在整流器上区分两类失真来源（源区域已不可恢复的部分、以及用单一算子满足所有被访问区域的代价）；对“已测量”的谐波载子给出封闭答案——线性实现存在当且仅当保留的谐波块在该作用下不变。实证方面：以颜色为实例，测量冻结视觉特征中色相轨道的能量分布（前两个谐波集中84-88%）及其继承/重塑关系（输入、架构、训练、深度的影响），并测试测量结构对预测、迁移、组合的支持，对比全局与局部实现。构造方面：依据测量构建紧凑接口，旋转作用由结构固定且永不拟合，实现对未见形状色相的零样本读取。

Result: 理论结果：完整刻画变换下降条件，给出线性缺陷度量与整流器失真的双源归因，以及谐波块的封闭判据。实证结果：色相轨道在前两个谐波集中84-88%能量，旋转平面跨形状共享；该组织主要继承自输入与架构，并被训练与深度重塑；测量结构支持预测、迁移和组合，其中全局与局部实现差异显著。构造结果：零样本色相读取达到3.4°中位误差。

Conclusion: 理论、结构测量与构造三方面共同确立了变换法则作为连接神经表征理解与表征设计的具体对象，说明表征的结构化内容是可测量、可解释且可被直接用于构建高效界面的操作性资源。

Abstract: How neural representations preserve the structure of input changes connects representation analysis with internal intervention. We study operable representational content through compatible actions of reference transformations on neural features. We characterise when a transformation descends through an encoder, and give a linear setting in which the defect is governed by the transformation's demand for discarded information, measured in the metric the representation induces. On a rectifier the failure to realise a transformation has two distinguishable sources --- what the source region has already made unrecoverable, and what it costs to satisfy every region the transformation visits with one operator --- and for a \textit{measured} harmonic carrier the same question has a closed answer: a linear realisation exists exactly when the retained harmonic blocks are invariant under the action. Using colour as the in-depth instance, we find that hue orbits in frozen visual features concentrate 84--88\% of their energy in the first two harmonics with rotation planes shared across shapes, that this organisation is substantially inherited from input and architecture and is reshaped by training and depth, and that the measured structure supports prediction, transport from new starting states, and composition --- with global and local realisations differing sharply in which they achieve. Guided by the measurements, we construct a compact interface whose rotation action is fixed by the structure and never fitted: it reads hue zero-shot at 3.4$^\circ$ median error on unseen shapes. Theory, structural measurement, and construction together establish transformation laws as a concrete object connecting the understanding of neural representations to their design.

</details>


### [322] [A Lightweight CNN Integrated Compact Convolutional Transformer for Multi-Scale Feature Learning and reducing computational complexity for breast cancer mammography image detection and classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18212)
*Md Taimur Ahad,Ainuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种 CNN 与紧凑卷积 Transformer（CCT）混合的乳腺癌分类框架：CNN 提取特征后，经 CCT tokenizer 重塑为紧凑 patch tokens 并添加位置编码，从而在保留空间结构的同时捕捉长距离上下文依赖。基于 5 折交叉验证，在 3 个乳腺 X 光数据集上以仅 25 万参数达到 99%–100% 的准确率，并集成可解释 AI（XAI）以增强临床信任，适用于资源受限的计算机辅助诊断场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
